ActCam: Zero-Shot Joint Camera and 3D Motion Control for Video Generation
Authors: Omar El Khalifi, Thomas Rossi, Oscar Fossey, Thibault Fouque, Ulysse Mizrahi, Philip Torr, Ivan Laptev, Fabio Pizzati, Baptiste Bellot-Gurlet
Venue: SIGGRAPH 2026
First: 2026-05-07T17:59:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:59:58+00:00
Comments: SIGGRAPH 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
For artistic applications, video generation requires fine-grained control over both performance and cinematography, i.e., the actor's motion and the camera trajectory. We present ActCam, a zero-shot method for video generation that jointly transfers character motion from a driving video into a new scene and enables per-frame control of intrinsic and extrinsic camera parameters. ActCam builds on any pretrained image-to-video diffusion model that accepts conditioning in terms of scene depth and character pose. Given a source video with a moving character and a target camera motion, ActCam generates pose and depth conditions that remain geometrically consistent across frames. We then run a single sampling process with a two-phase conditioning schedule: early denoising steps condition on both pose and sparse depth to enforce scene structure, after which depth is dropped and pose-only guidance refines high-frequency details without over-constraining the generation. We evaluate ActCam on multiple benchmarks spanning diverse character motions and challenging viewpoint changes. We find that, compared to pose-only control and other pose and camera methods, ActCam improves camera adherence and motion fidelity, and is preferred in human evaluations, especially under large viewpoint changes. Our results highlight that careful camera-consistent conditioning and staged guidance can enable strong joint camera and motion control without training. Project page: https://elkhomar.github.io/actcam/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ActCam：用于视频生成的零样本联合相机与三维运动控制
为艺术应用，视频生成需要对表演和 cinematography（即演员运动与相机轨迹）进行精细控制。我们提出 ActCam，一种零样本视频生成方法，可同时将驱动视频中的角色运动转移到新场景，并实现逐帧控制相机的内参和外参。ActCam 基于任何接受场景深度和角色姿态条件的预训练图像到视频扩散模型。给定一个包含移动角色的源视频和目标相机运动，ActCam 生成在帧间保持几何一致性的姿态与深度条件。随后，我们通过两阶段条件调度执行单次采样过程：早期去噪步骤同时依赖姿态与稀疏深度以强制场景结构，之后移除深度条件，仅用姿态引导细化高频细节，避免过度约束生成。我们在涵盖多样角色运动与挑战性视角变化的多个基准上评估 ActCam，发现相比仅姿态控制及其他姿态与相机方法，ActCam 在相机遵循性与运动保真度上表现更优，且在人类评估中更受青睐，尤其在大视角变化场景下。结果表明，精心设计的相机一致性条件与分阶段引导可实现无需训练的强联合相机与运动控制。项目页面：https://elkhomar.github.io/actcam/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for precise artistic control over both actor motion and camera movement in video generation, ActCam introduces a zero-shot method that jointly transfers character motion from a source video into a new scene while enabling per-frame camera parameter control. It leverages any pretrained image-to-video diffusion model conditioned on scene depth and character pose, generating geometrically consistent pose and depth conditions across frames. The method employs a two-phase sampling schedule: early steps use both pose and sparse depth to establish scene structure, then switch to pose-only guidance to refine details without over-constraining the output. Experiments across diverse motion and viewpoint benchmarks show ActCam outperforms pose-only and other joint methods in camera adherence and motion fidelity, and is favored in human evaluations, especially under large viewpoint changes.
ActCam 旨在实现对演员动作与摄像机轨迹的细粒度联合控制，无需训练即可零样本生成。它基于任意预训练的图像到视频扩散模型，通过从源视频和目标摄像机运动中生成几何一致的姿势与深度条件，实现动作与摄像机的协同控制。其两阶段采样策略先用姿势与稀疏深度引导结构，再切换为仅姿势引导以优化细节，避免过度约束。在多个基准测试中，ActCam 在大视角变化下显著优于仅姿势控制及其他联合方法，且在人工评估中更受青睐。
UniPool: A Globally Shared Expert Pool for Mixture-of-Experts
Authors: Minbin Huang, Han Shi, Chuanyang Zheng, Yimeng Wu, Guoxuan Chen, Xintong Yu, Yichun Yin, Hong Cheng
First: 2026-05-07T17:59:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:59:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures allocate expert capacity through a rigid per-layer rule: each transformer layer owns a separate expert set. This convention couples depth scaling with linear expert-parameter growth and assumes that every layer needs isolated expert capacity. However, recent analyses and our routing probe challenge this allocation rule: replacing a deeper layer's learned top-k router with uniform random routing drops downstream accuracy by only 1.0-1.6 points across multiple production MoE models. Motivated by this redundancy, we propose UniPool, an MoE architecture that treats expert capacity as a global architectural budget by replacing per-layer expert ownership with a single shared pool accessed by independent per-layer routers. To enable stable and balanced training under sharing, we introduce a pool-level auxiliary loss that balances expert utilization across the entire pool, and adopt NormRouter to provide sparse and scale-stable routing into the shared expert pool. Across five LLaMA-architecture model scales (182M, 469M, 650M, 830M, and 978M parameters) trained on 30B tokens from the Pile, UniPool consistently improves validation loss and perplexity over the matched vanilla MoE baselines. Across these scales, UniPool reduces validation loss by up to 0.0386 relative to vanilla MoE. Beyond raw loss improvement, our results identify pool size as an explicit depth-scaling hyperparameter: reduced-pool UniPool variants using only 41.6%-66.7% of the vanilla expert-parameter budget match or outperform layer-wise MoE at the tested scales. This shows that, under a shared-pool design, expert parameters need not grow linearly with depth; they can grow sublinearly while remaining more efficient and effective than vanilla MoE. Further analysis shows that UniPool's benefits compose with finer-grained expert decomposition.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UniPool：面向专家混合架构的全球共享专家池
现代专家混合（MoE）架构通过每层独立的专家容量分配规则进行资源分配：每个Transformer层拥有独立的专家集合。这一惯例将深度扩展与线性专家参数增长耦合，并假设每层都需要独立的专家容量。然而，近期分析及我们的路由探测挑战了这一分配规则：在多个生产级MoE模型中，将深层学习的Top-k路由替换为均匀随机路由，仅导致下游准确率下降1.0-1.6点。受此冗余启发，我们提出UniPool，一种MoE架构，将专家容量视为全局架构预算，通过单一共享池替代每层独立专家所有权，各层路由器独立访问该池。为实现共享下的稳定与均衡训练，我们引入池级辅助损失以平衡整个池中专家利用率，并采用NormRouter提供稀疏且尺度稳定的路由至共享专家池。在五个LLaMA架构模型规模（182M、469M、650M、830M、978M参数）上，使用Pile数据集30B tokens训练，UniPool在验证损失和困惑度上持续优于匹配的原始MoE基线。在这些规模下，UniPool相较原始MoE验证损失降低最高达0.0386。除损失提升外，我们的结果表明池大小是显式的深度扩展超参数：使用仅41.6%-66.7%原始专家参数预算的缩减池UniPool变体，在测试规模下可匹配或超越逐层MoE。这表明，在共享池设计下，专家参数无需随深度线性增长；其可呈亚线性增长，同时比原始MoE更高效且更有效。进一步分析表明，UniPool的优势可与更细粒度的专家分解协同作用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that per-layer expert allocation in MoE models is inefficient and unnecessary, UniPool reimagines expert capacity as a globally shared pool accessible via independent per-layer routers. To ensure balanced utilization and stable training, it introduces a pool-level auxiliary loss and employs NormRouter for sparse, scale-stable routing. Across five LLaMA-scale models trained on 30B tokens, UniPool consistently outperforms vanilla MoE, reducing validation loss by up to 0.0386 relative. Crucially, it demonstrates that expert parameters need not scale linearly with depth—variants using only 41.6%–66.7% of the vanilla budget match or exceed layer-wise MoE performance, revealing a sublinear scaling path that enhances efficiency.
为解决MoE模型中每层独立专家池导致的资源冗余问题，UniPool提出全局共享专家池，各层通过独立路由器访问，将专家容量视为统一架构预算。为保障训练时专家利用率均衡，引入池级辅助损失与NormRouter实现稳定稀疏路由。在五个LLaMA架构模型上，UniPool持续优于基线MoE，验证损失降低最高达0.0386。更重要的是，其专家容量无需随深度线性增长——仅使用41.6%至66.7%专家参数的变体即可匹配或超越分层MoE，证明共享池设计下可实现亚线性增长，更高效有效。
BAMI: Training-Free Bias Mitigation in GUI Grounding
Authors: Borui Zhang, Bo Zhang, Bo Wang, Wenzhao Zheng, Yuhao Cheng, Liang Tang, Yiqiang Yan, Jie Zhou, Jiwen Lu
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-07T17:59:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:59:31+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
GUI grounding is a critical capability for enabling GUI agents to execute tasks such as clicking and dragging. However, in complex scenarios like the ScreenSpot-Pro benchmark, existing models often suffer from suboptimal performance. Utilizing the proposed \textbf{Masked Prediction Distribution (MPD)} attribution method, we identify that the primary sources of errors are twofold: high image resolution (leading to precision bias) and intricate interface elements (resulting in ambiguity bias). To address these challenges, we introduce \textbf{Bias-Aware Manipulation Inference (BAMI)}, which incorporates two key manipulations, coarse-to-fine focus and candidate selection, to effectively mitigate these biases. Our extensive experimental results demonstrate that BAMI significantly enhances the accuracy of various GUI grounding models in a training-free setting. For instance, applying our method to the TianXi-Action-7B model boosts its accuracy on the ScreenSpot-Pro benchmark from 51.9\% to 57.8\%. Furthermore, ablation studies confirm the robustness of the BAMI approach across diverse parameter configurations, highlighting its stability and effectiveness. Code is available at https://github.com/Neur-IO/BAMI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BAMI：无训练偏见缓解的GUI定位方法
GUI定位是使GUI代理执行点击和拖拽等任务的关键能力。然而，在复杂场景如ScreenSpot-Pro基准测试中，现有模型常表现不佳。通过提出的\textbf{掩码预测分布（MPD）}归因方法，我们发现错误主要源于两类因素：高图像分辨率（导致精度偏见）和复杂界面元素（导致模糊偏见）。为解决这些问题，我们引入\textbf{偏见感知操作推理（BAMI）}，结合粗到细聚焦和候选选择两种关键操作，有效缓解上述偏见。大量实验表明，BAMI在无训练条件下显著提升了各类GUI定位模型的准确性。例如，将该方法应用于TianXi-Action-7B模型，其在ScreenSpot-Pro基准测试中的准确率从51.9%提升至57.8%。消融实验进一步验证了BAMI在不同参数配置下的鲁棒性，凸显其稳定性和有效性。代码见https://github.com/Neur-IO/BAMI。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor performance of existing GUI grounding models in complex scenarios like ScreenSpot-Pro, this work identifies two primary error sources—precision bias from high image resolution and ambiguity bias from intricate interface elements—using the Masked Prediction Distribution method. To address these, it proposes Bias-Aware Manipulation Inference (BAMI), which applies coarse-to-fine focus and candidate selection to reduce bias without requiring retraining. Experiments show BAMI significantly improves accuracy, boosting TianXi-Action-7B’s performance on ScreenSpot-Pro from 51.9% to 57.8%, with ablation studies confirming its robustness across model configurations.
为提升复杂场景下GUI定位的准确性，该研究通过Masked Prediction Distribution（MPD）分析识别出高分辨率图像导致的精度偏差和复杂界面元素引发的歧义偏差两大错误来源。提出训练免依赖的Bias-Aware Manipulation Inference（BAMI）方法，结合粗到细聚焦与候选选择策略以缓解上述偏差。实验表明，BAMI显著提升多种GUI定位模型的准确率，例如将TianXi-Action-7B在ScreenSpot-Pro基准上的准确率从51.9%提升至57.8%，且消融实验验证其在不同参数配置下表现稳定有效。
EMO: Pretraining Mixture of Experts for Emergent Modularity
Authors: Ryan Wang, Akshita Bhagia, Sewon Min
First: 2026-05-07T17:59:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:59:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models are typically deployed as monolithic systems, requiring the full model even when applications need only a narrow subset of capabilities, e.g., code, math, or domain-specific knowledge. Mixture-of-Experts (MoEs) seemingly offer a potential alternative by activating only a subset of experts per input, but in practice, restricting inference to a subset of experts for a given domain leads to severe performance degradation. This limits their practicality in memory-constrained settings, especially as models grow larger and sparser. We introduce EMO, an MoE designed for modularity-the independent use and composition of expert subsets-without requiring human-defined priors. Our key idea is to encourage tokens from similar domains to rely on similar experts. Since tokens within a document often share a domain, EMO restricts them to select experts from a shared pool, while allowing different documents to use different pools. This simple constraint enables coherent expert groupings to emerge during pretraining using document boundaries alone. We pretrain a 1B-active, 14B-total EMO on 1T tokens. As a full model, it matches standard MoE performance. Crucially, it enables selective expert use: retaining only 25% (12.5%) of experts incurs just a 1% (3%) absolute drop, whereas standard MoEs break under the same setting. We further find that expert subsets in EMO specialize at semantic levels (e.g., domains such as math or code), in contrast to the low-level syntactic specialization observed in standard MoEs. Altogether, our results demonstrate a path toward modular, memory-efficient deployment of large, sparse models and open new opportunities for composable architectures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EMO：用于涌现模块化的专家混合预训练
大型语言模型通常作为单体系统部署，即使应用仅需窄范围能力（如代码、数学或领域知识），仍需完整模型。专家混合（MoE）看似可通过仅激活输入对应的专家子集提供替代方案，但实践中，对特定领域限制仅激活部分专家会导致性能严重下降，这在内存受限环境下尤其限制其实用性，尤其当模型规模增大且更稀疏时。我们提出EMO，一种无需人工先验定义即可实现模块化（即专家子集的独立使用与组合）的MoE架构。核心思想是鼓励来自相似领域的token依赖相似专家。由于文档内token通常共享领域，EMO限制它们从共享专家池中选择专家，同时允许不同文档使用不同专家池。这一简单约束仅通过文档边界即可在预训练中促使连贯的专家分组涌现。我们在1T token上预训练了一个1B活跃、14B总参数的EMO模型。作为完整模型，其性能与标准MoE相当。关键的是，它支持选择性专家使用：仅保留25%（12.5%）专家时，性能仅下降1%（3%），而标准MoE在此设置下则失效。我们进一步发现，EMO中的专家子集在语义层面（如数学或代码等领域）实现专业化，与标准MoE中观察到的低层次句法专业化形成对比。总体而言，我们的结果展示了大型稀疏模型模块化、内存高效部署的路径，并为可组合架构开辟了新机遇。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of monolithic large language models that require full computation for narrow tasks, the paper proposes EMO, a Mixture-of-Experts architecture designed to enable emergent modularity without human-defined priors. EMO encourages tokens from similar domains to rely on shared expert pools, constrained by document boundaries during pretraining, which naturally induces coherent expert groupings. Evaluated on a 1B-active, 14B-total model pretrained on 1T tokens, EMO matches standard MoE performance as a full model but enables selective expert usage: retaining only 25% of experts causes just a 1% absolute performance drop, far outperforming standard MoEs that degrade severely under the same sparsity. Additionally, EMO’s experts specialize at semantic levels like math or code, contrasting with the syntactic specialization in conventional MoEs.
为解决大型语言模型在窄任务中仍需完整计算的低效问题，本文提出EMO架构，一种无需人工先验即可实现模块化涌现的专家混合模型。EMO通过文档边界约束，使同域令牌共享专家池，促使专家在预训练中自发形成语义一致的分组。在1T token数据上预训练1B活跃专家后，EMO在完整模型下性能与标准MoE相当，但在仅保留25%专家时仍仅损失1%性能，远优于标准MoE的失效表现。其专家分工呈现语义层面（如数学、代码）的特化，与标准MoE的句法特化形成对比。
Multi-Robot Coordination in V2X Environments
Authors: John Pravin Arockiasamy, Alexey Vinel
First: 2026-05-07T17:58:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:58:48+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication at the IEEE Intelligent Transportation Systems Conference (ITSC), 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) communication framework that enables decentralized cooperation among social robots operating in complex urban traffic environments. Building on ETSI Cooperative Awareness and Maneuver Coordination services, the framework introduces two robot-centric facility-layer services: the Robot Awareness Service (RAS) and the Robot Maneuver Coordination Service (RMCS), realized through the Robot Awareness Message (RAM) and the Robot Maneuver Coordination Message (RMCM), respectively. RAS enables role-aware, task-oriented robot awareness while integrating externally detected Vulnerable Road Users (VRUs), including non-V2X pedestrians, into cooperative awareness. RMCS supports event-driven, low-latency coordination of robot maneuvers under explicitly established roles, without centralized infrastructure or prior pairing. A real-world proof of concept demonstrates deterministic multi-robot coordination between a humanoid robot and a quadrupedal robot assisting a pedestrian during a road-crossing scenario, governed by a formally specified finite-state coordination model. Complementary simulations evaluate robot-mediated VRU clustering in mixed V2X environments, showing that RAS-based clustering integrates non-V2X VRUs in safety-critical areas while reducing redundant transmissions from V2X-enabled VRUs, thereby lowering channel load. Together, the proposed services provide a scalable and standards-aligned foundation for integrating cooperative robots into future Connected, Cooperative, and Automated Mobility ecosystems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：V2X环境中多机器人协同
本文提出了一种车对万物（V2X）通信框架，支持在复杂城市交通环境中运行的社会机器人实现去中心化协作。该框架基于ETSI协作感知与机动协调服务，引入了两个以机器人为核心的设施层服务：机器人感知服务（RAS）和机器人机动协调服务（RMCS），分别通过机器人感知消息（RAM）和机器人机动协调消息（RMCM）实现。RAS支持角色感知、任务导向的机器人感知，并将外部检测到的脆弱道路用户（VRUs），包括非V2X行人，整合进协作感知。RMCS支持事件驱动、低延迟的机器人机动协调，在明确设定的角色下运行，无需中心化基础设施或预先配对。真实场景概念验证展示了人形机器人与四足机器人在协助行人过马路时，依据形式化定义的有限状态协调模型实现确定性多机器人协同。配套仿真评估了机器人在混合V2X环境中的VRU聚类效果，表明基于RAS的聚类可将非V2X VRU整合至安全关键区域，同时减少V2X能力VRU的冗余传输，从而降低信道负载。整体而言，所提服务为未来互联、协作与自动驾驶交通生态系统中集成协作机器人提供了可扩展且符合标准的基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for decentralized, scalable coordination of social robots in complex urban traffic environments, this paper introduces a V2X framework featuring two robot-centric services: the Robot Awareness Service (RAS) and the Robot Maneuver Coordination Service (RMCS), implemented via RAM and RMCM messages. RAS enables role-aware awareness by integrating non-V2X pedestrians into cooperative perception, while RMCS supports low-latency, event-driven maneuver coordination without central infrastructure or prior pairing. Experimental results from a real-world scenario show deterministic coordination between a humanoid and quadruped robot assisting a pedestrian, governed by a formal finite-state model. Simulations further demonstrate that RAS-based clustering effectively incorporates non-V2X VRUs in safety-critical zones while reducing redundant transmissions from V2X-enabled users, thereby lowering channel load.
为实现复杂城市交通环境中社交机器人的去中心化、可扩展协同，本文提出基于V2X的框架，包含两个机器人中心服务：机器人感知服务（RAS）与机器人机动协调服务（RMCS），分别通过RAM与RMCM消息实现。RAS支持角色感知的感知融合，将非V2X行人纳入协同感知；RMCS实现事件驱动、低延迟的机动协调，无需中心化基础设施或预先配对。实验证明，人形与四足机器人在协助行人过街时，可基于形式化有限状态模型实现确定性协同。仿真结果表明，基于RAS的行人聚类可提升关键区域安全整合效率，减少V2X设备冗余传输，降低信道负载。
Verifier-Backed Hard Problem Generation for Mathematical Reasoning
Authors: Yuhang Lai, Jiazhan Feng, Yee Whye Teh, Ning Miao
First: 2026-05-07T17:58:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:58:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate strong capabilities for solving scientific and mathematical problems, yet they struggle to produce valid, challenging, and novel problems - an essential component for advancing LLM training and enabling autonomous scientific research. Existing problem generation approaches either depend on expensive human expert involvement or adopt naive self-play paradigms, which frequently yield invalid problems due to reward hacking. This work introduces VHG, a verifier-enhanced hard problem generation framework built upon three-party self-play. By integrating an independent verifier into the conventional setter-solver duality, our design constrains the setter's reward to be jointly determined by problem validity (evaluated by the verifier) and difficulty (assessed by the solver). We instantiate two verifier variants: a Hard symbolic verifier and a Soft LLM-based verifier, with evaluations conducted on indefinite integral tasks and general mathematical reasoning tasks. Experimental results show that VHG substantially outperforms all baseline methods by a clear margin.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于验证器的数学推理难题生成
大型语言模型（LLMs）在解决科学和数学问题方面表现出强大能力，但难以生成有效、具有挑战性和新颖的问题——这是推动LLM训练和实现自主科学研究的关键组成部分。现有问题生成方法要么依赖昂贵的人类专家参与，要么采用简单的自对弈范式，常因奖励作弊导致生成无效问题。本文提出VHG，一种基于三方自对弈的验证器增强型难题生成框架。通过将独立验证器整合进传统的出题者-解题者二元结构，我们的设计使出题者的奖励由问题有效性（由验证器评估）和难度（由解题者评估）共同决定。我们实现了两种验证器变体：硬符号验证器和软LLM验证器，并在不定积分任务和通用数学推理任务上进行评估。实验结果表明，VHG显著优于所有基线方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for LLMs to autonomously generate valid and challenging mathematical problems to advance training and scientific research, this work proposes VHG, a verifier-enhanced framework using three-party self-play. It integrates an independent verifier to jointly constrain the setter’s reward based on problem validity and difficulty, preventing reward hacking and invalid outputs. Experiments on indefinite integrals and general math reasoning show VHG significantly outperforms existing baselines.
为推动大语言模型自主生成有效且具有挑战性的数学问题以促进训练和科研，本文提出VHG框架，通过引入独立验证器构建三方自博弈系统，约束生成者奖励需同时满足验证器评估的问题有效性与求解者评估的难度，避免奖励欺骗和无效输出。在不定积分与通用数学推理任务上的实验表明，VHG显著优于现有基线方法，包括依赖人工专家和朴素自博弈的方法。
Relit-LiVE: Relight Video by Jointly Learning Environment Video
Authors: Weiqing Xiao, Hong Li, Xiuyu Yang, Houyuan Chen, Wenyi Li, Tianqi Liu, Shaocong Xu, Chongjie Ye, Hao Zhao, Beibei Wang
Venue: SIGGRAPH 2026
First: 2026-05-07T17:58:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:58:15+00:00
Comments: Accepted at SIGGRAPH 2026. Project site: https://github.com/zhuxing0/Relit-LiVE
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent advances have shown that large-scale video diffusion models can be repurposed as neural renderers by first decomposing videos into intrinsic scene representations and then performing forward rendering under novel illumination. While promising, this paradigm fundamentally relies on accurate intrinsic decomposition, which remains highly unreliable for real-world videos and often leads to distorted appearances, broken materials, and accumulated temporal artifacts during relighting. In this work, we present Relit-LiVE, a novel video relighting framework that produces physically consistent, temporally stable results without requiring prior knowledge of camera pose. Our key insight is to explicitly introduce raw reference images into the rendering process, enabling the model to recover critical scene cues that are inevitably lost or corrupted in intrinsic representations. Furthermore, we propose a novel environment video prediction formulation that simultaneously generates relit videos and per-frame environment maps aligned with each camera viewpoint in a single diffusion process. This joint prediction enforces strong geometric-illumination alignment and naturally supports dynamic lighting and camera motion, significantly improving physical consistency in video relighting while easing the requirement of known per-frame camera pose. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Relit-LiVE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art video relighting and neural rendering methods across synthetic and real-world benchmarks. Beyond relighting, our framework naturally supports a wide range of downstream applications, including scene-level rendering, material editing, object insertion, and streaming video relighting. The Project is available at https://github.com/zhuxing0/Relit-LiVE.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Relit-LiVE：通过联合学习环境视频实现视频重照明
近期研究表明，大规模视频扩散模型可通过先将视频分解为内在场景表示，再在新光照条件下进行前向渲染，从而被重新用作神经渲染器。然而，该范式依赖于精确的内在分解，而真实世界视频中的内在分解往往极不可靠，常导致重照明后外观失真、材质断裂及时间累积伪影。本文提出Relit-LiVE，一种无需预先知道相机姿态即可生成物理一致、时间稳定的视频重照明结果的新框架。我们的核心思路是显式引入原始参考图像进入渲染过程，使模型能恢复在内在表示中必然丢失或损坏的关键场景线索。此外，我们提出一种新颖的环境视频预测公式，通过单次扩散过程同时生成重照明视频及与各相机视角对齐的逐帧环境图。该联合预测强制实现几何-光照强对齐，自然支持动态光照与相机运动，显著提升视频重照明的物理一致性，同时降低对逐帧相机姿态的依赖。大量实验表明，Relit-LiVE在合成与真实世界基准上持续优于现有最先进的视频重照明与神经渲染方法。除重照明外，本框架天然支持多种下游应用，包括场景级渲染、材质编辑、物体插入及流媒体视频重照明。项目地址：https://github.com/zhuxing0/Relit-LiVE。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of intrinsic decomposition in real-world video relighting, this work introduces Relit-LiVE, a framework that jointly learns environment video and relit output using diffusion models without requiring camera pose. It incorporates raw reference images to recover lost scene cues and employs a novel environment map prediction scheme aligned with each viewpoint, enforcing geometric-illumination consistency. Experiments show it outperforms existing methods on both synthetic and real benchmarks, producing physically accurate, temporally stable results while enabling applications like material editing and object insertion.
为了解决真实视频中因内在分解不准确导致的视觉失真和时序不一致问题，Relit-LiVE提出了一种联合学习环境视频与重光照输出的新框架，通过引入原始参考图像恢复丢失的场景线索。该方法在单次扩散过程中生成与相机视角对齐的每帧环境图，无需已知相机姿态即可实现几何与光照的一致性。实验表明，Relit-LiVE在合成与真实数据集上均显著优于现有方法，同时支持材质编辑、物体插入等下游应用。
Why Global LLM Leaderboards Are Misleading: Small Portfolios for Heterogeneous Supervised ML
Authors: Jai Moondra, Ayela Chughtai, Bhargavi Lanka, Swati Gupta
First: 2026-05-07T17:57:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:57:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Ranking LLMs via pairwise human feedback underpins current leaderboards for open-ended tasks, such as creative writing and problem-solving. We analyze ~89K comparisons in 116 languages from 52 LLMs from Arena, and show that the best-fit global Bradley-Terry (BT) ranking is misleading. Nearly 2/3 of the decisive votes cancel out, and even the top 50 models according to the global BT ranking are statistically indistinguishable (pairwise win probabilities are at most 0.53 within the top 50 models). We trace this failure to strong, structured heterogeneity of opinions across language, task, and time. Moreover, we find an important characteristic - *language* plays a key role. Grouping by language (and families) increases the agreement of votes massively, resulting in two orders of magnitude higher spread in the ELO scores (i.e., very consistent rankings). What appears as global noise is in fact a mixture of coherent but conflicting subpopulations. To address such heterogeneity in supervised machine learning, we introduce the framework of $(λ, ν)$-portfolios, which are small sets of models that achieve a prediction error at most $λ$, "covering" at least a $ν$ fraction of users. We formulate this as a variant of the set cover problem and provide guarantees using the VC dimension of the underlying set system. On the Arena data, our algorithms recover just 5 distinct BT rankings that cover over 96% of votes at a modest $λ$, compared to the 21% coverage by the global ranking. We also provide a portfolio of 6 LLMs that cover twice as many votes as the top-6 LLMs from a global ranking. We further construct portfolios for a classification problem on the COMPAS dataset using an ensemble of fairness-regularized classification models and show that these portfolios can be used to detect blind spots in the data, which might be of independent interest to policymakers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：为何全球大语言模型排行榜具有误导性：异质监督机器学习中的小组合
当前开放任务（如创意写作和问题解决）的大语言模型排行榜基于成对人类反馈进行排序。我们分析了Arena平台中52个模型在116种语言下的约8.9万次比较，发现最佳拟合的全局Bradley-Terry（BT）排序具有误导性：近三分之二的决定性投票相互抵消，即使按全局BT排名前50的模型，在统计上也无显著差异（前50模型间的胜率最多为0.53）。我们归因于语言、任务和时间维度上意见的强结构异质性。此外，我们发现一个关键特征——*语言*起关键作用：按语言（及语系）分组可大幅提高投票一致性，使ELO分数差异提升两个数量级（即排名高度一致）。看似全局噪声，实为多个具内在一致性但相互冲突的子群体的混合。 为解决监督机器学习中的此类异质性，我们引入$(λ, ν)$-组合框架：即一组小规模模型，其预测误差不超过$λ$，且覆盖至少$ν$比例的用户。我们将此问题建模为集合覆盖问题的变体，并利用底层集合系统的VC维提供理论保证。在Arena数据上，我们的算法仅需5个不同BT排序即可覆盖超过96%的投票（在适度$λ$下），而全局排序仅覆盖21%。我们还构建了一个包含6个大语言模型的组合，其覆盖投票数是全局排名前6模型的两倍。进一步地，我们在COMPAS数据集的分类问题上，使用公平性正则化分类模型的集成构建组合，并证明这些组合可用于检测数据盲区，对政策制定者可能具有独立价值。
Summary / 总结
The paper critiques global LLM leaderboards for being misleading due to strong heterogeneity in human preferences across languages, tasks, and time, which causes decisive votes to largely cancel out and renders top-ranked models statistically indistinguishable. To address this, it introduces (λ, ν)-portfolios—small model sets that cover a significant fraction of users while maintaining bounded error—and frames the problem as a set cover variant with VC-dimension guarantees. Experiments on Arena data show that just five portfolios cover over 96% of votes, vastly outperforming the global ranking’s 21% coverage, and a six-model portfolio covers twice as many votes as the global top six. The framework also demonstrates utility in detecting data blind spots using fairness-regularized models on COMPAS.
该文指出全球LLM排行榜因未考虑语言、任务和时间维度的人类反馈异质性而误导性显著，顶级模型间统计上难以区分。作者提出(λ, ν)-组合框架——小规模模型集合，能在误差上限内覆盖大量用户，并基于集合覆盖理论与VC维提供理论保证。在Arena数据上，仅5个组合即可覆盖96%的投票，远优于全局排名；同时，该方法还能在公平性分类任务中识别数据盲区，对政策制定者具独立价值。
REMAP: Regularized Matching and Partial Alignment of Video Embeddings
Authors: Soumyadeep Chandra, Kaushik Roy
First: 2025-09-29T07:32:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:57:56+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 4 figures, 6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-world instructional videos are long, noisy, and often contain extended background segments, repeated actions, and execution variability that do not correspond to meaningful procedural steps. We propose **REMAP**, an unsupervised framework for procedure learning based on *Regularized Fused Partial Gromov-Wasserstein Optimal Transport*. REMAP relaxes balanced transport constraints, allowing non-informative or redundant frames to remain unmatched through partial transport. The formulation jointly models semantic similarity and temporal structure, while incorporating Laplacian-based smoothness and structural regularization to prevent degenerate alignments and reduce background interference. We evaluate REMAP on large-scale egocentric and third-person benchmarks. The method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, achieving up to **11.6\% (+4.45pp)** F1 and **19.6\% (+4.73pp)** IoU improvements on EgoProceL, and an average **41\% (+17.15pp)** F1 gain on ProceL and CrossTask. These results highlight the importance of partial alignment in handling real-world procedural variability and demonstrate that REMAP provides a robust and scalable approach for instructional video understanding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：REMAP：视频嵌入的正则化匹配与部分对齐
现实世界中的教学视频通常较长、噪声大，且常包含冗长的背景片段、重复动作和与有意义的程序步骤不对应的执行变体。我们提出**REMAP**，一种基于*正则化融合部分Gromov-Wasserstein最优传输*的无监督程序学习框架。REMAP放松了平衡传输约束，允许非信息性或冗余帧通过部分传输保持未匹配。该方法联合建模语义相似性和时间结构，同时引入基于拉普拉斯的平滑性和结构正则化，以防止退化对齐并减少背景干扰。我们在大规模第一人称和第三人称基准数据集上评估REMAP，结果表明该方法持续优于现有最先进方法，在EgoProceL上F1提升高达**11.6%（+4.45pp）**，IoU提升**19.6%（+4.73pp）**，在ProceL和CrossTask上平均F1提升**41%（+17.15pp）**。这些结果突显了部分对齐在处理现实世界程序变体中的重要性，并证明REMAP为教学视频理解提供了一种稳健且可扩展的方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of aligning long, noisy instructional videos with variable actions and background content, REMAP introduces an unsupervised framework based on regularized fused partial Gromov-Wasserstein optimal transport. It relaxes balanced transport constraints to allow non-informative frames to remain unmatched, while jointly modeling semantic similarity and temporal structure through Laplacian-based smoothness and structural regularization. Evaluated on egocentric and third-person benchmarks, REMAP consistently outperforms prior methods, achieving up to 11.6% higher F1 and 19.6% higher IoU on EgoProceL, and a 41% F1 gain on ProceL and CrossTask, demonstrating its effectiveness in handling real-world procedural variability.
为应对长时、噪声大且含冗余背景与重复动作的真实教学视频对齐难题，REMAP提出一种基于正则化融合部分Gromov-Wasserstein最优传输的无监督框架，通过放松平衡传输约束允许非信息帧不匹配，同时联合建模语义相似性与时间结构，并引入拉普拉斯平滑与结构正则化防止退化对齐与背景干扰。在多个第一人称与第三人称基准数据集上，该方法在EgoProceL上实现最高11.6% F1与19.6% IoU提升，在ProceL与CrossTask上平均F1提升达41%，验证了部分对齐在处理真实世界程序性变异中的关键作用。
Optimizer-Model Consistency: Full Finetuning with the Same Optimizer as Pretraining Forgets Less
Authors: Yuxing Liu, Jianyu Wang, Tong Zhang
First: 2026-05-07T17:57:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:57:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Optimizers play an important role in both pretraining and finetuning stages when training large language models (LLMs). In this paper, we present an observation that full finetuning with the same optimizer as in pretraining achieves a better learning-forgetting tradeoff, i.e., forgetting less while achieving the same or better performance on the new task, than other optimizers and, possibly surprisingly, LoRA, during the supervised finetuning (SFT) stage. We term this phenomenon optimizer-model consistency. To better understand it, through controlled experiments and theoretical analysis, we show that: 1) optimizers can shape the models by having regularization effects on the activations, leading to different landscapes around the pretrained checkpoints; 2) in response to this regularization effect, the weight update in SFT should follow some specific structures to lower forgetting of the knowledge learned in pretraining, which can be obtained by using the same optimizer. Moreover, we specifically compare Muon and AdamW when they are employed throughout the pretraining and SFT stages and find that Muon performs worse when finetuned for reasoning tasks. With a synthetic language modeling experiment, we demonstrate that this can come from Muon's strong tendency towards rote memorization, which may hurt pattern acquisition with a small amount of data, as for SFT.
中文标题/摘要
标题：优化器-模型一致性：使用与预训练相同优化器的全量微调遗忘更少
优化器在大型语言模型（LLM）的预训练和微调阶段均扮演重要角色。本文观察到，在监督微调（SFT）阶段，使用与预训练相同的优化器进行全量微调，相比其他优化器（甚至可能令人惊讶的是LoRA），能实现更好的学习-遗忘权衡，即在保持或提升新任务性能的同时遗忘更少。我们将此现象称为优化器-模型一致性。为深入理解该现象，通过受控实验与理论分析，我们发现：1）优化器可通过激活的正则化效应塑造模型，导致预训练检查点周围形成不同的优化景观；2）为降低预训练知识的遗忘，SFT阶段的权重更新应遵循特定结构，而使用相同优化器可实现此结构。此外，我们特别比较了Muon与AdamW在预训练和SFT阶段全程使用时的表现，发现Muon在推理任务微调时表现更差。通过合成语言建模实验，我们表明这可能源于Muon对机械记忆的强烈倾向，这在数据量较少时可能损害模式获取能力，尤其在SFT场景中。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how optimizer choice affects knowledge retention during supervised fine-tuning of large language models, motivated by the observation that using the same optimizer as during pretraining leads to less forgetting while maintaining or improving task performance. The authors demonstrate through controlled experiments and theoretical analysis that optimizers influence model behavior via regularization of activations, altering the local landscape around pretrained checkpoints; fine-tuning with the same optimizer aligns weight updates to preserve pretraining knowledge. Empirical comparisons reveal that Muon, despite its effectiveness in pretraining, performs worse on reasoning tasks during fine-tuning due to its tendency toward rote memorization, which impedes pattern learning with limited data.
为减少大语言模型监督微调过程中的知识遗忘，本研究探讨使用与预训练阶段相同的优化器是否能提升学习效率。实验发现，全程使用相同优化器进行全参数微调可更有效地在新任务上保持性能的同时减少遗忘，这一现象称为‘优化器-模型一致性’。通过控制实验与理论分析，研究指出优化器通过激活正则化塑造模型参数空间，而与预训练阶段一致的优化器能引导更有利于保留知识的权重更新。对比Muon与AdamW发现，Muon在小数据下倾向于机械记忆，损害模式学习能力，导致其在推理任务上表现更差。
When No Benchmark Exists: Validating Comparative LLM Safety Scoring Without Ground-Truth Labels
Authors: Sushant Gautam, Finn Schwall, Annika Willoch Olstad, Fernando Vallecillos Ruiz, Birk Torpmann-Hagen, Sunniva Maria Stordal Bjørklund, Leon Moonen, Klas Pettersen, Michael A. Riegler
First: 2026-05-07T17:56:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:56:41+00:00
Comments: SimpleAudit Repository: https://github.com/kelkalot/simpleaudit
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Many deployments must compare candidate language models for safety before a labeled benchmark exists for the relevant language, sector, or regulatory regime. We formalize this setting as benchmarkless comparative safety scoring and specify the contract under which a scenario-based audit can be interpreted as deployment evidence. Scores are valid only under a fixed scenario pack, rubric, auditor, judge, sampling configuration, and rerun budget. Because no labels are available, we replace ground-truth agreement with an instrumental-validity chain: responsiveness to a controlled safe-versus-abliterated contrast, dominance of target-driven variance over auditor and judge artifacts, and stability across reruns. We instantiate the chain in SimpleAudit, a local-first scoring instrument, and validate it on a Norwegian safety pack. Safe and abliterated targets separate with AUROC values between 0.89 and 1.00, target identity is the dominant variance component ($η^2 \approx 0.52$), and severity profiles stabilize by ten reruns. Applying the same chain to Petri shows that it admits both tools. The substantial differences arise upstream of the chain, in claim-contract enforcement and deployment fit. A Norwegian public-sector procurement case comparing Borealis and Gemma 3 demonstrates the resulting evidence in practice: the safer model depends on scenario category and risk measure. Consequently, scores, matched deltas, critical rates, uncertainty, and the auditor and judge used must be reported together rather than collapsed into a single ranking.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无基准时的验证：在无真实标签情况下验证比较式大语言模型安全评分
许多部署必须在相关语言、行业或监管体系尚无标注基准的情况下，比较候选语言模型的安全性。我们将此设定形式化为“无基准比较式安全评分”，并明确情景审计可作为部署证据的契约条件。评分仅在固定的情景包、评分标准、审计员、裁判、抽样配置和重跑预算下有效。由于无真实标签，我们以工具有效性链替代真实标签一致性：对可控安全与非安全对比的响应性、目标驱动方差对审计员与裁判偏差的主导性，以及重跑过程中的稳定性。我们通过本地优先评分工具SimpleAudit实例化该链，并在挪威安全情景包上验证：安全与非安全目标分离度AUROC介于0.89至1.00之间，目标身份是主导方差成分（η²≈0.52），严重性分布于十次重跑后稳定。将相同链应用于Petri表明其兼容两种工具。显著差异源于链上游的声明-契约执行与部署适配性。挪威公共部门采购案例对比Borealis与Gemma 3展示了实际证据：更安全模型依赖于情景类别与风险度量。因此，评分、匹配差值、临界率、不确定性及所用审计员与裁判必须一并报告，而非合并为单一排名。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to compare language models for safety in real-world deployments without existing labeled benchmarks, the paper formalizes benchmarkless comparative safety scoring and defines conditions under which scenario-based audits can serve as valid deployment evidence. It introduces an instrumental-validity chain replacing ground-truth labels, requiring responsiveness to safe-versus-abliterated contrasts, dominance of target-driven variance over auditor/judge artifacts, and stability across reruns. Evaluated via SimpleAudit on a Norwegian safety pack, the method achieves AUROC scores between 0.89 and 1.00, identifies target identity as the dominant variance component (η² ≈ 0.52), and confirms stability by ten reruns. Applied to Petri, it shows compatibility with both tools, but differences arise from upstream claim-contract enforcement and deployment fit. A public-sector procurement case comparing Borealis and Gemma 3 reveals that safety outcomes depend on scenario category and risk measure, necessitating full reporting of scores, deltas, uncertainty, and auditor/judge details rather than collapsing into a single ranking.
为在缺乏标注基准的情况下比较语言模型的安全性，本文形式化了无基准的比较安全评分框架，并定义了场景审计可作为部署证据的合同条件。方法通过一个替代真实标签的工具链验证有效性：模型需对安全与破坏性场景敏感、目标驱动的方差需主导审计员与评判者偏差、且评分需在多次重跑中稳定。在挪威安全场景包上测试SimpleAudit，安全与破坏性目标AUROC达0.89至1.00，目标身份贡献主导方差（η²≈0.52），十次重跑后稳定性达标。应用于Petri时，该工具链验证其有效性，但差异源于上游合同执行与部署适配。挪威公共部门采购案例对比Borealis与Gemma 3显示，安全结果依赖场景类别与风险度量，因此评分、差异、置信率、不确定性及审计员与评判者必须一并报告，而非简化为单一排名。
AI Co-Mathematician: Accelerating Mathematicians with Agentic AI
Authors: Daniel Zheng, Ingrid von Glehn, Yori Zwols, Iuliya Beloshapka, Lars Buesing, Daniel M. Roy, Martin Wattenberg, Bogdan Georgiev, Tatiana Schmidt, Andrew Cowie, Fernanda Viegas, Dimitri Kanevsky, Vineet Kahlon, Hartmut Maennel, Sophia Alj, George Holland, Alex Davies, Pushmeet Kohli
First: 2026-05-07T17:56:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:56:32+00:00
Comments: 22 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce the AI co-mathematician, a workbench for mathematicians to interactively leverage AI agents to pursue open-ended research. The AI co-mathematician is optimized to provide holistic support for the exploratory and iterative reality of mathematical workflows, including ideation, literature search, computational exploration, theorem proving and theory building. By providing an asynchronous, stateful workspace that manages uncertainty, refines user intent, tracks failed hypotheses, and outputs native mathematical artifacts, the system mirrors human collaborative workflows. In early tests, the AI co-mathematician helped researchers solve open problems, identify new research directions, and uncover overlooked literature references. Besides demonstrating a highly interactive paradigm for AI-assisted mathematical discovery, the AI co-mathematician also achieves state of the art results on hard problem-solving benchmarks, including scoring 48% on FrontierMath Tier 4, a new high score among all AI systems evaluated.

Beyond Negative Rollouts: Positive-Only Policy Optimization with Implicit Negative Gradients
Authors: Mingwei Xu, Hao Fang
First: 2026-05-07T17:55:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:55:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), due to the deterministic verification, becomes a dominant paradigm for enhancing the reasoning ability of large language models (LLMs). The community witnesses the rapid change from the Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) to Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), in which GRPO reduces the complicated advantage estimation with simple estimation over grouped positive and negative rollouts. However, we note that negative rollouts may admit no gradation of failure severity, and the combinatorial vastness makes penalizing a few sampled negatives unlikely to cover a meaningful reward signal under sparse binary rewards. In this work, we propose Positive-Only Policy Optimization (POPO), a novel RLVR framework in which learning can occur exclusively via online positive rollouts. Specifically, POPO utilizes bounded importance sampling over the positive rollout set. Thus, no disjoint negative rollouts are used for the gradient guidance. We show that implicit negative gradients can emerge naturally through reinforcing the positive probability via rollouts redistribution. Next, POPO stabilizes the policy optimization through two mechanisms. First, it applies a siamese policy network with a momentum-based adaptation law for stabilized policy evolution. Second, we replace the KL-divergence with a bounded similarity penalty term in the siamese representation space. We conduct extensive experiments using publicly available, well-established text-LLM models, e.g., the Qwen family, across all-level mathematical benchmarks. Our experiment demonstrates that POPO achieves performance comparable to, or even superior to GRPO. Notably, we show that POPO can achieve 36.67% in AIME 2025 with Qwen-Math-7B, outperforming GRPO 30.00%. Our ablation and sweep studies further illustrate the necessity and robustness of POPO components.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越负向回放：仅用正向回放的隐式负梯度策略优化
由于可验证奖励的确定性验证，基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）已成为增强大语言模型（LLMs）推理能力的主流范式。社区见证了从近端策略优化（PPO）到组相对策略优化（GRPO）的快速演进，其中GRPO通过分组正负回放的简单估计简化了复杂的优势估计。然而，我们注意到负向回放可能缺乏失败严重程度的渐进性，且组合规模巨大，使得在稀疏二元奖励下，仅惩罚少量采样负例难以覆盖有意义的奖励信号。本文提出仅正向策略优化（POPO），一种全新的RLVR框架，学习可完全通过在线正向回放实现。具体而言，POPO在正向回放集合上使用有界重要性采样。因此，无需独立负向回放用于梯度引导。我们证明，通过回放重分配强化正向概率，隐式负梯度可自然涌现。随后，POPO通过两种机制稳定策略优化：首先，采用双胞胎策略网络并结合动量自适应律以稳定策略演化；其次，在双胞胎表示空间中，用有界相似性惩罚项替代KL散度。我们在公开且成熟的文本LLM模型（如Qwen系列）上，于多层级数学基准测试中进行大量实验。实验表明，POPO性能可媲美甚至优于GRPO。值得注意的是，POPO在AIME 2025中以Qwen-Math-7B模型取得36.67%的分数，优于GRPO的30.00%。我们的消融与扫描研究进一步说明了POPO各组件的必要性与鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of negative rollouts in RLVR frameworks—such as lack of gradation in failure severity and sparse binary reward signals—this work introduces Positive-Only Policy Optimization (POPO), a novel method that learns exclusively from online positive rollouts without requiring explicit negative samples. POPO employs bounded importance sampling over positive rollouts and leverages implicit negative gradients through probability redistribution, while stabilizing optimization via a siamese policy network with momentum adaptation and a bounded similarity penalty replacing KL-divergence. Experiments on multiple text-LLM models across mathematical benchmarks show POPO achieves performance comparable to or exceeding GRPO, notably scoring 36.67% on AIME 2025 with Qwen-Math-7B, surpassing GRPO’s 30.00%.
鉴于负向轨迹在RLVR框架中存在失败严重性缺乏梯度、稀疏二元奖励难以覆盖有效信号等问题，本文提出正向轨迹独占式策略优化（POPO），仅利用在线正向轨迹进行学习，无需负向样本。POPO通过正向轨迹上的有界重要性采样隐式生成负梯度，并通过双胞胎策略网络结合动量自适应机制与有界相似性惩罚（替代KL散度）稳定优化过程。在Qwen系列大模型的数学基准测试中，POPO表现与GRPO相当甚至更优，Qwen-Math-7B在AIME 2025上达到36.67%，超越GRPO的30.00%，消融实验进一步验证了其核心组件的有效性与鲁棒性。
Superintelligent Retrieval Agent: The Next Frontier of Information Retrieval
Authors: Zeyu Yang, Qi Ma, Jason Chen, Anshumali Shrivastava
First: 2026-05-07T17:54:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:54:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval-augmented agents are increasingly the interface to large organizational knowledge bases, yet most still treat retrieval as a black box: they issue exploratory queries, inspect returned snippets, and iteratively reformulate until useful evidence emerges. This approach resembles how a newcomer searches an unfamiliar database rather than how an expert navigates it with strong priors about terminology and likely evidence, and results in unnecessary retrieval rounds, increased latency, and poor recall. We introduce \textit{SuperIntelligent Retrieval Agent} (SIRA), which defines \emph{superintelligence} in retrieval as the ability to compress multi-round exploratory search into a single corpus-discriminative retrieval action. SIRA does not merely ask what terms are relevant to the query; it asks which terms are likely to separate the desired evidence from corpus-level confusers. On the corpus side, an LLM enriches each document offline with missing search vocabulary; on the query side, it predicts evidence vocabulary omitted by the query; and document-frequency statistics as a tool call to filter proposed terms that are absent, overly common, or unlikely to create retrieval margin. The final retrieval step is a single weighted BM25 call combining the original query with the validated expansion. Across ten BEIR benchmarks and downstream question-answering tasks, SIRA achieves the significantly superior performance outperforming dense retrievers and state-of-the-art multi-round agentic baselines, demonstrating that one well-formed lexical query, guided by LLM cognition and lightweight corpus statistics, can exceed substantially more expensive multi-round search while remaining interpretable, training-free, and efficient.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超智能检索代理：信息检索的下一个前沿
检索增强型代理正日益成为大型组织知识库的接口，但大多数仍把检索视为黑箱：它们发出探索性查询，检查返回片段，并迭代重写直至出现有用证据。这种做法类似于新手搜索不熟悉的数据库，而非专家利用术语和可能证据的先验知识高效导航，导致不必要的检索轮次、增加延迟和召回率低下。 我们提出\textit{超智能检索代理}（SIRA），将\emph{超智能}定义为将多轮探索性搜索压缩为单次语料库判别性检索的能力。SIRA不仅询问哪些术语与查询相关，更询问哪些术语最可能将所需证据与语料库中的干扰项区分开。在语料库侧，大语言模型离线为每篇文档补充缺失的检索词汇；在查询侧，它预测查询中遗漏的证据词汇；并调用文档频率统计工具过滤掉缺失、过于常见或难以产生检索优势的候选术语。最终检索步骤是将原始查询与验证后的扩展词组合成单次加权BM25调用。 在十项BEIR基准和下游问答任务中，SIRA显著优于稠密检索器和最先进的多轮代理基线，证明一个由大语言模型认知引导、结合轻量语料统计的精心构造词汇查询，可大幅超越昂贵的多轮搜索，同时保持可解释性、无需训练和高效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of current retrieval-augmented agents that rely on iterative, exploratory querying, the paper proposes SIRA, a superintelligent retrieval agent that compresses multi-round search into a single, discriminative retrieval step. SIRA leverages an LLM to enrich documents offline with missing search vocabulary and predict query omissions, then uses document-frequency statistics to filter terms that lack discriminative power. The final retrieval combines the original query with validated expansions via a single weighted BM25 call. Experiments across ten BEIR benchmarks and QA tasks show SIRA outperforms dense retrievers and state-of-the-art multi-round baselines, achieving higher recall and efficiency without training or complex iteration.
针对当前检索增强代理依赖多轮探索式查询导致效率低下的问题，本文提出超智能检索代理（SIRA），将检索视为一次具有区分能力的单次操作。SIRA 通过大语言模型离线丰富文档缺失的检索词汇，并预测查询遗漏的术语，再结合文档频率统计筛选候选词，最终生成一个优化的 BM25 查询。在十项 BEIR 数据集和问答任务中，SIRA 表现显著优于稠密检索器和多轮代理基线，实现更高召回率与更低延迟，且无需训练或复杂推理循环。
Inductive Venn-Abers and related regressors
Authors: Ivan Petej, Vladimir Vovk
First: 2026-05-07T17:52:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:52:08+00:00
Comments: 33 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Venn-Abers predictors are probabilistic predictors that enjoy appealing properties of validity, but their major limitation is that they are applicable only to the case of binary classification, with a recent extension to bounded regression. We generalize them to the case of unbounded regression, which requires adding an element of conformal prediction. In our simulation and empirical studies we investigate the predictive efficiency of point regressors derived from Venn-Abers regressors and argue that they somewhat improve the predictive efficiency of standard regressors for larger training sets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：归纳型Venn-Abers及相关回归器
Venn-Abers预测器是具有良好有效性属性的概率预测器，但其主要局限在于仅适用于二分类问题，最近已扩展至有界回归。我们将其推广至无界回归情形，这需要引入一致性预测元素。在我们的模拟和实证研究中，我们考察了由Venn-Abers回归器导出的点回归器的预测效率，并认为它们在较大训练集上能略微提升标准回归器的预测效率。
Summary / 总结
