The method combines Venn-Abers principles with conformal inference to construct point regressors for unbounded outputs. Experimental results across simulations and real-world datasets show that these generalized regressors achieve improved predictive efficiency compared to standard regressors, particularly as training set size increases.
为解决Venn-Abers预测器仅适用于二分类和有界回归的局限性，该研究通过引入合意预测将其推广至无界回归。方法基于Venn-Abers回归器构建点预测器，保留其有效性优势并扩展适用范围。实验表明，这些推广后的回归器在训练集规模较大时，相较标准回归器具有适度但稳定的预测效率提升。
Edge-specific signal propagation on mature chromophore-region 3D mechanism graphs for fluorescent protein quantum-yield prediction
Authors: Yuchen Xiong, Swee Keong Yeap, Steven Aw Yoong Kit
First: 2026-05-07T17:51:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:51:41+00:00
Comments: Includes appendix; source code, processed feature tables and evaluation scripts are available from the first author upon reasonable request
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fluorescent protein quantum yield (QY) is governed by the mature chromophore and its three-dimensional microenvironment rather than sequence identity alone. Protein language models and emission-band averages capture global trends, but do not model how local physical signals act on specific chromophore regions. We present a chromophore-centred mechanism graph algorithm for QY prediction. Each PDB structure is converted into a typed 3D residue graph, registered to a mature-CRO state, partitioned into phenolate, bridge and imidazolinone regions, and transformed by channel-signal-region propagation. The representation contains 121 enrichment features; after removing identity shortcuts, 52 non-identity features are used for band-specific ExtraTrees regression. Because each feature encodes a contact channel, seed signal and target CRO region, interpretation is intrinsic rather than post hoc. On a 531-protein benchmark, the method achieved the best random-CV performance among model-based baselines (R = 0.772 +/- 0.008, MAE = 0.131 +/- 0.002), exceeding Band mean (R = 0.632), ESM-C (R = 0.734) and SaProt (R = 0.731), and ranked first in bright screening (Bright P@5 = 0.704). Under homology control, the advantage was clearest in the remote bucket (<50% similarity; R = 0.697 versus 0.633, 0.575 and 0.408), with the strongest overall bright/dark Top-K screening. Stable selected features recovered band-specific mechanisms: aromatic packing and clamp asymmetry in GFP-like proteins, charge/clamp balance in Red proteins, and flexibility-risk/bulky-contact features in Far-red proteins. Source code, feature tables and evaluation scripts are available from the first author upon request. Contact: yuchenak05@gmail.com
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于成熟发色团区域3D机制图的边缘特定信号传播荧光蛋白量子产率预测方法
荧光蛋白的量子产率（QY）由成熟发色团及其三维微环境决定，而非序列同源性。蛋白质语言模型和发射波段平均值可捕捉全局趋势，但无法建模局部物理信号如何作用于特定发色团区域。本文提出一种以发色团为中心的机制图算法用于QY预测。每个PDB结构转换为带类型标注的3D残基图，注册至成熟CRO状态，划分为酚盐、桥接和咪唑啉酮区域，并通过通道-信号-区域传播进行转换。该表示包含121个富集特征；去除序列同源性捷径后，使用52个非同源性特征进行波段特异性ExtraTrees回归。因每个特征编码接触通道、种子信号和目标CRO区域，其解释具有内在性而非事后分析。在531个蛋白基准测试中，该方法在模型基线中表现最佳（R = 0.772 ± 0.008，MAE = 0.131 ± 0.002），优于波段平均（R = 0.632）、ESM-C（R = 0.734）和SaProt（R = 0.731），并在明亮筛选中排名第一（Bright P@5 = 0.704）。在同源性控制下，优势在远缘组（<50%相似性；R = 0.697 vs 0.633, 0.575 和 0.408）最为明显，且整体明亮/暗淡Top-K筛选表现最强。稳定选择的特征恢复了波段特异性机制：GFP类蛋白中的芳香堆积与夹持不对称性，红蛋白中的电荷/夹持平衡，以及远红蛋白中的柔性风险/大体积接触特征。源代码、特征表和评估脚本可向第一作者请求获取。联系邮箱：yuchenak05@gmail.com
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model how local physical signals influence fluorescent protein quantum yield beyond sequence identity, the authors developed a chromophore-centred mechanism graph method that transforms PDB structures into 3D residue graphs partitioned into phenolate, bridge, and imidazolinone regions, then propagates signal through contact channels to encode 121 features. After removing identity shortcuts, 52 non-identity features are used for band-specific regression via ExtraTrees. On a 531-protein benchmark, the method outperformed baselines including Band mean, ESM-C, and SaProt with R = 0.772 and MAE = 0.131, and ranked first in bright screening. Under homology control, its advantage was most pronounced in remote homologs (<50% similarity), and stable features revealed distinct mechanistic patterns across GFP-like, Red, and Far-red proteins.
为解决荧光蛋白量子产率预测中序列同源性不足以捕捉局部物理信号的问题，该研究提出一种以发色团为中心的机制图方法，将PDB结构转化为带类型标注的3D残基图，按发色团区域划分，并通过通道-信号-区域传播生成121个富集特征。去除序列同源性捷径后，使用52个非同源特征进行带特定回归。在531个蛋白基准数据上，该方法优于Band mean、ESM-C和SaProt（R=0.772，MAE=0.131），在亮蛋白筛选中排名第一。在同源性控制下，其优势在远源蛋白（<50%相似度）中最为显著（R=0.697 vs 0.633、0.575、0.408），且稳定特征揭示了GFP类蛋白芳香堆积与夹持不对称、红蛋白电荷与夹持平衡、远红蛋白柔性风险与大基团接触等机制差异。
Are We Making Progress in Multimodal Domain Generalization? A Comprehensive Benchmark Study
Authors: Hao Dong, Hongzhao Li, Shupan Li, Muhammad Haris Khan, Eleni Chatzi, Olga Fink
First: 2026-05-07T17:51:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:51:16+00:00
Comments: Code: https://github.com/lihongzhao99/MMDG_Benchmark
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Despite the growing popularity of Multimodal Domain Generalization (MMDG) for enhancing model robustness, it remains unclear whether reported performance gains reflect genuine algorithmic progress or are artifacts of inconsistent evaluation protocols. Current research is fragmented, with studies varying significantly across datasets, modality configurations, and experimental settings. Furthermore, existing benchmarks focus predominantly on action recognition, often neglecting critical real-world challenges such as input corruptions, missing modalities, and model trustworthiness. This lack of standardization obscures a reliable assessment of the field's advancement. To address this issue, we introduce MMDG-Bench, the first unified and comprehensive benchmark for MMDG, which standardizes evaluation across six datasets spanning three diverse tasks: action recognition, mechanical fault diagnosis, and sentiment analysis. MMDG-Bench encompasses six modality combinations, nine representative methods, and multiple evaluation settings. Beyond standard accuracy, it systematically assesses corruption robustness, missing-modality generalization, misclassification detection, and out-of-distribution detection. With 7, 402 neural networks trained in total across 95 unique cross-domain tasks, MMDG-Bench yields five key findings: (1) under fair comparisons, recent specialized MMDG methods offer only marginal improvements over ERM baseline; (2) no single method consistently outperforms others across datasets or modality combinations; (3) a substantial gap to upper-bound performance persists, indicating that MMDG remains far from solved; (4) trimodal fusion does not consistently outperform the strongest bimodal configurations; and (5) all evaluated methods exhibit significant degradation under corruption and missing-modality scenarios, with some methods further compromising model trustworthiness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：我们在多模态领域泛化方面是否取得了进展？一项全面的基准研究
尽管多模态领域泛化（MMDG）因其增强模型鲁棒性而日益流行，但尚不清楚所报告的性能提升是真实算法进步还是评估协议不一致导致的伪象。当前研究碎片化，不同研究在数据集、模态配置和实验设置上差异显著。此外，现有基准主要聚焦于动作识别，常忽视现实世界中的关键挑战，如输入损坏、模态缺失和模型可信度。缺乏标准化阻碍了对该领域进展的可靠评估。为解决此问题，我们引入MMDG-Bench，这是首个统一且全面的MMDG基准，覆盖三个不同任务（动作识别、机械故障诊断、情感分析）的六个数据集，标准化评估六种模态组合、九种代表性方法及多种实验设置。除标准准确率外，它系统评估抗损坏能力、模态缺失泛化、误分类检测和分布外检测。在95个跨域任务中训练总计7,402个神经网络，MMDG-Bench得出五项关键发现：（1）在公平比较下，近期专用MMDG方法仅对ERM基线有微小改进；（2）无单一方法在所有数据集或模态组合中持续领先；（3）与上限性能仍存在显著差距，表明MMDG远未解决；（4）三模态融合并不总优于最强双模态配置；（5）所有评估方法在损坏和模态缺失场景下均显著退化，部分方法进一步损害模型可信度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of standardized evaluation in multimodal domain generalization, this study introduces MMDG-Bench, the first comprehensive benchmark that standardizes testing across six datasets, three tasks, and six modality combinations. It evaluates nine representative methods under multiple settings including corruption robustness, missing modality generalization, and trustworthiness metrics. Across 95 cross-domain tasks and 7,402 trained models, results reveal that recent specialized methods offer only marginal gains over ERM baselines, no single method dominates across all settings, significant performance gaps remain from theoretical upper bounds, trimodal fusion does not consistently outperform bimodal setups, and all methods suffer substantial degradation under real-world challenges like corruptions or missing modalities, often reducing model trustworthiness.
为解决多模态领域泛化研究缺乏统一评估标准的问题，本研究提出MMDG-Bench，首次覆盖六类数据集、三类任务、六种模态组合及九种方法的综合基准。除准确率外，还系统评估了抗干扰能力、缺失模态泛化性、误分类检测及分布外检测。实验在95个跨域任务、7402个模型上进行，结果表明：近期专用方法仅小幅优于ERM基线；无方法在所有数据集或模态组合中持续领先；与理论上限仍有显著差距；三模态融合未稳定优于双模态；所有方法在干扰或缺失模态下均显著退化，部分方法甚至降低模型可信度。
StraTA: Incentivizing Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Strategic Trajectory Abstraction
Authors: Xiangyuan Xue, Yifan Zhou, Zidong Wang, Shengji Tang, Philip Torr, Wanli Ouyang, Lei Bai, Zhenfei Yin
First: 2026-05-07T17:51:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:51:16+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 4 figures, 7 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as interactive agents, but optimizing them for long-horizon decision making remains difficult because current methods are largely purely reactive, which weakens both exploration and credit assignment over extended trajectories. In this work, we present Strategic Trajectory Abstraction (StraTA), a simple framework that introduces an explicit trajectory-level strategy into agentic reinforcement learning (RL). StraTA samples a compact strategy from the initial task state, conditions subsequent actions on that strategy, and trains strategy generation and action execution jointly with a hierarchical GRPO-style rollout design, further enhanced by diverse strategy rollout and critical self-judgment. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and SciWorld show that StraTA consistently improves both sample efficiency and final performance over strong baselines. StraTA reaches success rates of 93.1% on ALFWorld and 84.2% on WebShop. On SciWorld, StraTA attains a 63.5% overall score, outperforming frontier closed-source models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：StraTA：通过战略轨迹抽象激励代理强化学习
大型语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地用作交互式代理，但优化其进行长时序决策仍具挑战，因为当前方法大多纯反应式，削弱了在长轨迹中的探索与信用分配。本文提出战略轨迹抽象（StraTA），一个简单的框架，将显式的轨迹级策略引入代理强化学习（RL）。StraTA从初始任务状态采样紧凑策略，根据该策略条件化后续动作，并采用分层GRPO风格的展开设计联合训练策略生成与动作执行，进一步结合多样策略展开与关键自我判断。在ALFWorld、WebShop和SciWorld上的实验表明，StraTA在样本效率和最终性能上均优于强基线方法。在ALFWorld上，StraTA成功率达93.1%；在WebShop上达84.2%；在SciWorld上总分63.5%，超越前沿闭源模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of purely reactive LLM agents in long-horizon decision making, StraTA introduces a trajectory-level strategy abstraction to enhance exploration and credit assignment. It samples a compact strategy from the initial state, conditions actions on that strategy, and jointly trains strategy generation and action execution using a hierarchical GRPO-style rollout, augmented by diverse rollouts and self-judgment. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and SciWorld demonstrate consistent gains: 93.1% success on ALFWorld, 84.2% on WebShop, and a 63.5% overall score on SciWorld, outperforming frontier closed-source models.
为解决大语言模型在长时序决策中因纯反应式策略导致的探索不足与归因困难，StraTA提出在轨迹层面引入显式策略，从初始状态采样紧凑策略并据此引导后续动作，联合训练策略生成与动作执行，采用分层GRPO式 rollout 并结合多样策略 rollout 与自我评判。在 ALFWorld、WebShop 和 SciWorld 上实验表明，StraTA 在样本效率与最终性能上均优于强基线，分别达成 93.1% 的 ALFWorld 成功率、84.2% 的 WebShop 成功率，以及 63.5% 的 SciWorld 总分，超越前沿闭源模型。
Concept-Based Abductive and Contrastive Explanations for Behaviors of Vision Models
Authors: Ronaldo Canizales, Divya Gopinath, Corina Păsăreanu, Ravi Mangal
First: 2026-05-07T17:51:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:51:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
*Concept-based explanations* offer a promising approach for explaining the predictions of deep neural networks in terms of high-level, human-understandable concepts. However, existing methods either do not establish a causal connection between the concepts and model predictions or are limited in expressivity and only able to infer causal explanations involving single concepts. At the same time, the parallel line of work on *formal abductive and contrastive explanations* computes the minimal set of input features causally relevant for model outcomes but only considers low-level features such as pixels. Merging these two threads, in this work, we propose the notion of *concept-based abductive and contrastive explanations* that capture the minimal sets of high-level concepts causally relevant for model outcomes. We then present a family of algorithms that enumerate all minimal explanations while using *concept erasure* procedures to establish causal relationships. By appropriately aggregating such explanations, we are not only able to understand model predictions on individual images but also on collections of images where the model exhibits a user-specified, common *behavior*. We evaluate our approach on multiple models, datasets, and behaviors, and demonstrate its effectiveness in computing helpful, user-friendly explanations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于概念的视觉模型行为的溯因与对比解释
基于概念的解释为用高层次、人类可理解的概念解释深度神经网络的预测提供了有前景的方法。然而，现有方法要么未能建立概念与模型预测之间的因果联系，要么表达能力有限，仅能推断涉及单一概念的因果解释。与此同时，关于形式化溯因与对比解释的研究计算与模型结果因果相关的最小输入特征集，但仅考虑像素等低级特征。本工作融合这两条研究路线，提出基于概念的溯因与对比解释，捕捉与模型结果因果相关的最小高层概念集。我们随后提出一系列算法，通过概念擦除程序枚举所有最小解释，并通过适当聚合这些解释，不仅能够理解模型对单张图像的预测，还能理解模型在用户指定的共同行为下对图像集合的预测。我们在多个模型、数据集和行为上评估了该方法，证明其在计算有益且用户友好的解释方面有效。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for human-interpretable, causally grounded explanations of vision models, this work merges concept-based explanations with formal abductive and contrastive reasoning to identify minimal sets of high-level concepts causally responsible for model predictions. It introduces algorithms that enumerate such explanations using concept erasure to establish causal relevance, enabling both individual image interpretation and collective behavior analysis across image collections. Experiments across multiple models and datasets show the method successfully generates concise, user-friendly explanations that align with human intuition and capture model behaviors specified by users.
为提升视觉模型预测的可解释性，本文提出基于概念的溯因与对比解释方法，旨在识别最小的高阶概念集合，以因果方式解释模型输出。该方法结合概念擦除与算法枚举，建立因果关联，并支持对单张图像及具有特定行为的图像集合进行解释。在多个模型与数据集上的实验表明，该方法生成的解释简洁、语义清晰，且在行为一致性与因果准确性上优于现有方法。
GlazyBench: A Benchmark for Ceramic Glaze Property Prediction and Image Generation
Authors: Ziyu Zhai, Siyou Li, Juexi Shao, Juntao Yu
First: 2026-05-07T17:51:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:51:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Developing ceramic glazes is a costly, time-consuming process of trial and error due to complex chemistry, placing a significant burden on independent artists. While recent advances in multimodal AI offer a modern solution, the field lacks the large-scale datasets required to train these models. We propose GlazyBench, the first dataset for AI-assisted glaze design. Comprising 23,148 real glaze formulations, GlazyBench supports two primary tasks: predicting post-firing surface properties, such as color and transparency, from raw materials, and generating accurate visual representations of the glaze based on these properties. We establish comprehensive baselines for property prediction using traditional machine learning and large language models, alongside image generation benchmarks using deep generative and large multimodal models. Our experiments demonstrate promising yet challenging results. GlazyBench pioneers a new research direction in AI-assisted material design, providing a standardized benchmark for systematic evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GlazyBench：陶瓷釉料性质预测与图像生成的基准数据集
由于复杂的化学特性，陶瓷釉料开发是一个成本高昂、耗时且依赖试错的过程，给独立艺术家带来沉重负担。尽管多模态AI的最新进展提供了现代解决方案，但该领域缺乏大规模数据集以训练这些模型。我们提出GlazyBench，这是首个用于AI辅助釉料设计的数据集，包含23,148种真实釉料配方，支持两大核心任务：从原材料预测烧制后表面性质（如颜色和透明度），以及基于这些性质生成准确的釉料视觉表现。我们为性质预测任务建立了传统机器学习与大语言模型的全面基线，同时为图像生成任务建立了深度生成模型与大模型的基准。实验结果展现出前景与挑战并存。GlazyBench开创了AI辅助材料设计的新研究方向，为系统性评估提供了标准化基准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost and inefficiency of ceramic glaze development through trial-and-error, this work introduces GlazyBench, the first large-scale dataset of 23,148 real glaze formulations, to enable AI-assisted design. The dataset supports two tasks: predicting post-firing properties like color and transparency from raw materials, and generating realistic glaze images based on those properties. Experiments evaluate traditional machine learning, large language models, and deep generative models, revealing promising but challenging performance across both tasks, establishing GlazyBench as a foundational benchmark for AI-driven material design.
鉴于陶瓷釉料开发成本高、效率低，本研究提出GlazyBench，首个包含23,148种真实釉料配方的大规模数据集，旨在支持AI辅助设计。该数据集支持两项任务：根据原料成分预测烧制后属性（如颜色和透明度），以及基于这些属性生成真实釉面图像。实验评估了传统机器学习、大语言模型和深度生成模型在两项任务上的表现，结果表明性能尚可但挑战显著，从而确立了AI驱动材料设计的新基准。
Predictive and Prescriptive AI toward Optimizing Wildfire Suppression
Authors: Leonard Boussioux, Alexandre Jacquillat, Ryne Reger, Jacob Wachspress
First: 2026-05-06T05:26:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:49:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Intense wildfire seasons require critical prioritization decisions to allocate scarce suppression resources over a dispersed geographical area. This paper develops a predictive and prescriptive approach to jointly optimize crew assignments and wildfire suppression. The problem features a discrete resource-allocation structure with endogenous wildfire demand and non-linear wildfire dynamics. We formulate an integer optimization model with crew assignments on a time-space-rest network, wildfire dynamics on a time-state network, and linking constraints between them. We develop a two-sided branch-and-price-and-cut algorithm based on: (i) a two-sided column generation scheme that generates fire suppression plans and crew routes iteratively; (ii) a new family of cuts exploiting the knapsack structure of the linking constraints; and (iii) novel branching rules to accommodate non-linear wildfire dynamics. We also propose a data-driven double machine learning approach to estimate wildfire spread as a function of covariate information and suppression efforts, mitigating observed confounding between historical crew assignments and wildfire growth. Extensive computational experiments show that the optimization algorithm scales to otherwise intractable real-world instances; and that the methodology can enhance suppression effectiveness in practice, resulting in significant reductions in area burned over a wildfire season and guiding resource sharing across wildfire jurisdictions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向优化野火扑救的预测与处方型人工智能
高强度野火季节需要对分散地理区域内的稀缺扑救资源进行关键优先级决策。本文提出一种预测与处方相结合的方法，联合优化人员分配与野火扑救。该问题具有离散资源分配结构，野火需求内生且野火动态非线性。我们构建了一个整数优化模型，将人员分配建模于时空休息网络，野火动态建模于时态网络，并通过链接约束连接二者。我们开发了一种基于双侧列生成与切割的分支定价算法，包括：(i) 双侧列生成方案，迭代生成扑救计划与人员路线；(ii) 利用链接约束背包结构的新切割族；(iii) 适应非线性野火动态的新型分支规则。同时，我们提出一种数据驱动的双机器学习方法，用于估计野火蔓延与协变量信息及扑救努力之间的函数关系，缓解历史人员分配与野火增长间的观测混杂。大量计算实验表明，该优化算法可扩展至原本不可解的实际案例；且该方法可提升实际扑救效果，显著减少野火季节烧毁面积，并指导跨野火管辖区域的资源共享。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to efficiently allocate limited suppression resources during intense wildfire seasons, this study proposes a predictive and prescriptive AI framework that jointly optimizes crew assignments and suppression actions. The method formulates the problem as an integer optimization model integrating time-space-rest networks for crew routing and time-state networks for wildfire dynamics, linked by novel cutting planes and branching rules to handle non-linear fire behavior. A data-driven double machine learning approach estimates wildfire spread while correcting for historical confounding effects. Experimental results demonstrate the algorithm’s scalability to real-world instances and its practical impact, reducing total burned area significantly and enabling cross-jurisdictional resource coordination.
为应对野火季节中稀缺资源的高效分配问题，本研究提出了一种预测与决策相结合的AI框架，联合优化人员调度与灭火行动。方法构建了基于时空网络与状态网络的整数规划模型，并通过双侧分支-定价-切割算法处理非线性火势变化，该算法包含迭代生成灭火方案与人员路线、利用背包结构切割约束及创新分支规则。实验表明，该算法可扩展至实际大规模问题，显著降低火场烧毁面积，并支持跨行政区资源协同调度。
Recursive Agent Optimization
Authors: Apurva Gandhi, Satyaki Chakraborty, Xiangjun Wang, Aviral Kumar, Graham Neubig
First: 2026-05-07T17:49:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:49:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Recursive Agent Optimization (RAO), a reinforcement learning approach for training recursive agents: agents that can spawn and delegate sub-tasks to new instantiations of themselves recursively. Recursive agents implement an inference-time scaling algorithm that naturally allows agents to scale to longer contexts and generalize to more difficult problems via divide-and-conquer. RAO provides a method to train models to best take advantage of such recursive inference, teaching agents when and how to delegate and communicate. We find that recursive agents trained in this way enjoy better training efficiency, can scale to tasks that go beyond the model's context window, generalize to tasks much harder than the ones the agent was trained on, and can enjoy reduced wall-clock time compared to single-agent systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：递归代理优化
我们提出了递归代理优化（RAO），一种用于训练递归代理的强化学习方法：这些代理能够递归地生成并委派子任务给自身的新实例。递归代理实现了一种推理时扩展算法，自然支持代理扩展至更长上下文并通过对复杂问题进行分而治之以实现泛化。RAO提供了一种训练模型的方法，使其能最好地利用此类递归推理，教导代理何时以及如何委派和通信。我们发现，通过这种方式训练的递归代理具有更好的训练效率，能够扩展到超出模型上下文窗口的任务，泛化到比训练任务更难的问题，并且相比单代理系统可减少实际运行时间。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to handle longer contexts and harder tasks beyond a model's native capacity, Recursive Agent Optimization (RAO) trains agents capable of recursively spawning and delegating sub-tasks to new instances of themselves. The method teaches agents to strategically delegate and communicate during inference, enabling a divide-and-conquer approach that scales naturally with context length. Experiments show these agents achieve better training efficiency, successfully tackle tasks exceeding their context window, generalize to significantly harder problems than their training set, and reduce wall-clock execution time compared to single-agent systems.
为应对模型原生上下文长度和训练任务能力的限制，Recursive Agent Optimization（RAO）训练代理在推理时递归生成并委派子任务给自身新实例，实现分而治之。该方法教导代理何时及如何委派与通信，以优化递归执行效率。实验表明，RAO训练的代理具有更高的训练效率，可处理超出其上下文窗口的任务，能泛化至训练任务远更困难的问题，并相比单代理系统显著减少实际运行时间。
Can RL Teach Long-Horizon Reasoning to LLMs? Expressiveness Is Key
Authors: Tianle Wang, Zhaoyang Wang, Guangchen Lan, Xinpeng Wei, Sipeng Zhang, Guanwen Qiu, Abulhair Saparov
First: 2026-05-07T17:48:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:48:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) has been applied to improve large language model (LLM) reasoning, yet the systematic study of how training scales with task difficulty has been hampered by the lack of controlled, scalable environments. We introduce ScaleLogic, a synthetic logical reasoning framework that offers independent control over two axes of difficulty: the depth of the required proof planning (i.e., the horizon) and the expressiveness of the underlying logic. Our proposed framework supports a wide range of logics: from simple implication-only logic ("if-then") towards more expressive first-order reasoning with conjunction ("and"), disjunction ("or"), negation ("not"), and universal quantification ("for all"). Using this framework, we show that the RL training compute $T$ follows a power law with respect to reasoning depth $D$ ($T \propto D^γ$, $R^{2} > 0.99$), and that the scaling exponent $γ$ increases monotonically with logical expressiveness, from $1.04$ to $2.60$. On downstream mathematics and general reasoning benchmarks, more expressive training settings yield both larger performance gains (up to $+10.66$ points) and more compute-efficient transfer compared to less expressive settings, demonstrating that what a model is trained on, not just how much it is trained, shapes downstream transfer. We further show that the power-law relationship holds across multiple RL methods, and curriculum-based training substantially improves scaling efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：强化学习能否教会大语言模型长程推理？表达能力是关键
强化学习（RL）已被用于提升大语言模型（LLM）的推理能力，但其训练规模随任务难度变化的系统性研究受限于缺乏可控且可扩展的环境。我们提出了ScaleLogic，一个合成逻辑推理框架，可独立控制两个难度维度：所需证明规划的深度（即时间跨度）和底层逻辑的表达能力。该框架支持多种逻辑：从仅含蕴含关系（“如果-那么”）的简单逻辑，到包含合取（“且”）、析取（“或”）、否定（“非”）和全称量词（“对所有”）的更复杂一阶逻辑。利用该框架，我们发现RL训练计算量T与推理深度D呈幂律关系（T ∝ D^γ，R² > 0.99），且幂律指数γ随逻辑表达能力单调递增，从1.04增至2.60。在下游数学与通用推理基准测试中，更丰富的训练设置不仅带来更大性能提升（最高+10.66分），且相比低表达性设置更节省计算资源，表明模型训练内容本身（而非仅训练量）决定了下游迁移效果。我们进一步证明该幂律关系在多种RL方法中成立，且基于课程的训练显著提升缩放效率。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates whether reinforcement learning (RL) can effectively teach long-horizon reasoning to large language models (LLMs), addressing the lack of controlled environments for studying scaling with task difficulty. The authors introduce ScaleLogic, a synthetic framework that independently controls reasoning depth (horizon) and logical expressiveness, supporting logics from simple implication to full first-order logic. Experiments reveal that RL training compute scales as a power law with reasoning depth (T ∝ D^γ, R² > 0.99), and the scaling exponent γ increases with expressiveness—from 1.04 to 2.60. More expressive training leads to greater performance gains (up to +10.66 points) and more compute-efficient transfer on downstream tasks, indicating that training content matters as much as training volume. The power-law scaling holds across multiple RL methods, and curriculum-based training further improves efficiency.
该研究探讨强化学习（RL）是否能有效训练大语言模型（LLM）进行长程推理，解决了缺乏可控环境来研究任务难度与训练规模关系的问题。作者提出ScaleLogic框架，可独立控制推理深度（时域）和逻辑表达能力，支持从简单蕴含到一阶逻辑的多种逻辑形式。实验表明，RL训练计算量随推理深度呈幂律增长（T ∝ D^γ，R² > 0.99），且幂律指数γ随表达能力提升而单调增加（1.04至2.60）。高表达性训练带来更大性能提升（最高+10.66分）和更高效的下游迁移，证明训练内容与训练量同等重要。该幂律关系在多种RL方法中成立，且基于课程的训练显著提升效率。
DPM++: Dynamic Masked Metric Learning for Occluded Person Re-identification
Authors: Lei Tan, Yingshi Luan, Pincong Zou, Pingyang Dai, Liujuan Cao
First: 2026-05-07T17:47:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:47:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Although person re-identification has made impressive progress, occlusion caused by obstacles remains an unsettled issue in real applications. The difficulty lies in the mismatch between incomplete occluded samples and holistic identity representations. Severe occlusion removes discriminative body cues and introduces interference from background clutter and occluders, making global metric learning unreliable. Existing methods mainly rely on extra pre-trained models to estimate visible parts for alignment or construct occluded samples via data augmentation, but still lack a unified framework that learns robust visibility-consistent matching under realistic occlusion patterns. In this paper, we propose DPM++, a Dynamic Masked Metric Learning framework for occluded person re-identification. DPM++ learns an input-adaptive masked metric that dynamically selects reliable identity subspaces for each occluded instance, enabling matching to emphasize visibility-consistent evidence while suppressing unreliable components. Built upon the classifier-prototype space, DPM++ introduces a CLIP-based two-stage supervision scheme, where ID-level semantic priors are learned from the text branch and transferred into the classifier-prototype space for dynamic masked matching. To strengthen the masked metric, we introduce a saliency-guided patch transfer strategy to synthesize controllable and photo-realistic occluded samples during training. Exploiting real scene priors, this strategy exposes the model to realistic partial observations and provides richer supervision than random erasing. In addition, occlusion-aware sample pairing and mask-guided optimization improve the stability and effectiveness of the framework. Experiments on occluded and holistic person re-identification benchmarks show that DPM++ consistently outperforms previous state-of-the-art methods in both holistic and occlusion scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DPM++：面向遮挡行人重识别的动态掩码度量学习
尽管行人重识别已取得显著进展，但由障碍物引起的遮挡问题在实际应用中仍待解决。难点在于不完整遮挡样本与整体身份表征之间的不匹配。严重遮挡会移除判别性身体特征，并引入背景杂乱和遮挡物干扰，使全局度量学习不可靠。现有方法主要依赖额外预训练模型估计可见部分进行对齐，或通过数据增强构建遮挡样本，但仍缺乏一个统一框架，能在真实遮挡模式下学习鲁棒的可见性一致匹配。本文提出DPM++，一种面向遮挡行人重识别的动态掩码度量学习框架。DPM++学习一种输入自适应掩码度量，动态为每个遮挡实例选择可靠的身份子空间，使匹配能强调可见性一致的证据并抑制不可靠成分。基于分类器-原型空间，DPM++引入基于CLIP的两阶段监督方案，从文本分支学习ID级语义先验，并将其迁移到分类器-原型空间以实现动态掩码匹配。为强化掩码度量，我们引入基于显著性引导的块转移策略，在训练中合成可控且逼真的遮挡样本。该策略利用真实场景先验，使模型接触真实部分观测，提供比随机擦除更丰富的监督。此外，遮挡感知样本配对与掩码引导优化提升了框架的稳定性和有效性。在遮挡与整体行人重识别基准测试中，DPM++在整体和遮挡场景下均持续优于先前最先进方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of occlusion in person re-identification, where incomplete body cues and background interference degrade global metric learning, DPM++ introduces a dynamic masked metric framework that adaptively selects reliable identity subspaces for each occluded instance. It employs a CLIP-based two-stage supervision scheme to transfer ID-level semantic priors into a classifier-prototype space, enabling visibility-consistent matching. Enhanced by a saliency-guided patch transfer strategy for realistic occluded sample synthesis and occlusion-aware sample pairing, DPM++ achieves robust performance across both holistic and occluded benchmarks, consistently outperforming prior state-of-the-art methods.
为解决遮挡导致的人体重识别中全局特征匹配失效的问题，DPM++提出一种动态掩码度量学习框架，针对每个遮挡实例自适应选择可靠的身份子空间，强调可见区域特征并抑制干扰成分。该方法结合CLIP双阶段监督机制，将ID语义先验从文本分支迁移到分类器原型空间，并引入基于显著性引导的块级遮挡样本合成策略，增强模型对真实遮挡场景的适应性。实验表明，DPM++在遮挡与完整场景基准上均显著优于现有方法，验证其在真实遮挡条件下的鲁棒性。
Cited but Not Verified: Parsing and Evaluating Source Attribution in LLM Deep Research Agents
Authors: Hailey Onweller, Elias Lumer, Austin Huber, Pia Ramchandani, Vamse Kumar Subbiah, Corey Feld
First: 2026-05-07T17:46:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:46:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) power deep research agents that synthesize information from hundreds of web sources into cited reports, yet these citations cannot be reliably verified. Current approaches either trust models to self-cite accurately, risking bias, or employ retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) that does not validate source accessibility, relevance, or factual consistency. We introduce the first source attribution evaluation framework that uses a reproducible AST parser to extract and evaluate inline citations from LLM-generated Markdown reports at scale. Unlike methods that verify claims in isolation, our framework closes the loop by retrieving the actual cited content, enabling human or model evaluators to judge each citation against its source. Citations are evaluated along three dimensions. (1) Link Works verifies URL accessibility, (2) Relevant Content measures topical alignment, and (3) Fact Check validates factual accuracy against source content. We benchmark 14 closed-source and open-source LLMs across three evaluation dimensions using rubric-based LLM-as-a-judge evaluators calibrated through human review. Our results reveal that even the strongest frontier models maintain link validity above 94% and relevance above 80%, yet achieve only 39-77% factual accuracy, while fewer than half of open-source models successfully generate cited reports in a one-shot setting. Ablation studies on research depth show that Fact Check accuracy drops by approximately 42% on average across two frontier models as tool calls scale from 2 to 150, demonstrating that more retrieval does not produce more accurate citations. These findings reveal a critical disconnect between surface-level citation quality and factual reliability, and our framework provides the evaluation infrastructure to assess the disconnect.
中文标题/摘要
标题：被引用但未验证：解析与评估大型语言模型深度研究代理中的源引用
大型语言模型（LLMs）驱动的深度研究代理能够从数百个网络来源中综合信息并生成带引用的报告，但这些引用无法可靠验证。当前方法要么依赖模型自我准确引用，存在偏倚风险，要么采用检索增强生成（RAG），但不验证源内容的可访问性、相关性或事实一致性。我们提出了首个源引用评估框架，利用可复现的AST解析器大规模提取并评估LLM生成的Markdown报告中的行内引用。与仅孤立验证声明的方法不同，我们的框架通过检索实际引用内容，使人工或模型评估者能针对每个引用与其源内容进行判断。引用评估涵盖三个维度：（1）链接有效性验证URL可访问性，（2）相关内容性衡量主题一致性，（3）事实核查验证与源内容的事实准确性。我们使用基于评分标准的LLM作为裁判评估器（经人工审核校准），对14个闭源和开源LLM在三个评估维度上进行基准测试。结果表明，即使最强的前沿模型也保持链接有效性超过94%、相关性超过80%，但事实准确性仅为39%-77%；少于一半的开源模型能在单次设置中成功生成带引用报告。对研究深度的消融研究表明，当工具调用从2次扩展至150次时，两个前沿模型的事实核查准确性平均下降约42%，表明更多检索并不意味着更准确的引用。这些发现揭示了表面引用质量与事实可靠性之间的关键脱节，而我们的框架提供了评估该脱节的基础设施。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the unreliability of citations generated by LLM-powered research agents, this study introduces the first scalable framework to evaluate source attribution by parsing and validating inline citations in LLM-generated Markdown reports. The method uses a reproducible AST parser to extract citations, then retrieves actual source content to evaluate each against three dimensions: link accessibility, content relevance, and factual accuracy. Experiments across 14 LLMs show that even top models maintain high link validity (94%) and relevance (80%), but factual accuracy ranges from 39% to 77%. Fewer than half of open-source models can produce cited reports in one shot, and factual accuracy drops by 42% on average as tool calls increase from 2 to 150, indicating that more retrieval does not improve factual reliability.
为解决大语言模型驱动的研究代理生成的引用不可靠问题，本研究提出首个可规模化评估引用来源的框架，通过AST解析器提取Markdown报告中的引用，并检索真实源内容进行三维度评估：链接有效性、内容相关性与事实准确性。实验表明，前沿模型虽保持94%以上链接有效性与80%以上相关性，但事实准确率仅39%至77%；少于半数开源模型能单次生成带引用报告；当工具调用从2次增至150次时，事实准确率平均下降42%，说明检索量增加并不提升引用准确性，揭示表面引用质量与事实可靠性之间存在关键断层。
On the optimization dynamics of RLVR: Gradient gap and step size thresholds
Authors: Joe Suk, Yaqi Duan
First: 2025-10-09T17:53:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:44:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), which uses simple binary feedback to post-train large language models, has found significant empirical success. However, a principled understanding of why it works is lacking. This paper builds a theoretical foundation for RLVR by analyzing its training process at both the full-response (trajectory) and token levels. Central to our analysis is a new quantity called the Gradient Gap, which formalizes the direction of improvement from low-reward to high-reward regions of the response space. We prove that convergence critically depends on aligning the update direction with this Gradient Gap. Moreover, we derive a sharp step-size threshold based on the magnitude of the Gradient Gap: below it, learning converges, whereas above it, performance collapses. Our theory further predicts how the critical step size must scale with response length and the success rate, thereby explaining why practical heuristics such as length normalization improve stability and showing that, with a fixed learning rate, the success rate can stagnate strictly below $100\%$. Importantly, our theory holds flexibly for any policy-gradient algorithm and so characterizes the dynamics of popular approaches such as REINFORCE and GRPO. We validate these predictions through controlled bandit simulations and language model experiments on post-training Qwen2.5-Math-7B with GRPO.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RLVR优化动力学：梯度间隙与步长阈值
可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）通过简单的二元反馈对大语言模型进行后训练，取得了显著的实证成功。然而，其工作原理缺乏理论基础。本文通过分析RLVR在完整响应（轨迹）和token层面的训练过程，为其构建理论基础。核心分析引入了新的量——梯度间隙，用于形式化响应空间中从低奖励到高奖励区域的改进方向。我们证明，收敛性关键在于更新方向与梯度间隙的对齐。此外，我们推导出一个基于梯度间隙幅度的精确步长阈值：低于该阈值时学习收敛，高于时性能崩溃。理论进一步预测了临界步长需如何随响应长度和成功率缩放，从而解释了长度归一化等实用启发式方法为何能提升稳定性，并表明在固定学习率下，成功率会严格低于100%。重要的是，该理论适用于任何策略梯度算法，因此可刻画REINFORCE和GRPO等流行方法的动力学。我们通过受控的多臂老虎机模拟和在Qwen2.5-Math-7B模型上使用GRPO的后训练语言模型实验验证了这些预测。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the lack of theoretical understanding behind Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), which uses binary feedback to post-train large language models. It introduces the concept of Gradient Gap—a measure formalizing the direction of improvement from low- to high-reward regions—and demonstrates that convergence hinges on aligning policy updates with this gap. The authors derive a sharp step-size threshold: learning converges below it, but collapses above it, and show this threshold scales with response length and success rate, explaining why length normalization stabilizes training and why fixed learning rates prevent success rates from reaching 100%. The theory applies broadly to policy-gradient methods like REINFORCE and GRPO, validated through bandit simulations and experiments on Qwen2.5-Math-7B with GRPO.
鉴于RLVR在实践中表现优异但缺乏理论解释，本文提出“梯度间隙”这一新指标，用于形式化响应空间中从低奖励到高奖励区域的改进方向，并在轨迹和token层面分析其训练动态。作者证明收敛依赖于策略更新方向与梯度间隙的对齐，并推导出一个精确的步长阈值：低于该阈值学习收敛，高于则性能崩溃。理论进一步表明，临界步长需随响应长度和成功率缩放，解释了为何长度归一化能提升稳定性，以及固定学习率下成功率无法达到100%。该框架适用于任意策略梯度算法（如REINFORCE和GRPO），并通过带模拟和Qwen2.5-Math-7B上的GRPO实验验证了预测。
Crafting Reversible SFT Behaviors in Large Language Models
Authors: Yuping Lin, Pengfei He, Yue Xing, Yingqian Cui, Jiayuan Ding, Subhabrata Mukherjee, Hui Liu, Zhen Xiang
First: 2026-05-07T17:44:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:44:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) induces new behaviors in large language models, yet imposes no structural constraint on how these behaviors are distributed within the model. Existing behavior interpretation methods, such as circuit attribution approaches, identify sparse subnetworks correlated with SFT-induced behaviors post-hoc. However, such correlations do not imply *causal necessity*, limiting the ability to selectively control SFT-induced behaviors at inference time. We pursue an alternative by asking: can an SFT-induced behavior be deliberately compressed into a sparse, mechanistically necessary subnetwork, termed a *carrier*, while remaining controllable at inference time without weight modification? We propose (a) **Loss-Constrained Dual Descent (LCDD)**, which constructs such carriers by jointly optimizing routing masks and model weights under an explicit utility budget, and (b) **SFT-Eraser**, a soft prompt optimized via activation matching on extracted carrier channels, to reverse the SFT-induced behavior. Across safety, fixed-response, and style behaviors on multiple model families, LCDD yields sparse carriers that preserve target behaviors while enabling strong reversion when triggered by SFT-Eraser. Ablations further establish that the sparse structure is the key precondition for reversal: the same trigger optimization fails on standard SFT models, confirming that structure rather than trigger design is the operative factor. These results provide direct evidence that the learned carriers are causally necessary for the behaviors, pointing to a new direction for systematically localizing and selectively suppressing SFT-induced behaviors in deployed models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在大语言模型中构建可逆的监督微调行为
监督微调（SFT）会在大语言模型中诱导新行为，但不对其行为在模型内部的分布施加结构约束。现有行为解释方法（如电路归因方法）在事后识别与SFT诱导行为相关的稀疏子网络，但这些相关性不意味着*因果必要性*，限制了在推理时选择性控制SFT诱导行为的能力。我们提出另一种方法：能否将SFT诱导的行为刻意压缩为一个稀疏、机制上必要的子网络（称为*载体*），并在推理时无需修改权重即可控制？我们提出（a）**损失约束双下降（LCDD）**，通过在显式效用预算下联合优化路由掩码和模型权重，构建此类载体；以及（b）**SFT擦除器**，一种通过提取载体通道上的激活匹配优化的软提示，用于逆转SFT诱导行为。在多个模型家族的安全、固定响应和风格行为上，LCDD生成的稀疏载体在保留目标行为的同时，能通过SFT擦除器触发实现强逆转。消融实验进一步证实，稀疏结构是逆转的关键前提：相同的触发优化在标准SFT模型上失败，表明结构而非触发设计才是关键因素。这些结果直接证明所学载体对行为具有因果必要性，为系统性定位并选择性抑制部署模型中SFT诱导行为指明了新方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of structural control over SFT-induced behaviors in large language models, this work seeks to compress such behaviors into sparse, causally necessary subnetworks called 'carriers' that can be selectively reversed at inference time without modifying weights. It introduces Loss-Constrained Dual Descent (LCDD), a method that jointly optimizes routing masks and model weights under a utility budget to construct these carriers, and SFT-Eraser, a soft prompt optimized via activation matching on carrier channels to reverse the behavior. Experiments across safety, fixed-response, and style behaviors show that LCDD generates sparse carriers that preserve target behaviors while enabling strong reversal when triggered by SFT-Eraser; ablation studies confirm that the sparse structure is essential for reversal, not the trigger design, demonstrating causal necessity of the carriers.
为解决监督微调（SFT）行为缺乏结构化控制的问题，该研究提出将SFT诱导的行为压缩进稀疏、机制上必要的子网络（称为‘载体’），从而实现可逆控制。方法包括：Loss-Constrained Dual Descent（LCDD）通过联合优化路由掩码与权重，在效用预算下构建载体；以及SFT-Eraser，一种通过激活匹配优化的软提示，用于逆转行为。实验表明，LCDD生成的稀疏载体能保留目标行为并支持通过SFT-Eraser有效逆转；消融实验进一步证实，稀疏结构而非触发器设计是逆转的关键，表明载体对SFT行为具有因果必要性。
Hybrid Quantum-Classical GANs for the Generation of Adversarial Network Flows
Authors: Prateek Paudel, Nitin Jha, Abhishek Parakh, Mahadevan Subramaniam
First: 2026-05-07T17:43:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:43:25+00:00
Comments: 14 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Classical generative adversarial networks (GANs) have been applied to generate adversarial network traffic capable of attacking intrusion detection systems, but they suffer from shortcomings such as the need for large amounts of high-dimensional datasets, mode collapse, and high computational overhead. In this work, we propose a hybrid quantum-classical GAN (QC-GAN) framework where a variational quantum generator is used to generate synthetic network traffic flows mimicking malicious traffic using latent representations. Instead of sampling classical noise vectors, we encode the latent vector (the hidden features) as a quantum state, which is the basis for claiming more expressive latent representations and reducing computational overhead. A classical discriminator will be trained on real-world datasets (UNSW-NB15) and the proposed QC-GAN-generated fake network flows. In this configuration, the generator aims to minimize the discriminator's ability to distinguish real from fake traffic, while the discriminator aims to maximize its classification accuracy, in an iterative manner. In our attack model, we assume that the attacker is a state actor with access to limited quantum computing power, whereas the discriminator is chosen to be classical, as will likely be the case for most end users and organizations. We test the generated flows using classical intrusion detection system (IDS) models, such as a random forest classifier and a convolutional neural network-based classifier, for their ability to bypass the detection process. This work aims to highlight the possibilities of quantum machine learning as a means of generating advanced attack flows and stress testing classical IDS. Lastly, we further evaluate how hardware-based noise affects these attacks to offer a new perspective on IDS, highlighting the need for a quantum resilient defense system.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于生成对抗网络流量的混合量子-经典GAN
经典生成对抗网络（GAN）已用于生成可攻击入侵检测系统的对抗网络流量，但存在需大量高维数据集、模式崩溃和高计算开销等缺点。本文提出一种混合量子-经典GAN（QC-GAN）框架，其中变分量子生成器用于生成模拟恶意流量的合成网络流量，利用潜在表示。不同于采样经典噪声向量，我们将潜在向量（隐藏特征）编码为量子态，从而实现更丰富的潜在表示并降低计算开销。经典判别器将在真实数据集（UNSW-NB15）和所提出的QC-GAN生成的伪造网络流量上进行训练。在此配置中，生成器旨在最小化判别器区分真实与伪造流量的能力，而判别器则旨在最大化分类准确率，二者迭代进行。在攻击模型中，我们假设攻击者为具备有限量子计算能力的国家行为体，而判别器选择为经典模型，因大多数终端用户和组织很可能采用经典系统。我们使用经典入侵检测系统（IDS）模型（如随机森林分类器和基于卷积神经网络的分类器）测试生成流量以评估其绕过检测的能力。本工作旨在突出量子机器学习在生成高级攻击流量和压力测试经典IDS方面的潜力，并进一步评估硬件噪声对这些攻击的影响，为IDS提供新视角，强调构建量子抗性防御系统的重要性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of classical GANs in generating adversarial network traffic—such as high computational cost, mode collapse, and reliance on large datasets—this work introduces a hybrid quantum-classical GAN (QC-GAN) framework. The generator leverages a variational quantum circuit to encode latent representations as quantum states, enabling more expressive and computationally efficient generation of malicious traffic. A classical discriminator trained on real-world UNSW-NB15 data and QC-GAN-generated flows iteratively improves its ability to distinguish real from fake traffic, while the generator seeks to fool it. Experimental results show that QC-GAN-generated flows successfully evade detection by classical IDS models like random forest and CNN classifiers, demonstrating the potential of quantum machine learning for advanced adversarial attacks. The study also investigates hardware noise effects, suggesting the need for quantum-resilient defense mechanisms in future intrusion detection systems.
为克服传统GAN在生成对抗网络流量时存在的计算开销大、模式崩溃及依赖大规模高维数据等缺陷，本文提出一种混合量子-经典GAN（QC-GAN）框架。生成器采用变分量子电路，将潜在表示编码为量子态，以提升表达能力并降低计算负担。经典判别器在真实UNSW-NB15数据与QC-GAN生成的伪造流量上训练，通过迭代优化提升对真实与伪造流量的区分能力，而生成器则试图欺骗判别器。实验表明，QC-GAN生成的流量能有效绕过随机森林和CNN等经典入侵检测系统，凸显量子机器学习在构建高级对抗攻击中的潜力。研究还探讨了硬件噪声对攻击效果的影响，强调未来IDS需具备量子抗性防御能力。
LiVeAction: a Lightweight, Versatile, and Asymmetric Neural Codec Design for Real-time Operation
Authors: Dan Jacobellis, Neeraja J. Yadwadkar
First: 2026-05-07T17:42:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:42:38+00:00
Comments: DCC 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Modern sensors generate rich, high-fidelity data, yet applications operating on wearable or remote sensing devices remain constrained by bandwidth and power budgets. Standardized codecs such as JPEG and MPEG achieve efficient trade-offs between bitrate and perceptual quality but are designed for human perception, limiting their applicability to machine-perception tasks and non-traditional modalities such as spatial audio arrays, hyperspectral images, and 3D medical images. General-purpose compression schemes based on scalar quantization or resolution reduction are broadly applicable but fail to exploit inherent signal redundancies, resulting in suboptimal rate-distortion performance. Recent generative neural codecs, or tokenizers, model complex signal dependencies but are often over-parameterized, data-hungry, and modality-specific, making them impractical for resource-constrained environments. We introduce a Lightweight, Versatile, and Asymmetric neural codec architecture (LiVeAction), that addresses these limitations through two key ideas. (1) To reduce the complexity of the encoder to meet the resource constraints of the execution environments, we impose an FFT-like structure and reduce the overall size and depth of the neural-network-based analysis transform. (2) To allow arbitrary signal modalities and simplify training, we replace adversarial and perceptual losses with a variance-based rate penalty. Our design produces codecs that deliver superior rate-distortion performance compared to state-of-the-art generative tokenizers, while remaining practical for deployment on low-power sensors. We release our code, experiments, and python library at https://github.com/UT-SysML/liveaction .
中文标题/摘要
标题：LiVeAction：一种轻量、通用且非对称的神经编码器设计，适用于实时操作
现代传感器生成丰富、高保真数据，但运行于可穿戴或遥感设备上的应用仍受限于带宽和功耗预算。标准化编码器如JPEG和MPEG在比特率与感知质量之间实现了高效权衡，但其设计针对人类感知，限制了其在机器感知任务及非传统模态（如空间音频阵列、高光谱图像和3D医学图像）中的适用性。基于标量量化或分辨率降低的通用压缩方案虽具广泛适用性，却未能利用信号固有冗余，导致率失真性能不佳。近期的生成式神经编码器（或称分词器）虽能建模复杂信号依赖关系，但常存在参数过多、数据需求大、模态特定等问题，难以在资源受限环境中实用。我们提出一种轻量、通用且非对称的神经编码器架构（LiVeAction），通过两个关键思想解决上述局限：（1）为满足执行环境的资源约束，降低编码器复杂度，采用类似FFT的结构并减少基于神经网络的分析变换的整体规模与深度；（2）为支持任意信号模态并简化训练，用基于方差的速率惩罚替代对抗性与感知损失。该设计生成的编码器在率失真性能上优于当前最先进的生成式分词器，同时仍适用于低功耗传感器部署。我们的代码、实验及Python库已发布于https://github.com/UT-SysML/liveaction。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient compression on resource-constrained devices like wearables and remote sensors, LiVeAction proposes a lightweight, asymmetric neural codec that avoids the inefficiencies of traditional codecs and over-parameterized generative models. It achieves this by adopting an FFT-like structure to simplify the encoder and reduce network depth, while replacing complex perceptual losses with a variance-based rate penalty to enable training across diverse modalities without modality-specific tuning. Experimental results show that LiVeAction outperforms state-of-the-art generative tokenizers in rate-distortion efficiency while remaining deployable on low-power hardware.
为满足可穿戴设备和远程传感器等资源受限场景对高效压缩的需求，LiVeAction提出一种轻量级、非对称的神经编码架构，避免传统编解码器和过度参数化生成模型的效率缺陷。其通过采用类似FFT的结构简化编码器神经变换，并用基于方差的速率惩罚替代感知损失，以支持任意模态并降低训练复杂度。实验表明，该架构在率失真性能上优于当前最先进的生成式编码器，同时仍可部署于低功耗硬件上。
Flexible Agent Alignment with Goal Inference from Open-Ended Dialog
Authors: Rachel Ma, Jingyi Qu, Andreea Bobu, Dylan Hadfield-Menell
First: 2025-08-20T23:07:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:41:50+00:00
Comments: Previous version of the paper was titled: Open-Universe Assistance Games
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Open-Universe Assistance Games (OU-AGs), a formal framework extending assistance games to LLM-based agents. Effective assistance requires reasoning over human preferences that are unbounded, underspecified, and evolving. Current LLM agents struggle in multi-turn interactions and with maintaining accurate models of user intent in collaborative settings. Existing assistance game formulations assume fixed, predefined preferences, an assumption that breaks down in open-ended dialogue where goals are revised incrementally and expressed in natural language. Grounded in cognitive science accounts of preference construction, we represent human preferences as a dynamically updated distribution over discrete natural-language goals. To operationalize OU-AGs, we introduce GOOD (GOals from Open-ended Dialogue), a data-efficient online method that extracts and ranks candidate goals during interaction, using LLM-simulated users to perform probabilistic inference over goal hypotheses. This allows for interpretable, uncertainty-aware preference representations without large offline datasets. We evaluate GOOD across three text-based domains: grocery shopping, household robotics (AI2-THOR), and coding. Compared to baselines without explicit goal tracking, GOOD produces semantically coherent goal representations and improves alignment with user intent across domains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过开放对话中的目标推断实现灵活的代理对齐
我们提出了开放宇宙协助游戏（OU-AGs），这是一个将协助游戏扩展到基于大语言模型（LLM）代理的正式框架。有效的协助需要推理人类偏好，这些偏好是无界的、未充分指定且不断演变的。当前的LLM代理在多轮交互和协作环境中维持准确的用户意图模型方面存在困难。现有的协助游戏形式假设固定且预定义的偏好，这一假设在开放对话中失效，因为目标会逐步修订并以自然语言表达。基于认知科学对偏好构建的理论，我们将人类偏好表示为在离散自然语言目标上的动态更新分布。为实现OU-AGs，我们引入了GOOD（从开放对话中提取目标），一种数据高效的在线方法，它在交互过程中提取并排序候选目标，利用LLM模拟用户对目标假设进行概率推理。这使得无需大规模离线数据即可实现可解释且具有不确定性的偏好表示。我们在三个基于文本的领域——购物、家庭机器人（AI2-THOR）和编程中评估了GOOD。与没有显式目标跟踪的基线方法相比，GOOD生成语义连贯的目标表示，并在各领域中提升了与用户意图的对齐效果。

PianoCoRe: Combined and Refined Piano MIDI Dataset
Authors: Ilya Borovik
Venue: Transactions of the International Society for Music Information Retrieval, 9(1), 144-163, 2026
First: 2026-05-07T17:41:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:41:07+00:00
Comments: Published in TISMIR. Project repository: https://github.com/ilya16/PianoCoRe
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Symbolic music datasets with matched scores and performances are essential for many music information retrieval (MIR) tasks. Yet, existing resources often cover a narrow range of composers, lack performance variety, omit note-level alignments, or use inconsistent naming formats. This work presents PianoCoRe, a large-scale piano MIDI dataset that unifies and refines major open-source piano corpora. The dataset contains 250,046 performances of 5,625 pieces written by 483 composers, totaling 21,763 h of performed music. PianoCoRe is released in tiered subsets to support different applications: from large-scale analysis and pre-training (PianoCoRe-C and deduplicated PianoCoRe-B) to expressive performance modeling with note-level score alignment (PianoCoRe-A/A*). The note-aligned subset, PianoCoRe-A, provides the largest open-source collection of 157,207 performances aligned to 1,591 scores to date. In addition to the dataset, the contributions are: (1) a MIDI quality classifier for detecting corrupted and score-like transcriptions and (2) RAScoP, an alignment refinement pipeline that cleans temporal alignment errors and interpolates missing notes. The analysis shows that the refinement reduces temporal noise and eliminates tempo outliers. Moreover, an expressive performance rendering model trained on PianoCoRe demonstrates improved robustness to unseen pieces compared to models trained on raw or smaller datasets. PianoCoRe provides a ready-to-use foundation for the next generation of expressive piano performance research.

标题：PianoCoRe：综合与优化的钢琴MIDI数据集

Summary / 总结
To address limitations in existing piano MIDI datasets—such as limited composer coverage, lack of performance diversity, missing note-level alignments, and inconsistent naming—this work introduces PianoCoRe, a large-scale unified and refined dataset containing 250,046 performances of 5,625 pieces by 483 composers. The dataset is structured into tiered subsets: PianoCoRe-C and B for large-scale analysis and pre-training, and PianoCoRe-A/A* for expressive performance modeling with note-level score alignment. 为解决现有钢琴MIDI数据集在作曲家覆盖范围窄、表演多样性不足、缺乏音符级对齐及命名格式不统一等问题，本研究提出PianoCoRe，一个大规模统一且优化的钢琴MIDI数据集，包含483位作曲家创作的5625首作品共250046个表演，总时长21763小时。数据集按用途划分为多个子集：PianoCoRe-C用于大规模预训练，PianoCoRe-B用于去重，PianoCoRe-A/A*用于音符级对齐的表演建模。主要贡献包括用于检测损坏或谱面式转录的MIDI质量分类器，以及RAScoP对齐优化管道，可修正时序误差并插补缺失音符。实验表明，优化显著降低了时序噪声并消除异常速度，且基于PianoCoRe训练的表演模型在面对未见曲目时比基于原始或小规模数据训练的模型表现更稳健。
Parser agreement and disagreement in L2 Korean UD: Implications for human-in-the-loop annotation
Authors: Hakyung Sung, Gyu-Ho Shin
First: 2026-05-07T17:39:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:39:04+00:00
Comments: To be published in the 20th Linguistic Annotation Workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose a simplified human-in-the-loop workflow for second language (L2) Korean morphosyntactic annotation by leveraging agreement between two domain-adapted parsers. We first evaluate whether parser agreement can serve as a proxy for annotation correctness by comparing it with independent human judgments. The results show strong correspondence between parser and human judgments, supporting the feasibility of semi-automatic L2-Korean UD annotation. Further analysis demonstrates that parser disagreements cluster in linguistically predictable domains such as grammatical-relation distinctions and clause-boundary ambiguity. While many disagreement cases are tractable for iterative model refinement, others reflect deeper representational challenges inherent in parsing and tagging L2-Korean corpora.

我们提出了一种简化的人机协同工作流程，通过利用两个领域自适应解析器之间的一致性，实现第二语言（L2）韩语形态句法标注。我们首先评估解析器一致性是否可作为标注正确性的代理，通过将其与独立的人类判断进行比较。结果表明，解析器与人类判断高度一致，支持半自动L2韩语依存句法标注的可行性。进一步分析显示，解析器分歧主要集中在语言学可预测的领域，如句法关系区分和从句边界模糊性。虽然许多分歧案例可通过迭代模型优化解决，但其他案例则反映了在解析和标注L2韩语语料库时固有的深层表征挑战。
