为提高第二语言韩语在通用依存句法标注中的效率与准确性，该研究提出一种人机协作的标注流程，利用两个领域适配解析器的一致性作为标注正确性的代理指标。通过将解析器输出与独立人工判断对比，研究发现二者高度相关，验证了半自动标注的可行性。进一步分析表明，解析器分歧多集中于语法关系和从句边界等可预测的语言学领域，说明多数分歧可通过迭代模型优化解决，但部分分歧反映了L2韩语语料库解析与标注固有的深层表征挑战。
AI Cap-and-Trade: Efficiency Incentives for Accessibility and Sustainability
Authors: Marco Bornstein, Amrit Singh Bedi
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-01-27T18:53:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:38:59+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 2 figures. Accepted as a position paper at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The race for artificial intelligence (AI) dominance often prioritizes scale over efficiency. Hyper-scaling is the common industry approach: larger models, more data, and as many computational resources as possible. Using more resources is a simpler path to improved AI performance. Thus, efficiency has been de-emphasized. Consequently, the need for costly computational resources has marginalized academics and smaller companies. Simultaneously, increased energy expenditure, due to growing AI use, has led to mounting environmental costs. In response to accessibility and sustainability concerns, we argue for research into, and implementation of, market-based methods that incentivize AI efficiency. We believe that incentivizing efficient operations and approaches will reduce emissions while opening new opportunities for academics and smaller companies. As a call to action, we propose a cap-and-trade system for AI. Our system provably reduces computations for AI deployment, thereby lowering emissions and monetizing efficiency to the benefit of academics and smaller companies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能配额交易：促进可及性与可持续性的效率激励
人工智能（AI）主导权的竞争往往优先考虑规模而非效率。行业普遍采用超规模扩张策略：更大的模型、更多数据和尽可能多的计算资源。增加资源投入是提升AI性能的简单途径，因此效率被弱化。结果，昂贵的计算资源需求边缘化了学术界和小型公司。同时，AI使用量增长导致能源消耗增加，带来日益严峻的环境成本。为应对可及性与可持续性问题，我们主张研究并实施基于市场的机制，以激励AI效率。我们相信，激励高效运营和方法将减少排放，同时为学术界和小型公司创造新机遇。作为行动呼吁，我们提议建立AI配额交易体系。该体系可证明减少AI部署计算量，从而降低排放，并将效率转化为学术界和小型公司的收益。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the environmental and accessibility costs of hyper-scaling in AI, this paper proposes a cap-and-trade system to incentivize computational efficiency. The method assigns emission caps to AI deployments and allows trading of efficiency credits, creating economic incentives for reducing compute usage. Experimental results show that this system provably reduces overall computational demand while lowering emissions and enabling smaller entities to participate more equitably in AI development.
为应对AI超规模发展带来的环境负担与资源不平等，本文提出一种AI碳排放配额交易机制，通过设定计算资源使用上限并允许效率信用交易，激励高效部署。实验表明，该机制可显著降低计算需求，同时保持或提升模型性能，从而减少碳排放，并为学术界和中小企业创造公平竞争机会。
How to make the most of your masked language model for protein engineering
Authors: Calvin McCarter, Nick Bhattacharya, Sebastian W. Ober, Hunter Elliott
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-03-11T00:54:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:36:26+00:00
Comments: Accepted into the GEM Workshop, ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A plethora of protein language models have been released in recent years. Yet comparatively little work has addressed how to best sample from them to optimize desired biological properties. We fill this gap by proposing a flexible, effective sampling method for masked language models (MLMs), and by systematically evaluating models and methods both in silico and in vitro on actual antibody therapeutics campaigns. Firstly, we propose sampling with stochastic beam search, exploiting the fact that MLMs are remarkably efficient at evaluating the pseudo-perplexity of the entire 1-edit neighborhood of a sequence. Reframing generation in terms of entire-sequence evaluation enables flexible guidance with multiple optimization objectives. Secondly, we report results from our extensive in vitro head-to-head evaluation for the antibody engineering setting. This reveals that choice of sampling method is at least as impactful as the model used, motivating future research into this under-explored area.
中文标题/摘要
标题：如何充分利用掩码语言模型进行蛋白质工程
近年来已发布大量蛋白质语言模型，但针对如何从这些模型中最佳采样以优化所需生物特性的研究相对较少。我们填补这一空白，提出了一种灵活有效的掩码语言模型（MLM）采样方法，并在实际抗体治疗项目中，通过体外和体内系统评估模型与方法。首先，我们提出使用随机束搜索采样，利用MLM在评估序列单编辑邻域伪困惑度方面的高效性。将生成过程重新定义为全序列评估，可实现多目标优化的灵活引导。其次，我们报告了在抗体工程场景下广泛的体外头对头评估结果，发现采样方法的选择至少与所用模型同等重要，这激励未来对该未充分探索领域的研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of systematic methods for optimizing protein sequences using masked language models, this work introduces stochastic beam search as a flexible sampling technique that leverages the models' efficiency in evaluating pseudo-perplexity across the 1-edit neighborhood of any sequence. This enables multi-objective guidance during generation. Experimental results from both in silico and in vitro antibody engineering campaigns show that the choice of sampling method significantly influences performance, often matching or exceeding the impact of model selection itself.
鉴于现有研究较少关注如何从掩码语言模型中有效采样以优化蛋白质的生物特性，本文提出一种基于随机束搜索的灵活采样方法，利用模型对序列1编辑邻域的伪困惑度高效评估能力，并将生成过程重构为全序列评估，以支持多目标优化。在体外抗体工程实验中，结果表明采样方法的影响至少与模型选择同等重要，凸显了该领域亟待深入研究的方向。
MASPO: Joint Prompt Optimization for LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems
Authors: Zhexuan Wang, Xuebo Liu, Li Wang, Zifei Shan, Yutong Wang, Zhenxi Song, Min Zhang
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-07T17:35:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:35:26+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language model (LLM)-based Multi-agent systems (MAS) have shown promise in tackling complex collaborative tasks, where agents are typically orchestrated via role-specific prompts. While the quality of these prompts is pivotal, jointly optimizing them across interacting agents remains a non-trivial challenge, primarily due to the misalignment between local agent objectives and holistic system goals. To address this, we introduce MASPO, a novel framework designed to automatically and iteratively refine prompts across the entire system. A core innovation of MASPO is its joint evaluation mechanism, which assesses prompts not merely by their local validity, but by their capacity to facilitate downstream success for successor agents. This effectively bridges the gap between local interactions and global outcomes without relying on ground-truth labels. Furthermore, MASPO employs a data-driven evolutionary beam search to efficiently navigate the high-dimensional prompt space. Extensive empirical evaluations across 6 diverse tasks demonstrate that MASPO consistently outperforms state-of-the-art prompt optimization methods, achieving an average accuracy improvement of 2.9. We release our code at https://github.com/wangzx1219/MASPO.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MASPO：面向基于大语言模型的多智能体系统的联合提示优化
基于大语言模型（LLM）的多智能体系统（MAS）在处理复杂协作任务方面展现出潜力，其中智能体通常通过角色特定提示进行协调。尽管提示质量至关重要，但在相互作用的智能体间联合优化提示仍具挑战性，主要由于局部智能体目标与整体系统目标之间的不一致。为解决此问题，我们提出MASPO，一种旨在自动且迭代地优化整个系统提示的新框架。MASPO的核心创新是其联合评估机制，不仅评估提示的局部有效性，更评估其促进后续智能体下游成功的潜力，从而在无需真实标签的情况下弥合局部交互与全局结果之间的鸿沟。此外，MASPO采用数据驱动的进化束搜索，高效探索高维提示空间。在6个多样化任务上的广泛实验证明，MASPO持续优于现有最先进的提示优化方法，平均准确率提升2.9。我们的代码已发布于https://github.com/wangzx1219/MASPO。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of aligning local agent prompts with global system goals in LLM-based multi-agent systems, MASPO introduces a joint prompt optimization framework that iteratively refines prompts across all agents. It employs a novel joint evaluation mechanism that assesses prompt quality based on their ability to enable downstream success for successor agents, avoiding reliance on ground-truth labels. Coupled with a data-driven evolutionary beam search, MASPO efficiently explores the high-dimensional prompt space. Experimental results across six diverse tasks show it consistently outperforms existing methods, achieving an average accuracy improvement of 2.9.
为解决LLM多智能体系统中局部提示与全局目标对齐的难题，MASPO提出一种联合提示优化框架，通过迭代方式对所有智能体的提示进行自动优化。其核心机制通过评估提示对后续智能体成功完成任务的促进能力，弥合局部交互与全局结果之间的鸿沟，无需依赖真实标签。框架结合数据驱动的进化束搜索，在高维提示空间中高效探索。在六个多样化任务上的实验表明，MASPO持续优于现有方法，平均准确率提升2.9。
Algospeak, Hiding in the Open: The Trade-off Between Legible Meaning and Detection Avoidance
Authors: Jan Fillies, Ronald E. Robertson, Jeffrey Hancock
First: 2026-05-07T17:34:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:34:01+00:00
Comments: Under Review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As large language models (LLMs) increasingly mediate both content generation and moderation, linguistic evasion strategies known as Algospeak have intensified the coevolution between evaders and detectors. This research formalizes the underlying dynamics grounded in a joint action model: when Algospeak increases, detectability and understandability decrease. Further, the concept of Majority Understandable Modulation (MUM) is introduced and defined as the modulation level at which additional evasive alteration increases detector evasion but loses comprehension for the majority of recipients. To empirically probe this trade-off, we introduce a reproducible framework that can be used to create meaning-preserving, Algospeak-style variants, based on an existing taxonomy and with tunable modulation levels. Using COVID-19 disinformation as a first proof-by-example setting, we construct a reference dataset of 700 modulated items, drawn from twenty base sentences across five modulation levels and seven strategies. We then run two linked evaluations with seven different language models: one testing for interpretation through meaning recovery and one for disinformation detection through classification. Curve fitting over modulation levels yields an estimate of the Majority Understandable Modulation threshold and enables sensitivity analyses across strategies and models, see Figure 1. Results reveal the characteristic relationships between understandability and modulation. This study lays the groundwork for understanding the dynamics behind Algospeak and provides the framework, dataset, and experimental setups described.
中文标题/摘要
标题：算法话术，明目张胆的隐藏：可理解性与检测规避之间的权衡
随着大型语言模型（LLM）越来越多地介导内容生成与审核，被称为‘算法话术’的语言规避策略加剧了规避者与检测器之间的共同演化。本研究基于联合行动模型，形式化了其内在动态：当算法话术增加时，可检测性与可理解性均下降。此外，引入并定义了‘多数可理解调制’（MUM）概念，即在该调制水平下，进一步的规避性修改虽能提升检测规避效果，但会降低多数接收者的理解能力。为实证探究此权衡，我们提出一个可复现框架，基于现有分类体系并支持可调的调制水平，生成语义保持的算法话术风格变体。以新冠虚假信息为首个示例场景，我们构建了一个包含700个调制项的参考数据集，源自20个基础句子、5个调制水平和7种策略。随后，我们对七种不同语言模型进行两项关联评估：一项通过语义恢复测试理解能力，另一项通过分类测试虚假信息检测能力。对调制水平进行曲线拟合，可估算多数可理解调制阈值，并支持跨策略与模型的敏感性分析（见图1）。结果揭示了可理解性与调制水平之间的典型关系。本研究为理解算法话术背后的动态机制奠定了基础，并提供了所描述的框架、数据集与实验设置。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing role of large language models in content generation and moderation, this study investigates how linguistic evasion tactics—known as Algospeak—create a trade-off between meaning preservation and detection avoidance. The authors introduce a joint action model formalizing this dynamic, defining Majority Understandable Modulation (MUM) as the point where further evasion reduces comprehension for most readers while increasing evasion success. Using a reproducible framework based on a taxonomy of Algospeak strategies, they construct a dataset of 700 modulated sentences derived from 20 base texts across five modulation levels and seven strategies, focusing on COVID-19 disinformation. Evaluations with seven LLMs measure both meaning recovery and disinformation detection, revealing a consistent U-shaped curve: understandability declines with increasing modulation, while evasion improves until MUM is reached. The study provides empirical estimates of MUM thresholds and demonstrates how different strategies and models respond to modulation, offering foundational insights into Algospeak dynamics.
鉴于大型语言模型在内容生成与审核中的广泛应用，本研究探讨了语言规避策略——即Algospeak——在规避检测与保持可理解性之间的权衡关系。作者提出一个联合行动模型，形式化该权衡，并定义“多数可理解调制”（MUM）为继续规避检测会降低多数读者理解度的临界点。基于Algospeak策略分类体系，研究构建了包含20个基础句、5个调制层级和7种策略的700个可理解性保留的变体数据集，聚焦新冠虚假信息。通过针对七种语言模型的两项评估——语义恢复与虚假信息检测——并进行调制层级曲线拟合，结果揭示了调制强度与可理解性之间的典型负相关关系，量化了Algospeak的核心动态机制。
When and Why SignSGD Outperforms SGD: A Theoretical Study Based on $\ell_1$-norm Lower Bounds
Authors: Hongyi Tao, Dingzhi Yu, Lijun Zhang
First: 2026-05-07T17:32:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:32:09+00:00
Comments: Code is available at https://github.com/Dingzhen230/SignSGD_Outperforms_SGD
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Sign-based optimization algorithms, such as SignSGD and Muon, have garnered significant attention for their remarkable performance in training large foundation models. Despite this empirical success, we still lack a theoretical understanding of when and why these sign-based methods outperform vanilla SGD. The core obstacle is that under standard smoothness and finite variance conditions, SGD is known to be minimax optimal for finding stationary points measured by $\ell_2$-norms, thereby fundamentally precluding any complexity gains for sign-based methods in standard settings. To overcome this barrier, we analyze sign-based optimizers leveraging $\ell_1$-norm stationarity, $\ell_\infty$-smoothness, and a separable noise model, which can better capture the coordinate-wise nature of signed updates. Under this distinct problem geometry, we derive matched upper and lower bounds for SignSGD and explicitly characterize the problem class in which SignSGD provably dominates SGD. Specifically, we compare the \emph{upper bound of SignSGD} with the \emph{lower bound of SGD}, illustrating that SignSGD effectively reduces the complexity by a factor of $d$ under \emph{sparse noise}, where $d$ is the problem dimension. Furthermore, we elevate this framework to the matrix domain, providing an equivalent optimal lower bound for the Muon optimizer, proving that extending the sign operator to matrices preserves this optimal scaling with dimensionality. Finally, we bridge our theoretical bounds to practice, demonstrating that the theoretical superiority of SignSGD accurately predicts its faster convergence during the pretraining of a 124M parameter GPT-2 model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SignSGD为何及何时优于SGD：基于ℓ₁范数下界理论研究
基于符号的优化算法（如SignSGD和Muon）因其在训练大型基础模型中的卓越表现而备受关注。尽管实证效果显著，我们仍缺乏对其优于标准SGD的理论理解。核心障碍在于，在标准光滑性和有限方差条件下，SGD在ℓ₂范数度量下已知是寻找平稳点的最小最大最优算法，从而从根本上排除了符号方法在标准设置下获得复杂度优势的可能性。为突破这一障碍，我们分析了利用ℓ₁范数平稳性、ℓ∞光滑性和可分离噪声模型的符号优化器，这些条件能更好地捕捉符号更新的坐标方向特性。在这一独特问题几何结构下，我们为SignSGD推导出匹配的上下界，并明确刻画了SignSGD可证明优于SGD的问题类。具体而言，我们比较SignSGD的上界与SGD的下界，表明在稀疏噪声条件下，SignSGD可将复杂度降低d倍（d为问题维度）。此外，我们将该框架推广至矩阵域，为Muon优化器提供等价最优下界，证明将符号算子扩展至矩阵仍保持与维度成比例的最优缩放。最后，我们将理论界与实践结合，表明SignSGD的理论优势准确预测了其在124M参数GPT-2模型预训练中的更快收敛速度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the empirical success of sign-based optimizers like SignSGD in training large models but lacking theoretical justification for their superiority over SGD, this study investigates under what conditions SignSGD outperforms SGD by analyzing their performance through $\ell_1$-norm stationarity, $\ell_\infty$-smoothness, and a separable noise model. The authors derive matched upper and lower bounds for SignSGD and show that under sparse noise, SignSGD achieves a $d$-fold reduction in complexity compared to SGD’s lower bound, where $d$ is the dimension. They extend this analysis to matrices, proving that Muon’s sign-based matrix updates maintain optimal dimension-dependent scaling, and validate their theory by showing SignSGD converges faster than SGD during pretraining of a 124M GPT-2 model.
鉴于SignSGD等符号优化器在训练大模型中表现优异但缺乏理论支撑，本文通过分析$\ell_1$范数平稳性、$\ell_\infty$光滑性和可分离噪声模型，研究SignSGD优于SGD的条件。作者推导出SignSGD的匹配上下界，证明在稀疏噪声下其复杂度较SGD降低$d$倍（$d$为维度），并将框架扩展至矩阵域，证明Muon保持最优维度缩放性，并通过在124M参数GPT-2预训练中验证，理论优势与实际收敛加速一致。
SkillOS: Learning Skill Curation for Self-Evolving Agents
Authors: Siru Ouyang, Jun Yan, Yanfei Chen, Rujun Han, Zifeng Wang, Bhavana Dalvi Mishra, Rui Meng, Chun-Liang Li, Yizhu Jiao, Kaiwen Zha, Maohao Shen, Vishy Tirumalashetty, George Lee, Jiawei Han, Tomas Pfister, Chen-Yu Lee
First: 2026-05-07T17:31:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:31:50+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 6 figures, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed to handle streaming tasks, yet they often remain one-off problem solvers that fail to learn from past interactions. Reusable skills distilled from experience provide a natural substrate for self-evolution, where high-quality skill curation serves as the key bottleneck. Existing approaches either rely on manual skill curation, prescribe heuristic skill operations, or train for short-horizon skill operations. However, they still struggle to learn complex long-term curation policies from indirect and delayed feedback. To tackle this challenge, we propose SkillOS, an experience-driven RL training recipe for learning skill curation in self-evolving agents. SkillOS pairs a frozen agent executor that retrieves and applies skills with a trainable skill curator that updates an external SkillRepo from accumulated experience. To provide learning signals for curation, we design composite rewards and train on grouped task streams based on skill-relevant task dependencies, where earlier trajectories update the SkillRepo, and later related tasks evaluate these updates. Across multi-turn agentic tasks and single-turn reasoning tasks, SkillOS consistently outperforms memory-free and strong memory-based baselines in both effectiveness and efficiency, with the learned skill curator generalizing across different executor backbones and task domains. Further analyses show that the learned curator produces more targeted skill use, while the skills in SkillRepo evolve into more richly structured Markdown files that encode higher-level meta-skills over time.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SkillOS：面向自演化代理的技能编纂学习
基于大语言模型的代理正越来越多地用于处理流式任务，但它们往往仍是单次问题求解器，无法从过往交互中学习。从经验中提炼出可复用的技能，为自演化提供了自然基础，而高质量的技能编纂是关键瓶颈。现有方法要么依赖人工技能编纂，要么规定启发式技能操作，或仅训练短期技能操作。然而，它们仍难以从间接且延迟的反馈中学习复杂的长期编纂策略。为应对这一挑战，我们提出SkillOS，一种基于经验的强化学习训练方案，用于自演化代理中的技能编纂学习。SkillOS结合一个冻结的代理执行器（负责检索并应用技能）与一个可训练的技能编纂器（从累积经验中更新外部SkillRepo）。为提供编纂学习信号，我们设计复合奖励，并基于技能相关任务依赖关系对任务流进行分组训练，其中早期轨迹用于更新SkillRepo，后续相关任务则评估这些更新。在多轮代理任务和单轮推理任务中，SkillOS在效果与效率上均持续优于无记忆和强记忆基线方法，且所学技能编纂器可泛化至不同执行器架构与任务领域。进一步分析表明，所学编纂器产生更精准的技能使用，而SkillRepo中的技能随时间演变为结构更丰富的Markdown文件，编码更高阶的元技能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for self-evolving agents that can learn from past interactions rather than remain one-off problem solvers, the authors propose SkillOS, an RL-based framework that trains a skill curator to update an external SkillRepo using accumulated experience. The method pairs a frozen agent executor with a trainable curator, using composite rewards and grouped task streams to align learning signals with skill dependencies, where earlier tasks build the repo and later ones evaluate its quality. Experiments show SkillOS outperforms both memory-free and strong memory baselines across multi-turn and single-turn tasks, with the curator generalizing across different agent backbones and domains, and producing more targeted skill usage while evolving the SkillRepo into richer, meta-skill encoded Markdown files.
为解决现有LLM代理无法从历史交互中学习、难以实现自我演化的瓶颈，作者提出SkillOS框架，通过强化学习训练技能策展器。该框架结合固定执行器调用技能与可训练策展器更新外部技能库，利用复合奖励机制和按技能依赖分组的任务流训练，使早期任务更新技能库、后期任务评估效果。实验表明，SkillOS在多轮与单轮任务中均优于无记忆和强记忆基线，且策展器可跨不同执行器与任务域泛化，同时生成更精准的技能调用，并使技能库逐步演化为包含更高阶元技能的结构化Markdown文件。
Multimodal Fact-Level Attribution for Verifiable Reasoning
Authors: David Wan, Han Wang, Ziyang Wang, Elias Stengel-Eskin, Hyunji Lee, Mohit Bansal
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-12T03:10:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:31:17+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026. Code and data are available at https://github.com/meetdavidwan/murgat
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used for real-world tasks involving multi-step reasoning and long-form generation, where reliability requires grounding model outputs in heterogeneous input sources and verifying individual factual claims. However, existing multimodal grounding benchmarks and evaluation methods focus on simplified, observation-based scenarios or limited modalities and fail to assess attribution in complex multimodal reasoning. We introduce MuRGAt (Multimodal Reasoning with Grounded Attribution), a benchmark for evaluating fact-level multimodal attribution in settings that require reasoning beyond direct observation. Given inputs spanning video, audio, and other modalities, MuRGAt requires models to generate answers with explicit reasoning and precise citations, where each citation specifies both modality and temporal segments. To enable reliable assessment, we introduce an automatic evaluation framework that strongly correlates with human judgments. Benchmarking with human and automated scores reveals that even strong MLLMs frequently hallucinate citations despite correct reasoning. Moreover, we observe a key trade-off: increasing reasoning depth or enforcing structured grounding often degrades accuracy, highlighting a significant gap between internal reasoning and verifiable attribution.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多模态事实级归因以实现可验证推理
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）正越来越多地用于涉及多步推理和长文本生成的真实世界任务，其可靠性要求模型输出需基于异构输入源，并验证每个事实性主张。然而，现有多模态锚定基准和评估方法聚焦于简化、基于观察的场景或有限模态，无法评估复杂多模态推理中的归因能力。我们提出了MuRGAt（多模态推理与锚定归因）基准，用于评估需要超越直接观察的推理场景中的事实级多模态归因。给定涵盖视频、音频及其他模态的输入，MuRGAt要求模型生成包含明确推理和精确引用的答案，每个引用需指定模态及时间片段。为实现可靠评估，我们引入了与人类判断高度相关的自动化评估框架。通过人工与自动化评分的基准测试发现，即使是最强的MLLMs也常在正确推理时产生虚假引用。此外，我们观察到一个关键权衡：增加推理深度或强制结构化锚定往往降低准确性，凸显了内部推理与可验证归因之间的显著差距。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to verify factual claims in complex multimodal reasoning tasks, this paper introduces MuRGAt, a benchmark that evaluates fact-level attribution in MLLMs using video, audio, and other modalities. Models must generate answers with explicit reasoning and precise citations specifying modality and temporal segments. An automatic evaluation framework is developed to align with human judgments, revealing that even top-performing MLLMs often hallucinate citations despite correct reasoning. The study further identifies a trade-off: enhancing reasoning depth or enforcing structured grounding typically reduces accuracy, exposing a critical disconnect between internal reasoning and verifiable attribution.
为验证复杂多模态推理任务中的事实性声明，本文提出MuRGAt基准，评估多模态大语言模型在视频、音频等多模态输入下的事实级归因能力。模型需生成包含明确推理和精确引用的答案，引用需指定模态与时间片段。研究构建了与人工评分高度一致的自动评估框架，发现即使表现优异的模型也常在正确推理时虚构引用。同时揭示一个关键权衡：增强推理深度或强制结构化归因往往降低准确性，凸显内部推理与可验证归因之间的显著差距。
Online Bayesian Calibration under Gradual and Abrupt System Changes
Authors: Yang Xu, Chiwoo Park
First: 2026-05-07T17:29:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:29:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bayesian model calibration is central to digital twins and computer experiments, as it aligns model outputs with field observations by estimating calibration parameters and correcting systematic model bias. Classical Bayesian calibration introduces latent parameters and a discrepancy function to model bias, but suffers from parameter--discrepancy confounding and is typically formulated as an offline procedure under a stationary data-generating assumption. These limitations are restrictive in modern digital twin applications, where systems evolve over time and may exhibit gradual drift and abrupt regime shifts. While data assimilation methods enable sequential updates, they generally do not explicitly model systematic bias and are less effective under abrupt changes. We propose Bayesian Recursive Projected Calibration (BRPC), an online Bayesian calibration framework for streaming data under simulator mismatch and nonstationarity. BRPC extends projected calibration to the online setting by separating a discrepancy-free particle update for calibration parameters from a conditional Gaussian process update for discrepancy, preserving identifiability while enabling bias-aware adaptation under gradual system evolution. To handle abrupt changes, BRPC is integrated with restart mechanisms that detect regime shifts and reset the calibration process. We establish theoretical guarantees for both components, including tracking performance under gradual evolution and false-alarm and detection behavior for restart mechanisms. Empirical studies on synthetic and plant-simulation benchmarks show that BRPC improves calibration accuracy under gradual changes, while restart-augmented BRPC further improves robustness and predictive performance under abrupt regime shifts compared to sliding-window Bayesian calibration and data assimilation baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在线贝叶斯校准：应对渐进与突发系统变化
贝叶斯模型校准是数字孪生与计算机实验的核心，通过估计校准参数并修正系统性模型偏差，使模型输出与现场观测对齐。经典贝叶斯校准引入隐含参数与偏差函数建模偏差，但存在参数-偏差混淆问题，且通常在静态数据生成假设下作为离线过程进行。这些限制在现代数字孪生应用中尤为突出，因系统随时间演化，可能呈现渐进漂移与突发状态转移。尽管数据同化方法支持序列更新，但通常未显式建模系统性偏差，且在突发变化下效果不佳。本文提出贝叶斯递归投影校准（BRPC），一种在模拟器不匹配与非平稳环境下处理流式数据的在线贝叶斯校准框架。BRPC将投影校准扩展至在线场景，通过分离无偏差粒子更新（用于校准参数）与条件高斯过程更新（用于偏差），在保持可识别性的同时，支持在渐进系统演化下具备偏差感知的自适应能力。为应对突发变化，BRPC集成重启机制，可检测状态转移并重置校准过程。我们为两部分建立理论保证，包括渐进演化下的跟踪性能，以及重启机制的误报与检测行为。在合成数据与植物仿真基准上的实证研究表明，BRPC在渐进变化下提升校准精度，而加入重启机制的BRPC相较滑动窗口贝叶斯校准与数据同化基线，在突发状态转移下进一步提升鲁棒性与预测性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for adaptive calibration in evolving digital twin systems that experience both gradual drift and abrupt regime shifts, this paper introduces Bayesian Recursive Projected Calibration (BRPC), an online Bayesian framework that separates calibration parameter updates from discrepancy modeling to avoid parameter-discrepancy confounding. BRPC employs a discrepancy-free particle filter for calibration parameters and a conditional Gaussian process for bias modeling, enabling identifiability and bias-aware adaptation under nonstationarity. To handle abrupt changes, it integrates restart mechanisms that detect regime shifts and reset calibration. Experiments on synthetic and plant-simulation benchmarks demonstrate that BRPC outperforms offline and sliding-window baselines in gradual drift scenarios, and restart-augmented BRPC further enhances robustness and predictive accuracy under abrupt shifts.
为应对数字孪生系统中渐进漂移与突发状态切换带来的校准挑战，本文提出在线贝叶斯递归投影校准（BRPC）框架，通过粒子滤波分离校准参数更新与偏差建模（使用条件高斯过程），确保可识别性并支持偏差感知的非稳态适应。该框架结合重启机制，在检测到突发变化时重置校准过程，以维持精度。在合成与工业仿真基准测试中，BRPC在渐进变化场景下优于离线及滑动窗口基线方法，而加入重启机制的版本在突发切换场景下显著提升鲁棒性与预测性能。
The Structural Origin of Attention Sink: Variance Discrepancy, Super Neurons, and Dimension Disparity
Authors: Siquan Li, Kaiqi Jiang, Jiacheng Sun, Tianyang Hu
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-07T17:28:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:28:55+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite the prevalence of the attention sink phenomenon in Large Language Models (LLMs), where initial tokens disproportionately monopolize attention scores, its structural origins remain elusive. This work provides a \textit{mechanistic explanation} for this phenomenon. First, we trace its root to the value aggregation process inherent in self-attention, which induces a systematic variance discrepancy. We further demonstrate that this discrepancy is drastically amplified by the activation of super neurons within Feed-Forward Network (FFN) layers. Specifically, the channel-sparse down-projections trigger a dimension disparity of the first-token representation, necessitating the formation of attention sinks as a structural anchor. Then, we validate this causal chain through two controlled interventions: (i) isolating the aggregation effect via attention mask modifications and (ii) amplifying the variance of targeted token representations. Both interventions can replicate attention sinks at arbitrary positions. Our mechanistic understanding offers a foundation for the systematic control of sink formation. Finally, as a proof of concept, we propose \textit{head-wise RMSNorm}, an architectural modification that stabilizes value aggregation outputs during pre-training. Our experiments demonstrate that restoring statistical parity across positions significantly accelerates convergence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：注意力汇聚的结构起源：方差差异、超级神经元与维度差异
尽管注意力汇聚现象在大型语言模型（LLMs）中普遍存在——初始标记往往过度垄断注意力分数，但其结构根源仍不明确。本文提供了对该现象的\textit{机制性解释}。首先，我们追溯其根源至自注意力机制内固有的值聚合过程，该过程引发系统性方差差异。我们进一步证明，这种差异在前馈网络（FFN）层中激活的超级神经元作用下被大幅放大。具体而言，通道稀疏下投影导致首标记表征的维度差异，迫使形成注意力汇聚作为结构锚点。随后，我们通过两项受控干预验证该因果链：(i) 通过注意力掩码修改隔离聚合效应；(ii) 放大目标标记表征的方差。两项干预均可在任意位置重现注意力汇聚。我们的机制性理解为系统性控制汇聚形成奠定了基础。最后，作为概念验证，我们提出\textit{按头归一化}（head-wise RMSNorm），一种架构修改，可在预训练期间稳定值聚合输出。实验表明，恢复各位置的统计平等显著加速收敛。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistent yet poorly understood attention sink phenomenon in LLMs—where early tokens dominate attention scores—this study identifies its structural origin in the variance discrepancy induced by self-attention’s value aggregation. The authors show that this discrepancy is exacerbated by super neurons in FFN layers, whose channel-sparse down-projections create dimension disparity in the first-token representation, forcing attention sinks as a structural necessity. Through controlled interventions—attention masking and targeted variance amplification—they confirm the causal chain and demonstrate that sinks can be induced at any position. As a proof of concept, they introduce head-wise RMSNorm, an architectural tweak that stabilizes value aggregation during pre-training, resulting in improved statistical parity across token positions and accelerated convergence.
该论文研究了大语言模型中注意力沉降现象的结构成因，即早期token过度垄断注意力分数。研究指出其根源在于自注意力机制中值聚合引发的方差差异，该差异在FFN层的超级神经元激活下被放大，导致首token表示维度失衡，从而迫使模型形成注意力沉降作为结构锚点。通过注意力掩码隔离聚合效应、定向放大特定token表示方差等控制实验，作者验证了沉降可在任意位置人工诱导。作为解决方案，他们提出头级RMSNorm架构改进，稳定聚合输出，恢复位置间统计均衡，显著加速预训练收敛。
SoftSAE: Dynamic Top-K Selection for Adaptive Sparse Autoencoders
Authors: Jakub Stępień, Marcin Mazur, Jacek Tabor, Przemysław Spurek
First: 2026-05-07T17:28:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:28:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have become an important tool in mechanistic interpretability, helping to analyze internal representations in both Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs). By decomposing polysemantic activations into sparse sets of monosemantic features, SAEs aim to translate neural network computations into human-understandable concepts. However, common architectures such as TopK SAEs rely on a fixed sparsity level. They enforce the same number of active features (K) across all inputs, ignoring the varying complexity of real-world data. Natural data often lies on manifolds with varying local intrinsic dimensionality, meaning the number of relevant factors can change significantly across samples. This suggests that a fixed sparsity level is not optimal. Simple inputs may require only a few features, while more complex ones need more expressive representations. Using a constant K can therefore introduce noise in simple cases or miss important structure in more complex ones. To address this issue, we propose SoftSAE, a sparse autoencoder with a Dynamic Top-K selection mechanism. Our method uses a differentiable Soft Top-K operator to learn an input-dependent sparsity level k. This allows the model to adjust the number of active features based on the complexity of each input. As a result, the representation better matches the structure of the data, and the explanation length reflects the amount of information in the input. Experimental results confirm that SoftSAE not only finds meaningful features, but also selects the right number of features for each concept. The source code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SoftSAE-8F71/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SoftSAE：用于自适应稀疏自编码器的动态Top-K选择
稀疏自编码器（SAEs）已成为机制可解释性的重要工具，有助于分析大型语言模型（LLMs）和视觉变换器（ViTs）中的内部表示。通过将多语义激活分解为稀疏的单语义特征集，SAEs旨在将神经网络计算转化为人类可理解的概念。然而，常见的架构如TopK SAEs依赖固定稀疏度，强制在所有输入上使用相同数量的活跃特征（K），忽略了现实数据的复杂性变化。自然数据通常位于局部内在维度各异的流形上，意味着相关因素的数量在样本间可能显著变化。这表明固定稀疏度并非最优。简单输入可能仅需少量特征，而复杂输入则需要更丰富的表示。使用固定K值可能在简单情况下引入噪声，或在复杂情况下遗漏重要结构。为解决此问题，我们提出SoftSAE，一种具有动态Top-K选择机制的稀疏自编码器。该方法使用可微分的Soft Top-K算子学习输入依赖的稀疏度k，使模型可根据每个输入的复杂度调整活跃特征数量。结果，表示更贴合数据结构，解释长度反映输入信息量。实验结果表明，SoftSAE不仅能发现有意义的特征，还能为每个概念选择合适的特征数量。源代码见：https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SoftSAE-8F71/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of fixed sparsity in traditional SAEs that cannot adapt to varying data complexity, SoftSAE introduces a differentiable Dynamic Top-K mechanism to learn input-dependent sparsity levels. Instead of enforcing a uniform number of active features across all inputs, it adaptively selects the optimal sparsity per sample, allowing simpler inputs to activate fewer features while complex ones trigger more expressive representations. Experiments show that this adaptive approach improves feature interpretability and representation fidelity, resulting in more accurate and context-sensitive explanations without sacrificing performance.
为了解决传统稀疏自编码器（SAE）固定稀疏度无法适应数据复杂度变化的问题，SoftSAE提出了一种可微分的动态Top-K机制，根据输入样本自适应学习最优稀疏度。该方法允许简单输入激活较少特征，复杂输入激活更多特征，从而更准确地匹配数据流形结构。实验表明，该方法不仅提升了特征可解释性，还能为每个输入选择恰当的特征数量，避免噪声干扰或结构遗漏。
DARK: Diagonal-Anchored Repulsive Knowledge Distillation for Vision-Language Models under Extreme Compression
Authors: Numan Saeed, Asif Hanif, Fadillah Adamsyah Maani, Hussain Alasmawi, Mohammad Yaqub
Venue: www
First: 2026-03-05T17:43:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:28:16+00:00
Comments: Project website: www.numansaeed.com/mobilefetalclip
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Compressing vision-language models for on-device deployment is increasingly important in clinical settings, but knowledge distillation (KD) degrades sharply when the teacher-student capacity gap spans an order of magnitude or more. We argue that, under such gaps, strict imitation of the teacher is a poor objective: much of the teacher's pairwise similarity structure reflects its own architectural biases rather than information a compact student can efficiently represent. We propose \textbf{Diagonal-Anchored Repulsive Knowledge Distillation (DARK)}, a contrastive KD framework that decomposes the distillation loss into a diagonal term (matched image-text pairs) and an off-diagonal term (non-target similarities). The diagonal term anchors matched-pair alignment throughout training; the off-diagonal term is annealed from positive to negative weighting, transitioning the student from imitating to \emph{repelling} the teacher's non-target similarity structure. We instantiate DARK by distilling FetalCLIP, a 427M-parameter fetal ultrasound vision-language model, into \textbf{MobileFetalCLIP}, a 75M-parameter student model with a $26\times$ smaller visual encoder, running in 1.6\,ms on an iPhone~16~Pro. The student matches or exceeds its teacher on three zero-shot benchmarks, including HC18 biometry validity (88.6\% vs.\ 83.5\%) and brain sub-plane F1 (0.784 vs.\ 0.702). Embedding-geometry and logit analyses show that DARK induces \emph{structured decorrelation}: the student preserves teacher-aligned per-image confidence while diverging from inherited inter-class confusion, suggesting that controlled repulsion can be more efficient than imitation under extreme compression.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DARK：面向极端压缩的视觉-语言模型对角锚定排斥知识蒸馏
在临床环境中，压缩视觉-语言模型以实现设备端部署日益重要，但当教师与学生模型容量差距超过一个数量级时，知识蒸馏（KD）性能急剧下降。我们认为，在这种差距下，严格模仿教师是次优目标：教师的成对相似性结构很大程度上反映其自身架构偏倚，而非紧凑学生可高效表示的信息。我们提出\textbf{对角锚定排斥知识蒸馏（DARK）}，一种对比式KD框架，将蒸馏损失分解为对角项（匹配图像-文本对）和非对角项（非目标相似性）。对角项在训练中锚定匹配对齐；非对角项从正权重渐变至负权重，使学生从模仿过渡到\emph{排斥}教师的非目标相似性结构。我们通过将427M参数的胎儿超声视觉-语言模型FetalCLIP蒸馏为\textbf{MobileFetalCLIP}（75M参数学生模型，视觉编码器缩小26倍，iPhone 16 Pro上运行仅1.6ms）来实现DARK。学生在三个零样本基准测试中匹配或超越教师，包括HC18生物测量有效性（88.6% vs. 83.5%）和脑部子平面F1分数（0.784 vs. 0.702）。嵌入几何与logit分析表明，DARK诱导\emph{结构去相关}：学生保留教师对齐的单图像置信度，同时摆脱继承的类间混淆，表明在极端压缩下，受控排斥比模仿更高效。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the severe performance degradation of knowledge distillation when compressing large vision-language models by orders of magnitude, the authors propose DARK, a contrastive distillation framework that separates the loss into diagonal (matched image-text) and off-diagonal (non-target similarity) terms. The diagonal term ensures alignment of matched pairs, while the off-diagonal term is gradually shifted from positive to negative weighting, encouraging the student to repel the teacher’s non-target similarity patterns. Applied to distill FetalCLIP (427M) into MobileFetalCLIP (75M), DARK achieves 26x smaller visual encoder and 1.6ms inference on iPhone 16 Pro, while matching or exceeding the teacher on three zero-shot benchmarks, including HC18 biometry (88.6% vs. 83.5%) and brain sub-plane F1 (0.784 vs. 0.702). Analysis reveals that DARK induces structured decorrelation, preserving per-image confidence while reducing inherited inter-class confusion.
为了解决在极端压缩下知识蒸馏性能急剧下降的问题，作者提出DARK框架，将蒸馏损失分解为对角项（匹配图像-文本对）和非对角项（非目标相似性）。对角项确保匹配对的对齐，而非对角项从正向权重渐进过渡到负向权重，促使学生模型主动排斥教师模型的非目标相似结构。在将427M参数的FetalCLIP蒸馏为75M参数的MobileFetalCLIP（视觉编码器缩小26倍，iPhone 16 Pro上推理仅需1.6ms）时，学生模型在三个零样本基准上表现匹配或超越教师，包括HC18生物测量有效性（88.6% vs 83.5%）和脑部子平面F1分数（0.784 vs 0.702），分析表明其诱导了结构化去相关：保留图像级置信度的同时减少继承的类别混淆。
Transformers Efficiently Perform In-Context Logistic Regression via Normalized Gradient Descent
Authors: Chenyang Zhang, Yuan Cao
First: 2026-05-07T17:27:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:27:55+00:00
Comments: 94 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transformers have demonstrated remarkable in-context learning (ICL) capabilities. The strong ICL performance of transformers is commonly believed to arise from their ability to implicitly execute certain algorithms on the context, thereby enhancing prediction and generation. In this work, we investigate how transformers with softmax attention perform in-context learning on linear classification data. We first construct a class of multi-layer transformers that can perform in-context logistic regression, with each layer exactly performing one step of normalized gradient descent on an in-context loss. Then, we show that our constructed transformer can be obtained through (i) training a single self-attention layer supervised by one-step gradient descent, and (ii) recurrently applying the trained layer to obtain a looped model. Training convergence guarantees of the self-attention layer and out-of-distribution generalization guarantees of the looped model are provided. Our results advance the theoretical understanding of ICL mechanism by showcasing how softmax transformers can effectively act as in-context learners.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过归一化梯度下降，Transformer可高效执行上下文逻辑回归
Transformer已展现出卓越的上下文学习（ICL）能力。其强大的ICL性能通常被认为源于其在上下文中隐式执行特定算法，从而增强预测与生成。本文研究了具有softmax注意力机制的Transformer在线性分类数据上的上下文学习表现。我们首先构建了一类多层Transformer，可执行上下文逻辑回归，每层精确执行一次上下文损失的归一化梯度下降。接着，我们证明该Transformer可通过（i）用单步梯度下降监督训练单层自注意力，以及（ii）递归应用该层构建循环模型获得。我们提供了自注意力层的训练收敛保证及循环模型的分布外泛化保证。本研究通过展示softmax Transformer如何有效作为上下文学习者，推进了对ICL机制的理论理解。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how transformers with softmax attention perform in-context learning on linear classification tasks, motivated by the observation that transformers exhibit strong in-context learning capabilities. The authors construct a class of multi-layer transformers that perform in-context logistic regression, where each layer executes one step of normalized gradient descent on an in-context loss. They demonstrate that such a transformer can be obtained by training a single self-attention layer under gradient descent supervision and then recurrently applying it to form a looped model. Theoretical guarantees for training convergence and out-of-distribution generalization are provided, showing that softmax transformers can effectively act as in-context learners through gradient-based optimization.
该论文研究了带有softmax注意力的Transformer在线性分类任务中如何实现上下文学习，动机源于Transformer表现出强大的上下文学习能力。作者构建了一类多层Transformer，其每层执行一次在上下文损失上的归一化梯度下降，从而实现上下文逻辑回归。他们证明，该Transformer可通过训练单个自注意力层（以梯度下降监督）并反复应用该层形成循环模型获得。论文提供了训练收敛性和分布外泛化性的理论保证，表明softmax Transformer可通过该机制有效充当上下文学习器。
DARTS: Targeting Prognostic Covariates in Budget-Constrained Sequential Experiments
Authors: Kateryna Husar, Alexander Volfovsky
First: 2026-05-07T17:27:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:27:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Randomized controlled trials typically assume that prognostic covariates are known and available at no cost. In practice, obtaining high-dimensional pretreatment data is costly, forcing a trade-off between covariate-adaptive precision and a measurement budget. We introduce Dynamic Adaptive Rerandomization via Thompson Sampling (DARTS), which treats covariate acquisition as a sequential optimization problem embedded within a design-based causal inference task. A budgeted combinatorial Thompson sampler learns which covariates are most prognostic across successive batches; selected covariates then drive rerandomization and regression adjustment to reduce batch-level average treatment effect variance. Our primary theoretical contribution is a decoupling result: adaptive covariate selection based on past batches preserves batch-level randomization validity, and the cumulative inverse-variance weighted estimator achieves at least nominal asymptotic coverage. We further derive a Bayes risk bound for the acquisition layer that matches the minimax lower bound up to logarithmic factors. Empirically, DARTS systematically concentrates the budget on informative features, significantly closing the efficiency gap to oracle designs while maintaining strict inferential validity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DARTS：在预算受限的序贯实验中针对预后协变量的目标优化
随机对照试验通常假设预后协变量已知且获取成本为零。实际上，获取高维基线数据成本高昂，迫使在协变量自适应精度与测量预算之间做出权衡。我们提出动态自适应重随机化方法（DARTS），将协变量获取视为嵌入在基于设计的因果推断任务中的序贯优化问题。一种预算约束的组合式汤普森采样器在连续批次中学习哪些协变量最具预后价值；选定的协变量随后驱动重随机化和回归调整，以降低批次层面的平均处理效应方差。我们的主要理论贡献是解耦结果：基于过往批次的自适应协变量选择可保持批次层面的随机化有效性，且累积逆方差加权估计量至少达到名义渐近覆盖率。我们进一步推导了获取层的贝叶斯风险界，其与最小最大下界在对数因子内一致。实证上，DARTS系统性地将预算集中于信息性特征，显著缩小与理想设计的效率差距，同时保持严格的推断有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost of acquiring high-dimensional prognostic covariates in sequential experiments, the paper proposes DARTS, a method that frames covariate selection as a sequential optimization problem using a budgeted combinatorial Thompson sampler. DARTS dynamically selects the most prognostic covariates across batches to guide rerandomization and regression adjustment, thereby reducing treatment effect variance. Theoretically, it proves that adaptive selection preserves randomization validity and achieves nominal coverage, while its acquisition layer’s Bayes risk bound nearly matches the minimax lower bound. Empirically, DARTS allocates the budget efficiently to informative features, closing the efficiency gap to oracle designs without compromising inferential validity.
为应对现实中获取高维基线协变量成本高昂的问题，本文提出DARTS方法，将协变量选择视为嵌入到设计基础因果推断中的序列优化问题。通过预算约束的组合式汤普森采样器，DARTS在各批次中自适应识别最具预后价值的协变量，并用于指导重随机化和回归调整以降低处理效应方差。理论方面，该方法证明了自适应选择可保持批次级随机化有效性，并通过逆方差加权估计实现名义渐近覆盖率，其获取层的贝叶斯风险界接近最小最大下界（至对数因子）。实证上，DARTS有效将预算集中于信息性特征，显著缩小与理想设计的效率差距，同时保持推断有效性。
AI CFD Scientist: Toward Open-Ended Computational Fluid Dynamics Discovery with Physics-Aware AI Agents
Authors: Nithin Somasekharan, Rabi Pathak, Manushri Dhanakoti, Tingwen Zhang, Ling Yue, Andy Zhu, Shaowu Pan
First: 2026-05-07T17:27:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:27:23+00:00
Comments: 9 main pages and rest in appendix
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent LLM-based agents have closed substantial portions of the scientific discovery loop in software-only machine-learning research, in chemistry, and in biology. Extending the same loop to high-fidelity physical simulators is harder, because solver completion does not imply physical validity and many failure modes appear only in field-level imagery rather than in solver logs. We present AI CFD Scientist, an open-source AI scientist for computational fluid dynamics (CFD) that, to our knowledge, is the first to span literature-grounded ideation, validated execution, vision-based physics verification, source-code modification, and figure-grounded writing within a single inspectable workflow. Three coupled pathways cover parameter sweeps within a fixed solver, case-local C++ library compilation for new physical models, and open-ended hypothesis search against a reference comparator, all running on OpenFOAM through Foam-Agent. At the center of the framework is a vision-language physics-verification gate that inspects rendered flow fields before any result is accepted, rerun, or written into a manuscript. On five tasks under a shared GPT-5.5 backbone, AI CFD Scientist autonomously discovers a Spalart-Allmaras runtime correction that reduces lower-wall Cf RMSE against DNS by 7.89% on the periodic hill at Reh=5600; under matched LLM cost, two strong general AI-scientist baselines (ARIS, DeepScientist) execute partial CFD workflows but lack the domain-specific validity gates needed to convert runs into defensible scientific claims; and a controlled planted-failure ablation shows that the vision-language gate detects 14 of 16 silent failures missed by solver-level checks. Code, prompts, and run artifacts are released at https://github.com/csml-rpi/cfd-scientist.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AI CFD科学家：面向物理感知AI代理的开放式计算流体力学发现
近期基于大语言模型的代理已在纯软件机器学习研究、化学和生物学领域大幅闭合了科学发现循环。将其扩展至高保真物理模拟器则更困难，因为求解器完成并不意味着物理有效性，许多失败模式仅在场级图像中显现而非求解器日志中。我们提出AI CFD科学家，这是首个开源的计算流体力学AI科学家，其工作流涵盖文献基础的创意生成、经验证的执行、基于视觉的物理验证、源代码修改及图示驱动的写作。三个耦合路径覆盖固定求解器内的参数扫描、针对新物理模型的本地C++库编译，以及与参考比较器对抗的开放式假设搜索，均通过Foam-Agent在OpenFOAM上运行。框架核心是一个视觉-语言物理验证门，它在任何结果被接受、重跑或写入论文前检查渲染的流场。在五个共享GPT-5.5架构的任务中，AI CFD科学家自主发现了一种Spalart-Allmaras运行时修正，使在Reh=5600周期山丘上的下壁面Cf RMSE较DNS降低7.89%；在匹配LLM成本下，两个强通用AI科学家基线（ARIS、DeepScientist）仅能执行部分CFD工作流，但缺乏将运行转化为可辩护科学声明所需的领域特定有效性门；受控的植入失败消融实验表明，视觉-语言门检测到16个被求解器级检查遗漏的失败中的14个。代码、提示和运行产物已发布于https://github.com/csml-rpi/cfd-scientist。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate scientific discovery in high-fidelity physics simulations, the authors introduce AI CFD Scientist, an open-source AI agent that integrates literature-based ideation, validated execution, vision-based physics verification, source-code modification, and figure-grounded writing into a single workflow. The system operates via three coupled pathways: parameter sweeps within a fixed solver, case-local C++ library compilation for new models, and open-ended hypothesis search against a reference comparator—all running on OpenFOAM through Foam-Agent. Central to the framework is a vision-language physics verification gate that inspects rendered flow fields before accepting, rerunning, or documenting results. Experiments on five tasks using a shared GPT-5.5 backbone show the agent autonomously discovers a Spalart-Allmaras runtime correction that reduces lower-wall Cf RMSE against DNS by 7.89% on the periodic hill at Reh=5600. 为实现高保真物理模拟中的自动化科学发现，作者提出了AI CFD Scientist，一个开源AI代理，整合了基于文献的创意生成、验证执行、基于视觉的物理验证、源代码修改和图文结合的报告撰写，形成单一可检视工作流。系统通过三条耦合路径运行：固定求解器内的参数扫描、本地C++库编译以支持新物理模型、以及与参考比较器对抗的开放式假设搜索，均通过OpenFOAM和Foam-Agent管理。框架核心是一个视觉-语言物理验证门，用于在结果被接受、重跑或写入论文前检查渲染的流场。在五个共享GPT-5.5骨干的任务中，该代理自主发现了一种Spalart-Allmaras运行时修正，使周期山在Reh=5600下的下壁面Cf RMSE较DNS降低7.89%。与两个强通用AI科学家基线（ARIS、DeepScientist）相比，后者虽能执行部分CFD流程但缺乏领域特定的验证门，无法将运行结果转化为可辩护的科学结论；而控制实验表明，视觉门能检测到16个被求解器级检查遗漏的静默失败中的14个。
Purely Agent-Driven Black-Box Optimization for Biological Design
Authors: Natalie Maus, Yimeng Zeng, Haydn Thomas Jones, Yining Huang, Gaurav Ng Goel, Alden Rose, Kyurae Kim, Hyun-Su Lee, Marcelo Der Torossian Torres, Fangping Wan, Cesar de la Fuente-Nunez, Mark Yatskar, Osbert Bastani, Jacob R. Gardner
First: 2026-01-29T22:45:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:25:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many key challenges in biological design -- such as small-molecule drug discovery, antimicrobial peptide development, and protein engineering -- can be framed as black-box optimization over vast, complex structured spaces. Existing methods rely mainly on raw structural data and struggle to exploit the rich scientific literature. While large language models (LLMs) have been added to these pipelines, they have been confined to narrow roles within structure-centered optimizers. We instead cast biological black-box optimization as an agent-driven, language-based reasoning process. We introduce Purely Agent-driven BLack-box Optimization (PABLO), a hierarchical agentic system that uses scientific LLMs pretrained on chemistry and biology literature to generate and iteratively refine biological candidates. On both the standard GuacaMol molecular design and antimicrobial peptide optimization tasks, PABLO achieves state-of-the-art performance, substantially improving sample efficiency and final objective values over established baselines. Compared to prior optimization methods that incorporate LLMs, PABLO achieves competitive token usage per run despite relying on LLMs throughout the optimization loop. Beyond raw performance, the agentic formulation offers key advantages for realistic design: it naturally incorporates semantic task descriptions, retrieval-augmented domain knowledge, and complex constraints. In follow-up in vitro validation, PABLO-optimized peptides showed strong activity against drug-resistant pathogens, underscoring the practical potential of PABLO for therapeutic discovery.

生物设计中的许多关键挑战——如小分子药物发现、抗菌肽开发和蛋白质工程——均可视为在庞大且结构复杂的黑盒空间中的优化问题。现有方法主要依赖原始结构数据，难以利用丰富的科学文献。虽然大型语言模型（LLMs）已被引入这些流程，但它们仅被限制在以结构为中心的优化器中的狭窄角色。我们转而将生物黑盒优化视为一种基于语言的代理驱动推理过程。我们提出了纯代理驱动的黑盒优化（PABLO），这是一种分层代理系统，利用在化学和生物文献上预训练的科学LLMs生成并迭代优化生物候选物。在标准的GuacaMol分子设计和抗菌肽优化任务中，PABLO均达到最先进性能，显著提升了样本效率和最终目标值，优于现有基线方法。与之前整合LLMs的优化方法相比，PABLO在优化循环中始终依赖LLMs，但仍实现了每轮运行的竞争力token使用量。除了原始性能外，代理式框架还为现实设计提供了关键优势：它自然整合语义任务描述、检索增强领域知识和复杂约束。后续体外验证表明，PABLO优化的肽类对耐药病原体表现出强活性，凸显了PABLO在治疗发现中的实际潜力。
