Motivated by the need to optimize complex biological design tasks like drug discovery and peptide engineering without relying on raw structural data, the authors propose PABLO, a purely agent-driven black-box optimization framework that leverages large language models pretrained on scientific literature to generate and iteratively refine biological candidates. Unlike prior methods that use LLMs only in narrow roles, PABLO treats optimization as a language-based reasoning process, enabling semantic task understanding, retrieval-augmented knowledge, and constraint handling. Experimental results show PABLO outperforms existing baselines on GuacaMol and antimicrobial peptide tasks, achieving state-of-the-art objective values with improved sample efficiency and competitive token usage. In vitro validation further confirms that PABLO-optimized peptides exhibit potent activity against drug-resistant pathogens, demonstrating practical therapeutic potential.
为解决小分子药物发现、抗菌肽开发和蛋白质工程等生物设计任务中在复杂结构空间中的黑盒优化难题，本文提出PABLO框架，该框架完全基于代理驱动，利用在化学与生物学文献上预训练的大语言模型生成并迭代优化生物候选物。与现有仅将LLM用于狭窄角色的方法不同，PABLO将优化视为语言推理过程，自然支持语义任务描述、检索增强领域知识和复杂约束。实验表明，PABLO在GuacaMol和抗菌肽优化任务上达到最先进性能，显著提升样本效率和最终目标值，同时保持每轮运行的token使用量竞争力。体外验证进一步证实，PABLO优化的肽类对耐药病原体具有强活性，凸显其在治疗发现中的实际潜力。
How Many Iterations to Jailbreak? Dynamic Budget Allocation for Multi-Turn LLM Evaluation
Authors: Shai Feldman, Yaniv Romano
First: 2026-05-07T17:25:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:25:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evaluating and predicting the performance of large language models (LLMs) in multi-turn conversational settings is critical yet computationally expensive; key events -- e.g., jailbreaks or successful task completion by an agent -- often emerge only after repeated interactions. These events might be rare, and under any feasible computational budget, remain unobserved. Recent conformal survival frameworks construct reliable lower predictive bounds (LPBs) on the number of iterations to trigger the event of interest, but rely on static budget allocation that is inefficient in multi-turn setups. To address this, we introduce \emph{Dynamic Allocation via PRojected Optimization} (DAPRO), the first theoretically valid dynamic budget allocation framework for bounding the time-to-event in multi-turn LLM interactions. We prove that DAPRO satisfies the budget constraint and provides distribution-free, finite-sample coverage guarantees without requiring the conditional independence between censoring and event times assumed by prior conformal survival approaches. A key theoretical contribution is a novel coverage bound that scales with the square root of the mean censoring weight rather than the worst-case weight, yielding provably tighter guarantees than prior work. Furthermore, DAPRO can be employed to obtain unbiased, low-variance estimates of population-level evaluation metrics, such as the jailbreak rate, under limited computing resources. Comprehensive experiments across agentic task success, adversarial jailbreaks, toxic content generation, and RAG hallucinations using LLMs such as Llama 3.1 and Qwen 2.5 demonstrate that DAPRO consistently achieves coverage closer to the nominal level with lower variance than static baselines, while satisfying the budget constraint.
中文标题/摘要
标题：需要多少次迭代才能越狱？多轮LLM评估中的动态预算分配
评估和预测大型语言模型（LLM）在多轮对话场景中的性能至关重要，但计算成本高昂；关键事件（如越狱或代理成功完成任务）往往需多次交互后才出现，且可能稀有，在任何可行的计算预算下均可能未被观测到。 近期的符合性生存框架可构建对触发目标事件所需迭代次数的可靠下界预测，但依赖静态预算分配，在多轮设置中效率低下。为解决此问题，我们提出\emph{通过投影优化的动态分配}（DAPRO），这是首个在多轮LLM交互中对事件发生时间进行理论有效动态预算分配的框架。 我们证明DAPRO满足预算约束，并在无需假设此前符合性生存方法所依赖的删失与事件时间条件独立性的前提下，提供无分布假设、有限样本覆盖保证。 一个关键理论贡献是提出一种新颖的覆盖界，其随平均删失权重的平方根缩放，而非最坏情况权重，从而比先前工作提供可证明更紧的保证。此外，DAPRO可在计算资源受限条件下，用于获得无偏、低方差的群体级评估指标（如越狱率）估计。 在使用LLM（如Llama 3.1和Qwen 2.5）进行代理任务成功、对抗性越狱、有害内容生成及RAG幻觉的全面实验中，DAPRO始终在满足预算约束的前提下，实现比静态基线更接近名义水平的覆盖度且方差更低。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of static budget allocation in multi-turn LLM evaluations, where rare events like jailbreaks often occur late and remain unobserved under fixed budgets, this work introduces DAPRO, a dynamic budget allocation framework that optimally reallocates computational resources over time. DAPRO provides theoretically valid, distribution-free coverage guarantees without assuming conditional independence between censoring and event times, and achieves tighter bounds by scaling coverage with the square root of mean censoring weight rather than worst-case weight. Experiments across diverse LLM tasks—including jailbreak detection, toxic generation, and RAG hallucinations—show DAPRO consistently delivers coverage closer to nominal levels with lower variance than static baselines while strictly respecting budget constraints.
为解决多轮LLM评估中静态预算分配导致的计算资源浪费问题——罕见事件如越狱常发生在后期且在固定预算下难以观测——本文提出DAPRO动态预算分配框架，通过时间动态重分配计算资源以优化评估效率。DAPRO在不依赖事件与审查独立性假设的前提下，提供分布无关的有限样本覆盖保证，并通过以平均审查权重平方根为尺度实现更紧的置信界。在包括越狱检测、任务成功率、毒性生成和幻觉等多类任务的实验中，DAPRO在满足预算约束的同时，显著优于静态基线方法，实现更接近目标置信水平且方差更低的评估结果。
Patch2Vuln: Agentic Reconstruction of Vulnerabilities from Linux Distribution Binary Patches
Authors: Isaac David, Arthur Gervais
First: 2026-05-07T17:22:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:22:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Security updates create a short but important window in which defenders and attackers can compare vulnerable and patched software. Yet in many operational settings, the most accessible artifacts are binary packages rather than source patches or advisory text. This paper asks whether a language-model agent, restricted to local binary-derived evidence, can reconstruct the security meaning of Linux distribution updates. Patch2Vuln is a local, resumable pipeline that extracts old/new ELF pairs, diffs them with Ghidra and Ghidriff, ranks changed functions, builds candidate dossiers, and asks an offline agent to produce a preliminary audit, bounded validation plan, and final audit. We evaluate Patch2Vuln on 25 Ubuntu `.deb` package pairs: 20 security-update pairs and five negative controls, all manually adjudicated against private source-patch and binary-function ground truth. The agent localizes a verified security-relevant patch function in 10 of 20 security pairs and assigns an accepted final root-cause class in 11 of 20. Oracle diagnostics show that six security pairs fail before model reasoning because the binary differ or ranker omits the right function, with one additional context-export miss. A separate bounded validation pass produces two target-level minimized behavioral old/new differentials, both for tcpdump, but no crash, timeout, sanitizer finding, or memory-corruption proof; all five negative controls are classified as unknown and produce no validation differentials. These results support agentic vulnerability reconstruction from binary patches as a useful research target while showing that binary-diff coverage and local behavioral validation remain the limiting components.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Patch2Vuln：从Linux发行版二进制补丁中通过代理重建漏洞
安全更新创造了一个短暂但重要的窗口，防御者和攻击者可以比较存在漏洞和已修补的软件。然而，在许多实际环境中，最易获取的工件是二进制包，而非源代码补丁或公告文本。本文探讨：受限于本地二进制衍生证据的语言模型代理，能否重建Linux发行版更新的安全含义。Patch2Vuln是一个本地、可恢复的流水线，提取旧/新ELF对，使用Ghidra和Ghidriff进行差异分析，对变更函数进行排序，构建候选档案，并请求离线代理生成初步审计、有界验证计划和最终审计。 我们在25对Ubuntu `.deb`软件包上评估Patch2Vuln：20对安全更新和5个负向对照组，所有结果均与私有源代码补丁和二进制函数真实值人工比对。代理在20对安全更新中定位到10对经验证的安全相关补丁函数，并在20对中为11对分配了被接受的最终根本原因类别。Oracle诊断显示，6对安全更新在模型推理前即失败，因二进制差异或排序器遗漏了正确函数，另1个因上下文导出遗漏。独立的有界验证阶段为tcpdump生成了两个目标级最小化行为差异，但未发现崩溃、超时、内存破坏或Sanitizer检测；5个负向对照组均被分类为未知，未生成任何验证差异。这些结果支持从二进制补丁中通过代理重建漏洞作为有用的研究目标，同时表明二进制差异覆盖和本地行为验证仍是限制性因素。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of analyzing security updates using only binary packages rather than source code or advisories, this paper introduces Patch2Vuln, a local pipeline that uses a language model agent to reconstruct vulnerabilities from Linux binary patches. The method extracts ELF binaries, diffs them with Ghidra, ranks changed functions, builds candidate dossiers, and prompts the agent to generate an audit, validation plan, and final root-cause classification. Evaluated on 25 Ubuntu .deb pairs, the agent correctly localized a verified security-relevant function in 10 of 20 security updates and assigned an accepted root-cause class in 11 of 20. However, six cases failed due to binary diff or ranking errors, and only two behavioral differentials were produced during bounded validation—both for tcpdump—without evidence of crashes or memory corruption. All five negative controls were classified as unknown, highlighting limitations in binary-diff coverage and behavioral validation.
为了解决在缺乏源码补丁或公告文本的情况下，仅通过二进制包分析安全更新的难题，本文研究语言模型代理能否从Linux二进制补丁中重建漏洞信息。Patch2Vuln是一个本地可恢复的流程，提取旧新ELF文件对，使用Ghidra和Ghidriff进行差异分析，对变更函数排序，构建候选档案，并调用离线代理生成初步审计、验证计划和最终审计。在25个Ubuntu .deb包对（20个安全更新，5个负样本）上评估，代理在10/20个安全对中定位到经验证的安全相关函数，11/20个安全对中正确分配了接受的根因类别。Oracle诊断显示6个安全对因二进制差异或排序器遗漏正确函数而失败，另1个因上下文导出缺失。独立的有界验证阶段仅对tcpdump生成了两个目标级最小化行为差异，未发现崩溃、超时、内存破坏等证据；5个负样本均被归类为未知且无验证差异，表明二进制差异覆盖和本地行为验证仍是当前主要瓶颈。
Weight-Decay Turns Transformer Loss Landscapes Villani: Functional-Analytic Foundations for Optimization and Generalization
Authors: Abhijit Das, Sayantan Dutta
First: 2026-05-07T17:22:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:22:19+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Weight decay is widely used as a regularizer in large language models, yet its precise role in shaping Transformer loss landscapes remains theoretically underexplored. This paper provides the first rigorous functional-analytic characterization of the standard Transformer objective--cross-entropy loss with $L^2$ regularization--by proving it satisfies Villani's criteria for coercive energy functions. Specifically, we show that the regularized loss $\mathcal{F}$ is infinitely differentiable, grows at least quadratically, has Gaussian-integrable tails, and satisfies the differential growth condition $-Δ\mathcal{F} + \tfrac{1}{s}\|\nabla\mathcal{F}\|^{2} \to \infty$ as $\|θ\| \to \infty$ for all $s>0$. From this structure, we derive explicit log-Sobolev and Poincaré constants $C_{\mathrm{LS}} \leq λ^{-1} + d/λ^{2}$, linking the regularization strength $λ$ and model dimension $d$ to finite-time convergence guarantees for noisy stochastic gradient descent and PAC-Bayesian generalization bounds that tighten with increasing $λ$. To validate our theory, we introduce a scalable Villani diagnostic $Ψ_s(θ) = -Δ\mathcal{F} + s^{-1}\|\nabla \mathcal{F}\|^2$ and estimate it efficiently using Hutchinson trace probes in models with over 100M parameters. Experiments on GPT-Neo-125M across Penn Treebank and WikiText-103 confirm the predicted quadratic growth of $Ψ_s$, spectral inflation of the Hessian, and exponential convergence behavior consistent with our log-Sobolev analysis. These results demonstrate that weight decay not only improves generalization empirically but also establishes the mathematical conditions required for fast Langevin mixing and theoretically grounded curvature-aware optimization in deep learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：权重衰减重塑Transformer损失景观：Villani函数分析框架下的优化与泛化基础
权重衰减在大型语言模型中广泛用作正则化手段，但其在塑造Transformer损失景观中的精确作用仍缺乏理论探索。本文首次对标准Transformer目标函数——带L²正则化的交叉熵损失——进行严格的函数分析刻画，证明其满足Villani的强能量函数准则。具体而言，我们证明正则化损失F在无穷阶可微、至少二次增长、具有高斯可积尾部，并满足微分增长条件：当||θ||→∞时，-ΔF + (1/s)||∇F||² → ∞（对所有s>0）。由此我们推导出显式的对数Sobolev和Poincaré常数C_LS ≤ λ⁻¹ + d/λ²，将正则化强度λ与模型维度d关联至噪声随机梯度下降的有限时间收敛保证及PAC-Bayesian泛化界，且λ增大时界更紧。为验证理论，我们引入可扩展的Villani诊断量Ψ_s(θ) = -ΔF + s⁻¹||∇F||²，并利用Hutchinson迹探针在超1亿参数模型中高效估计。在GPT-Neo-125M模型上对Penn Treebank与WikiText-103的实验验证了Ψ_s的二次增长、Hessian谱膨胀及与对数Sobolev分析一致的指数收敛行为。结果表明，权重衰减不仅在实证上提升泛化能力，更建立了深度学习中快速Langevin混合与曲率感知优化所需的数学条件。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how weight decay influences Transformer loss landscapes, this paper provides a functional-analytic framework proving that the regularized cross-entropy loss satisfies Villani’s criteria for coercive energy functions, including quadratic growth, Gaussian-integrable tails, and differential growth conditions. The authors derive explicit log-Sobolev and Poincaré constants tied to regularization strength and model dimension, enabling finite-time convergence and generalization bounds. Empirical validation via a scalable Villani diagnostic using Hutchinson trace probes confirms quadratic growth and spectral inflation in large models, aligning with predicted exponential convergence and supporting weight decay’s role in both empirical generalization and theoretical optimization.
为理解权重衰减在Transformer优化中的理论作用，本文首次从泛函分析角度刻画了带L2正则化的交叉熵损失，证明其满足Villani的强能量函数条件，包括无限可微、至少二次增长、高斯可积尾部及无穷远处的微分增长条件，由此推导出显式的对数Sobolev与Poincaré常数，将正则化强度与模型维度关联至收敛性与泛化界。通过在GPT-Neo-125M上使用Hutchinson迹探针高效计算可扩展的Villani诊断量，实验证实该诊断量呈二次增长、Hessian谱膨胀，且收敛行为符合理论预测，表明权重衰减不仅提升泛化能力，也构建了快速Langevin混合与曲率感知优化所需的数学基础。
UniSD: Towards a Unified Self-Distillation Framework for Large Language Models
Authors: Yiqiao Jin, Yiyang Wang, Lucheng Fu, Yijia Xiao, Yinyi Luo, Haoxin Liu, B. Aditya Prakash, Josiah Hester, Jindong Wang, Srijan Kumar
First: 2026-05-07T17:22:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:22:11+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 12 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Self-distillation (SD) offers a promising path for adapting large language models (LLMs) without relying on stronger external teachers. However, SD in autoregressive LLMs remains challenging because self-generated trajectories are free-form, correctness is task-dependent, and plausible rationales can still provide unstable or unreliable supervision. Existing methods mainly examine isolated design choices, leaving their effectiveness, roles, and interactions unclear. In this paper, we propose UniSD, a unified framework to systematically study self-distillation. UniSD integrates complementary mechanisms that address supervision reliability, representation alignment, and training stability, including multi-teacher agreement, EMA teacher stabilization, token-level contrastive learning, feature matching, and divergence clipping. Across six benchmarks and six models from three model families, UniSD reveals when self-distillation improves over static imitation, which components drive the gains, and how these components interact across tasks. Guided by these insights, we construct UniSDfull, an integrated pipeline that combines complementary components and achieves the strongest overall performance, improving over the base model by +5.4 points and the strongest baseline by +2.8 points. Extensive evaluation highlights self-distillation as a practical and steerable approach for efficient LLM adaptation without stronger external teachers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UniSD：面向大语言模型的统一自蒸馏框架
自蒸馏（SD）为无需依赖更强外部教师的大语言模型（LLMs）适配提供了有前景的路径。然而，在自回归LLMs中，SD仍具挑战性，因为自生成轨迹自由形式、正确性依赖任务，且合理推理仍可能提供不稳定或不可靠的监督。现有方法主要考察孤立的设计选择，其有效性、作用及交互关系尚不明确。本文提出UniSD，一个统一框架以系统研究自蒸馏。UniSD整合互补机制，解决监督可靠性、表征对齐与训练稳定性问题，包括多教师一致性、EMA教师稳定化、token级对比学习、特征匹配与发散裁剪。在六项基准与三类模型家族的六种模型上，UniSD揭示了自蒸馏何时优于静态模仿、哪些组件驱动性能提升，以及这些组件在不同任务中的交互方式。基于这些洞见，我们构建UniSDfull，一个整合互补组件的流水线，实现最强整体性能，相较基线模型提升+5.4分，相较最强基线提升+2.8分。广泛评估表明，自蒸馏是无需更强外部教师的高效LLM适配实用且可控的方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to adapt large language models without external teachers, this paper introduces UniSD, a unified self-distillation framework that systematically integrates multiple mechanisms—such as multi-teacher agreement, EMA stabilization, token-level contrastive learning, feature matching, and divergence clipping—to address supervision unreliability, representation misalignment, and training instability. Evaluated across six benchmarks and six models from three families, UniSD identifies which components drive performance gains and how they interact across tasks, revealing that self-distillation outperforms static imitation in many cases. The resulting UniSDfull pipeline, combining all effective components, achieves the best overall results, improving the base model by +5.4 points and the strongest baseline by +2.8 points, demonstrating self-distillation as a practical and steerable adaptation method.
为实现无需外部教师的大语言模型高效适配，本文提出UniSD统一自蒸馏框架，旨在解决自回归模型中自生成轨迹不可靠、监督信号不稳定等核心问题。该框架整合多教师一致性、EMA教师稳定化、词元级对比学习、特征匹配与发散裁剪等互补机制，分别提升监督可靠性、表征对齐与训练稳定性。在六个基准与六种模型（涵盖三类架构）的实验中，结果表明自蒸馏在特定任务下优于静态模仿，且各组件协同作用显著影响性能增益。集成所有有效组件的UniSDfull方案表现最优，相较基线模型提升5.4分，相较最强基线提升2.8分，验证了自蒸馏作为实用且可控的无外部教师适配方法的有效性。
FedAttr: Towards Privacy-preserving Client-Level Attribution in Federated LLM Fine-tuning
Authors: Su Zhang, Junfeng Guo, Heng Huang
First: 2026-05-07T17:21:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:21:02+00:00
Comments: 39 pages, 4 figures, 21 tables (including appendix)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Watermark radioactivity testing type of methods can detect whether a model was trained on watermarked documents, and have become key tools for protecting data ownership in the fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs). Existing works have proved their effectiveness in centralized LLM fine-tuning. However, this type of method faces several challenges and remains underexplored in federated learning (FL), a widely-applied paradigm for fine-tuning LLMs collaboratively on private data across different users. FL mainly ensures privacy through secure aggregation (SA), which allows the server to aggregate updates while keeping clients' updates private. This mechanism preserves privacy but makes it difficult to identify which client trained on watermarked documents. In this work, we propose FedAttr, a new client-level attribution protocol for FL. FedAttr identifies which clients trained on watermarked data via a paired-subset-difference mechanism, while preserving the privacy guarantees of SA and FL performance. FedAttr proceeds in three steps: (i) estimate each client's update by differencing two SA queries, (ii) score the estimate with the watermark detector via differential scoring, and (iii) combine scores across rounds via Stouffer method. We theoretically show that FedAttr produces an unbiased estimator of each client's update with bounded mutual information leakage (i.e., $O(d^*/N)$ per-round update). Moreover, FedAttr empirically achieves 100% TPR and 0% FPR, outperforming all baselines by at least 44.4% in TPR or 19.1% in FPR, with only 6.3% overhead relative to FL training time. Ablation studies confirm that FedAttr is robust to protocol parameters and configurations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FedAttr：面向联邦大语言模型微调的客户端级隐私保护归因方法
水印放射性检测类方法可识别模型是否在水印文档上训练，已成为保护大语言模型（LLM）微调中数据所有权的关键工具。现有方法在集中式LLM微调中已证明有效，但在联邦学习（FL）中仍面临诸多挑战，尚未充分探索。FL主要通过安全聚合（SA）保障隐私，使服务器可聚合更新同时保持客户端更新私密，但该机制难以识别哪些客户端训练了水印文档。本文提出FedAttr，一种FL中的新客户端级归因协议。FedAttr通过配对子集差分机制识别使用水印数据训练的客户端，同时保持SA与FL性能的隐私保障。FedAttr分三步执行：(i) 通过差分两次SA查询估计各客户端更新；(ii) 通过微分评分机制用水印检测器评分估计值；(iii) 用Stouffer方法跨轮次合并评分。理论证明FedAttr对各客户端更新产生无偏估计，且每轮更新的互信息泄漏有界（即$O(d^*/N)$）。实验表明FedAttr达到100% TPR和0% FPR，相较所有基线在TPR上高44.4%或FPR低19.1%，仅增加6.3%的训练时间开销。消融研究证实FedAttr对协议参数和配置鲁棒。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to attribute client-level data usage in federated learning while preserving privacy, FedAttr introduces a novel protocol that identifies which clients trained on watermarked documents without compromising secure aggregation. It operates by estimating each client’s update through differencing two secure aggregation queries, scoring these estimates with a watermark detector, and combining scores across rounds using the Stouffer method. Theoretically, FedAttr ensures an unbiased estimator with bounded mutual information leakage, and empirically achieves perfect true positive rate (100%) and zero false positive rate (0%); it outperforms all baselines by at least 44.4% in TPR or 19.1% in FPR, with only 6.3% overhead over standard federated training.
为在联邦学习中实现客户端级数据归属识别同时保护隐私，FedAttr 提出了一种新协议，通过差分安全聚合查询估计客户端更新，结合水印检测器进行差分评分，并通过 Stouffer 方法聚合多轮评分。理论上，该方法保证估计无偏且信息泄露有界；实验表明其在真实正例率（TPR）上达到100%，假正例率（FPR）为0%，优于所有基线方法至少44.4%（TPR）或19.1%（FPR），仅增加6.3%的训练开销。
Cross-Modal Navigation with Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Shuo Liu, Xinzichen Li, Christopher Amato
First: 2026-05-07T17:20:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:20:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robust embodied navigation relies on complementary sensory cues. However, high-quality and well-aligned multi-modal data is often difficult to obtain in practice. Training a monolithic model is also challenging as rich multi-modal inputs induce complex representations and substantially enlarge the policy space. Cross-modal collaboration among lightweight modality-specialized agents offers a scalable paradigm. It enables flexible deployment and parallel execution, while preserving the strength of each modality. In this paper, we propose \textbf{CRONA}, a Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) framework for \textbf{Cro}ss-Modal \textbf{Na}vigation. CRONA improves collaboration by leveraging control-relevant auxiliary beliefs and a centralized multi-modal critic with global state. Experiments on visual-acoustic navigation tasks show that multi-agent methods significantly improve performance and efficiency over single-agent baselines. We find that homogeneous collaboration with limited modalities is sufficient for short-range navigation under salient cues; heterogeneous collaboration among agents with complementary modalities is generally efficient and effective; and navigation in large, complex environments requires both richer multi-modal perception and increased model capacity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于多智能体强化学习的跨模态导航
稳健的具身导航依赖互补的感官线索。然而，实际中获取高质量且对齐良好的多模态数据往往困难。训练单一模型也颇具挑战，因为丰富的多模态输入会引发复杂表征并显著扩大策略空间。轻量级模态专用智能体间的跨模态协作提供了一种可扩展范式，它支持灵活部署与并行执行，同时保留各模态的优势。本文提出\textbf{CRONA}，一种用于\textbf{Cro}ss-Modal \textbf{Na}vigation的多智能体强化学习（MARL）框架。CRONA通过利用与控制相关的辅助信念和全局状态的集中式多模态批评者，提升协作效果。在视觉-听觉导航任务上的实验表明，多智能体方法在性能和效率上显著优于单智能体基线。我们发现：在显著线索引导下的短程导航中，有限模态的同质协作已足够；智能体间异质协作（互补模态）通常高效且有效；而在大型复杂环境中导航，则需更丰富的多模态感知与更大的模型容量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of obtaining high-quality multi-modal data and the complexity of training monolithic models for embodied navigation, this paper introduces CRONA, a multi-agent reinforcement learning framework that enables cross-modal navigation through lightweight, modality-specialized agents. The method enhances collaboration via control-relevant auxiliary beliefs and a centralized multi-modal critic that uses global state. Experimental results demonstrate that multi-agent approaches outperform single-agent baselines in visual-acoustic navigation tasks, with homogeneous agent collaboration sufficient for short-range navigation under strong cues, heterogeneous collaboration more effective in complex environments, and optimal performance requiring richer modalities and greater model capacity.
为应对高质量多模态数据获取困难及单模型训练复杂度高的问题，本文提出CRONA框架，通过轻量级、模态专用的多智能体强化学习实现跨模态导航。该方法通过控制相关辅助信念与全局状态的集中式多模态评论员提升协作效率。实验表明，多智能体方法在视觉-听觉导航任务中显著优于单智能体基线，同质协作足以应对显著线索下的短程导航，异质协作在复杂环境中更高效，而最优性能需结合更丰富的模态感知与更大的模型容量。
Automated Clinical Report Generation for Remote Cognitive Remediation: Comparing Knowledge-Engineered Templates and LLMs in Low-Resource Settings
Authors: Yongxin Zhou, Fabien Ringeval, François Portet
First: 2026-05-07T17:20:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:20:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The growing demand for cognitive remediation therapy, combined with limited speech therapist availability, has accelerated the adoption of remote rehabilitation tools. These systems generate large volumes of interaction data that are difficult for clinicians to review efficiently. This paper investigates automated clinical report generation for avatar-guided, home-based cognitive remediation sessions in a low-resource setting with no reference reports. We present and compare two approaches: (1) a rule-based template system encoding speech therapy domain knowledge as explicit decision rules and validated templates, ensuring clinical reliability and traceability; and (2) a zero-shot LLM-based approach (GPT-4) aimed at more fluent and concise output. Both systems use identical pre-extracted, expert-validated structured variables, enabling a controlled factual comparison. Outputs were evaluated by eight speech therapists and final-year students using a nine-criterion questionnaire. Results reveal a clear trade-off between clinical reliability and linguistic quality. The template-based system scored higher on fluidity, coherence, and results presentation, while GPT-4 produced more concise output. Directional differences are consistent across evaluation dimensions, though no comparison reached statistical significance after correction, reflecting the scale constraints of expert clinical evaluation. Based on evaluator feedback, we derive eight design recommendations for clinical reporting systems in remote rehabilitation settings. More broadly, this work contributes a replicable methodology combining expert elicitation, taxonomy-driven generation, and multi-dimensional human evaluation for clinical NLG in low-resource settings, and illustrates how controlled comparisons can inform the responsible adoption of generative AI in healthcare.
中文标题/摘要
标题：远程认知康复中自动化临床报告生成：在资源匮乏环境下比较知识工程模板与大语言模型
认知康复治疗需求增长与言语治疗师资源有限的矛盾，加速了远程康复工具的采用。这些系统产生大量交互数据，临床医生难以高效审阅。本文研究在无参考报告的资源匮乏环境下，针对由虚拟形象引导的居家认知康复会话的自动化临床报告生成。我们比较两种方法：(1) 基于规则的模板系统，将言语治疗领域知识编码为显式决策规则和验证模板，确保临床可靠性和可追溯性；(2) 零样本大语言模型（GPT-4）方法，旨在生成更流畅简洁的输出。两种系统均使用相同预提取的、专家验证的结构化变量，实现可控的事实性比较。输出由八名言语治疗师和高年级学生通过九项标准问卷评估。结果揭示临床可靠性和语言质量之间的明显权衡：模板系统在流畅性、连贯性和结果呈现方面得分更高，而GPT-4输出更简洁。各评估维度的方向性差异一致，但经校正后无比较达到统计显著性，反映专家临床评估的规模限制。基于评估反馈，我们提出八条针对远程康复环境中临床报告系统的优化建议。更广泛地说，本研究贡献了一种可复制的方法，结合专家访谈、分类学驱动生成与多维人工评估，用于资源匮乏环境下临床自然语言生成，并说明如何通过受控比较指导生成式AI在医疗领域的负责任采用。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for scalable cognitive remediation in low-resource settings with scarce speech therapists, this study compares two automated clinical report generation methods: a knowledge-engineered rule-based template system and a zero-shot LLM (GPT-4) approach. Both systems process identical structured input variables validated by experts, enabling a controlled comparison. Evaluated by eight speech therapists and students across nine criteria, the template system outperformed GPT-4 in clinical reliability, coherence, and structured presentation, while GPT-4 produced more concise reports. Though directional differences were consistent, no result reached statistical significance due to small sample size and evaluation constraints. The study concludes with eight design recommendations for clinical reporting systems and proposes a replicable methodology combining expert elicitation, taxonomy-driven generation, and multi-dimensional human evaluation for responsible AI adoption in healthcare.
为应对低资源环境下认知康复治疗需求增长与言语治疗师短缺的矛盾，本研究对比了两种自动化临床报告生成方法：基于领域知识的规则模板系统与零样本LLM（GPT-4）方法。二者使用相同的专家验证结构化输入变量，确保可比性。经八位言语治疗师与学生在九项指标上评估，模板系统在临床可靠性、连贯性与结构化呈现方面表现更优，而GPT-4输出更简洁。虽因样本量小未达统计显著性，但各维度趋势一致，据此提出八项系统设计建议。该研究构建了结合专家访谈、分类驱动生成与多维人工评估的可复现方法，为医疗领域负责任地采用生成式AI提供参考。
ReActor: Reinforcement Learning for Physics-Aware Motion Retargeting
Authors: David Müller, Agon Serifi, Sammy Christen, Ruben Grandia, Espen Knoop, Moritz Bächer
Venue: SIGGRAPH 2026
First: 2026-05-07T17:20:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:20:15+00:00
Comments: SIGGRAPH 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retargeting human kinematic reference motion onto a robot's morphology remains a formidable challenge. Existing methods often produce physical inconsistencies, such as foot sliding, self-collisions, or dynamically infeasible motions, which hinder downstream imitation learning. We propose a bilevel optimization framework that jointly adapts reference motions to a robot's morphology while training a tracking policy using reinforcement learning. To make the optimization tractable, we derive an approximate gradient for the upper-level loss. Our framework requires only a sparse set of semantic rigid-body correspondences and eliminates the need for manual tuning by identifying optimal values for a parameterization expressive enough to preserve characteristic motion across different embodiments. Moreover, by integrating retargeting directly with physics simulation, we produce physically plausible motions that facilitate robust imitation learning. We validate our method in simulation and on hardware, demonstrating challenging motions for morphologies that differ significantly from a human, including retargeting onto a quadruped.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReActor：面向物理感知运动重定向的强化学习
将人类运动学参考动作重定向到机器人形态仍是一项艰巨挑战。现有方法常产生物理不一致现象，如脚部滑动、自碰撞或动态不可行运动，阻碍下游模仿学习。我们提出一种双层优化框架，联合适应参考动作至机器人形态，并通过强化学习训练跟踪策略。为使优化可行，我们推导出上层损失的近似梯度。该框架仅需稀疏语义刚体对应关系，无需手动调参，通过识别足够表达不同形态间特征运动的参数化最优值实现。此外，通过将重定向直接集成到物理仿真中，我们生成物理合理运动，促进稳健模仿学习。我们在仿真与硬件上验证方法，展示了针对与人类形态显著不同的机器人（包括四足机器人）的挑战性动作重定向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to adapt human motion to robots without physical inconsistencies, this work introduces ReActor, a bilevel optimization framework that jointly retargets reference motions and trains a reinforcement learning policy. The method uses an approximate gradient for the upper-level loss to enable tractable optimization, requiring only sparse semantic correspondences and eliminating manual tuning. Experiments in simulation and on real hardware show that ReActor generates physically plausible motions even for non-human morphologies like quadrupeds, significantly improving the feasibility of downstream imitation learning.
为解决将人类运动参考迁移到机器人形态时出现的物理不一致问题，作者提出ReActor框架，通过双层优化联合运动重定向与强化学习策略训练。该方法通过近似梯度使上层损失可优化，仅需稀疏语义刚体对应关系，自动优化参数化以保留运动特征，无需手动调参。实验表明，该方法在仿真与真实硬件上均能生成符合物理规律的运动，适用于形态差异显著的机器人（如四足机器人），有效支持稳健的模仿学习。
DINORANKCLIP: DINOv3 Distillation and Injection for Vision-Language Pretraining with High-Order Ranking Consistency
Authors: Shuyang Jiang, Nan Yu, Yiming Zhang, Zenghui Ding, Zhenyu Wu
First: 2026-05-07T17:19:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:19:52+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 7 figures, 9 tables. Code will be made publicly available upon acceptance
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Contrastive language-image pretraining (CLIP) suffers from two structural weaknesses: the symmetric InfoNCE loss discards the relative ordering among unmatched in-batch pairs, and global pooling collapses the visual representation into a semantic bottleneck that is poorly sensitive to fine-grained local structure. RANKCLIP partially addresses the first issue with a list-wise Plackett-Luce ranking-consistency loss, but its model is strictly first-order and inherits the second weakness untouched. We propose DINORANKCLIP, a pretraining framework that addresses both jointly. Our principal contribution is injecting a frozen DINOv3 teacher into the contrastive trunk through a dual-branch lightweight student and a multi-scale fusion module with channel-spatial attention, a self-attention refiner, and a conflict-aware gate that preserves the cross-modal alignment up to first order. Complementarily, we introduce a high-order Plackett-Luce ranking model in which the per-position utility is augmented with attention-parameterised pairwise and tuple-wise transition terms; the family contains CLIP and RANKCLIP as nested zero-order and first-order special cases, and the optimal order on every benchmark is $R^*=3$. The full empirical study -- order sweep, Fine-grained Probe on five datasets, four-node Modality-Gap analysis, six-variant Fusion ablation -- fits in 72 hours on a single eight-GPU H100 node and trains entirely on Conceptual Captions 3M. DINORANKCLIP consistently outperforms CLIP, CyCLIP, ALIP, and RANKCLIP under matched compute, with the largest relative gains on the fine-grained and out-of-distribution evaluations that most directly stress local structural reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DINORANKCLIP：基于高阶排序一致性的DINOv3蒸馏与注入视觉-语言预训练框架
对比语言-图像预训练（CLIP）存在两个结构性缺陷：对称的InfoNCE损失忽略了批次内不匹配样本间的相对排序，全局池化将视觉表示压缩为语义瓶颈，难以感知细粒度局部结构。RANKCLIP通过列表式Plackett-Luce排序一致性损失部分缓解第一个问题，但其模型严格为一阶，未解决第二个缺陷。我们提出DINORANKCLIP框架，联合解决上述两个问题。主要贡献是通过双分支轻量学生模型与多尺度融合模块（含通道-空间注意力、自注意力精炼器及冲突感知门控）将冻结的DINOv3教师模型注入对比主干，保持跨模态对齐至一阶。此外，我们引入高阶Plackett-Luce排序模型，每位置效用叠加注意力参数化的成对与元组过渡项；该模型包含CLIP（零阶）与RANKCLIP（一阶）作为嵌套特例，且各基准最优排序均为R*=3。完整实证研究（排序扫描、五数据集细粒度探针、四节点模态差距分析、六种融合消融）可在单个八卡H100节点72小时内完成，且完全基于Conceptual Captions 3M训练。DINORANKCLIP在同等计算资源下持续超越CLIP、CyCLIP、ALIP与RANKCLIP，尤其在细粒度与分布外评估中相对增益最大，直接考验局部结构推理能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by CLIP's structural limitations—namely, its symmetric loss ignoring relative pair rankings and global pooling losing fine-grained visual detail—this work proposes DINORANKCLIP, a vision-language pretraining framework that jointly addresses both issues. It injects a frozen DINOv3 teacher into a lightweight dual-branch student via a multi-scale fusion module incorporating channel-spatial attention, self-attention refinement, and a conflict-aware gate to preserve cross-modal alignment. Additionally, it introduces a high-order Plackett-Luce ranking loss that extends beyond first-order by modeling pairwise and tuple-wise transitions, with optimal performance observed at order three. Experiments across five datasets, ablation studies, and modality-gap analyses show DINORANKCLIP consistently outperforms CLIP, CyCLIP, ALIP, and RANKCLIP, especially on fine-grained and out-of-distribution tasks that demand local structural reasoning.
为解决CLIP在对比学习中忽略未匹配样本相对排序及全局池化导致的细粒度视觉结构丢失问题，DINORANKCLIP通过双分支轻量学生模型与多尺度融合模块（含通道空间注意力、自注意力精炼及冲突感知门控）注入冻结的DINOv3教师模型，保持跨模态对齐。同时引入高阶Plackett-Luce排序损失，通过注意力加权的成对与元组过渡项扩展至三阶，优于零阶（CLIP）与一阶（RANKCLIP）模型。在Conceptual Captions 3M数据集上，DINORANKCLIP在细粒度与分布外评估中显著超越CLIP、CyCLIP、ALIP及RANKCLIP，尤其在依赖局部结构推理的任务中表现最优。
BRICKS: Compositional Neural Markov Kernels for Zero-Shot Radiation-Matter Simulation
Authors: Richard Hildebrandt, Evangelos Kourlitis, Baran Hashemi, Manuel Bünstorf, Thierry Meyer, Nikola Boskov, Michael Kagan, Dan Rosenbaum, Sanmay Ganguly, Lukas Heinrich
First: 2026-05-07T17:19:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:19:31+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce a new strategy for compositional neural surrogates for radiation-matter interactions, a key task spanning domains from particle physics through nuclear and space engineering to medical physics. Exploiting the locality and the Markov nature of particle interactions, we create a \emph{next-particle prediction} kernel using hybrid discrete-continuous transformer models based on Riemannian Flow Matching on product manifolds. The model generates variable-sized typed sets of particles and radiation side effects that are the result of the interaction of an incident particle with a material volume. The resulting kernel can be composed to simulate unseen large-scale material distributions in a zero-shot manner. Unlike mechanistic simulators, our model is designed to be differentiable, provides tractable likelihoods for future downstream applications. A significant computational speed-up on GPU compared to CPU-bound mechanistic simulation is observed for single-kernel execution. We evaluate the model at the kernel level and demonstrate predictive stability over multi-round autoregressive rollouts. We additionally release a novel 20M-event radiation-matter interaction dataset for further research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BRICKS：用于零样本辐射-物质模拟的组合神经马尔可夫核
我们提出了一种新的组合神经代理策略，用于模拟辐射-物质相互作用，这是从粒子物理到核工程、空间工程及医学物理等多个领域的关键任务。利用粒子相互作用的局部性和马尔可夫特性，我们基于乘积流形上的黎曼流匹配构建了混合离散-连续变换器模型，以实现\emph{下一粒子预测}核。该模型可生成不同大小、类型化的粒子集合及辐射副作用，这些是入射粒子与材料体积相互作用的结果。所生成的核可组合用于零样本模拟未见过的大规模材料分布。与机制模拟器不同，我们的模型设计为可微分，且能提供便于下游应用的可处理似然。在单核执行时，与CPU绑定的机制模拟相比，GPU上实现了显著的计算加速。我们在核级别评估该模型，并展示了多轮自回归推演中的预测稳定性。此外，我们还发布了一个包含2000万事件的新型辐射-物质相互作用数据集，供进一步研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, differentiable, and zero-shot simulation of radiation-matter interactions across diverse engineering and physics domains, the authors propose BRICKS, a compositional neural Markov kernel using hybrid discrete-continuous transformers based on Riemannian Flow Matching. The model predicts next-particle states and radiation side effects in an autoregressive manner, generating variable-sized particle sets from incident particles interacting with material volumes. Experimental results show the kernel achieves significant GPU-accelerated speedup over CPU-based mechanistic simulators, maintains predictive stability across multi-round rollouts, and enables zero-shot simulation of unseen material distributions. The authors also release a 20M-event dataset to support further research.
为解决辐射-物质相互作用在粒子物理、核工程、航天工程及医学物理等领域中对高效、可微分且零样本模拟的需求，作者提出BRICKS：一种基于黎曼流匹配的混合离散-连续Transformer的组合神经马尔可夫核。该模型以马尔可夫局部性为基础，预测下一粒子状态及辐射效应，生成可变大小的粒子集合。实验表明，该核在GPU上显著优于CPU依赖的机制模拟器，多轮自回归推演中保持预测稳定性，并在核级层面验证有效。作者同时发布一个2000万事件的辐射-物质相互作用数据集以支持后续研究。
Towards Metric-Faithful Neural Graph Matching
Authors: Jyotirmaya Shivottam, Subhankar Mishra
First: 2026-05-07T17:16:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:16:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Graph Edit Distance (GED) is a fundamental, albeit NP-hard, metric for structural graph similarity. Recent neural graph matching architectures approximate GED by first encoding graphs with a Graph Neural Network (GNN) and then applying either a graph-level regression head or a matching-based alignment module. Despite substantial architectural progress, the role of encoder geometry in neural GED estimation remains poorly understood. In this paper, we develop a theoretical framework that connects encoder geometry to GED estimation quality for two broad classes of neural GED estimators: graph similarity predictors and alignment-based methods. On fixed graph collections, where the doubly-stochastic metric $d_{\mathrm{DS}}$ is comparable to GED, we show that graph-level bi-Lipschitz encoders yield controlled GED surrogates and improved ranking stability; for matching-based estimators, node-level bi-Lipschitz geometry propagates to encoder-induced alignment costs and the resulting optimized alignment objective. We instantiate this perspective using FSW-GNN, a bi-Lipschitz WL-equivalent encoder, as a drop-in replacement in representative neural GED architectures. Across representative baselines and benchmark datasets, the resulting geometry-aware variants significantly improve GED prediction and ranking metrics. A faithfulness case study of untrained encoders, together with ablations and transfer experiments, supports the view that these gains arise from improved representation geometry, positioning encoder geometry as a useful design principle for neural graph matching.
中文标题/摘要
标题：迈向度量忠实的神经图匹配
图编辑距离（GED）是衡量结构图相似性的基本且NP难的度量。近期的神经图匹配架构通过先用图神经网络（GNN）编码图，再应用图级回归头或基于匹配的对齐模块来近似GED。尽管架构上取得了显著进展，但编码器几何结构在神经GED估计中的作用仍不明确。本文构建了一个理论框架，将编码器几何结构与两类神经GED估计器（图相似性预测器和基于对齐的方法）的GED估计质量联系起来。在固定图集合上，其中双随机度量$d_{\mathrm{DS}}$与GED相当，我们证明图级双利普希茨编码器可产生可控的GED替代量并提升排序稳定性；对于基于匹配的估计器，节点级双利普希茨几何结构会传播至编码器诱导的对齐代价，从而形成优化的对齐目标。我们以FSW-GNN（双利普希茨WL等价编码器）为例，将其作为即插即用模块替换代表性神经GED架构中的编码器。在多个基准数据集和基线模型上，所得几何感知变体显著提升了GED预测和排序指标。对未训练编码器的忠实性案例研究，结合消融实验和迁移实验，支持了这些改进源于更优的表示几何结构的观点，从而将编码器几何结构确立为神经图匹配的有用设计原则。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how encoder geometry affects neural graph matching performance, this paper develops a theoretical framework linking encoder properties to Graph Edit Distance (GED) estimation quality. It analyzes two major neural GED approaches—graph similarity predictors and alignment-based methods—and demonstrates that bi-Lipschitz encoders yield more stable and faithful GED approximations. The study evaluates these insights using FSW-GNN, a bi-Lipschitz Weisfeiler-Lehman equivalent encoder, as a plug-in replacement in existing architectures. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks show consistent improvements in GED prediction accuracy and ranking stability, with ablation and transfer studies confirming that geometry-aware encoders enhance representation quality, making encoder geometry a key design consideration for neural graph matching.
鉴于现有神经图匹配方法中编码器几何结构对图编辑距离（GED）估计影响尚不明确，本文构建了一个理论框架，将编码器几何特性与GED估计质量关联起来，分析了两类神经GED估计器——图相似性预测器和基于匹配的方法。研究发现，双Lipschitz编码器能产生更稳定、更忠实的GED近似值。作者将FSW-GNN（一种双Lipschitz Weisfeiler-Lehman等价编码器）作为替换模块嵌入代表性架构中，实验表明其在多个基准数据集上显著提升GED预测精度和排序稳定性，消融与迁移实验进一步证实，性能提升源于编码器几何表示质量的改善。
How Fast Should a Model Commit to Supervision? Training Reasoning Models on the Tsallis Loss Continuum
Authors: Chu-Cheng Lin, Eugene Ie
First: 2026-04-28T17:52:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:16:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
SFT-then-RLVR is widely used for post-training reasoning models, but why this specific ordering, and why RLVR-only stalls at cold start, have lacked a unifying theoretical account. We provide that account under a unified loss family $J_Q$ using the Tsallis $q$-logarithm. $J_Q$ is a single-parameter family that interpolates between RLVR (at $q{=}0$, the \textit{exploitation pole}) and the log-marginal-likelihood over latent trajectories (at $q{=}1$, the \textit{density-estimation pole}), under which the standard pipeline corresponds to a stepwise $q{=}1 \to 0$ schedule. All members share the same per-example gradient direction, differing only by a per-instance amplification $P_θ^{-q}$ that reweights each instance independently of the learning rate. Under gradient flow analysis, we show that the exploitation pole requires $Ω(\frac{1}{p_0})$ time to escape cold start but is robust to label noise, while the density-estimation pole escapes in $Θ\big(\log(\frac{1}{p_0})\big)$ but memorizes label noise. This separation explains how SFT ($q{=}1$) first moves the model out of the cold-start regime, followed by the more robust RLVR ($q{=}0$), under the SFT-then-RLVR paradigm. We further derive two Monte Carlo estimators that directly optimize fixed-$q$ on the $J_Q$ continuum, without annotated rationales: Gradient-Amplified RL (GARL) and Posterior-Attenuated Fine-Tuning (PAFT), with shared bias $O\big(\frac{q}{M P_θ^q}\big)$ but different variance and stability properties. On FinQA, HotPotQA, and MuSiQue, GARL at sufficiently high $q$ substantially mitigates cold-start stalling, escaping cold start where GRPO fails entirely. In warm start, GARL at low $q$ dominates FinQA where training is stable; on HotPotQA and MuSiQue, GARL destabilizes and PAFT at $q{=}0.75$ remains stable, reaching $47.9$ \texttt{m@16} on HotPotQA ($+13.9$ over GRPO).
中文标题/摘要
标题：模型应在何时承诺监督？在Tsallis损失连续体上训练推理模型
SFT后接RLVR是推理模型后训练的常用方法，但为何采用此特定顺序，以及为何仅RLVR在冷启动时停滞，此前缺乏统一的理论解释。我们通过Tsallis q-对数定义的统一损失族J_Q提供该解释。J_Q是一个单参数族，在q=0（即“利用极点”）时对应RLVR，在q=1（即“密度估计极点”）时对应对潜在轨迹的对数边际似然。标准流程对应于q=1到q=0的分步调度。所有成员具有相同的每样本梯度方向，仅在每实例的放大因子P_θ^{-q}上不同，该因子独立于学习率重新加权每个实例。通过梯度流分析，我们表明利用极点需Ω(1/p_0)时间才能摆脱冷启动，但对标签噪声鲁棒；而密度估计极点可在Θ(log(1/p_0))时间内摆脱冷启动，但会记忆标签噪声。这种分离解释了SFT（q=1）先将模型移出冷启动区域，随后更鲁棒的RLVR（q=0）在SFT后接RLVR范式中的作用。我们进一步推导出两种蒙特卡洛估计器，可在J_Q连续体上直接优化固定q值，且无需标注推理：梯度放大强化学习（GARL）和后验衰减微调（PAFT），二者共享偏置O(q/(M P_θ^q))，但方差与稳定性特性不同。在FinQA、HotPotQA和MuSiQue数据集上，GARL在足够高的q值下显著缓解冷启动停滞，甚至在GRPO完全失败的场景中成功摆脱冷启动。在暖启动时，GARL在低q值下主导FinQA（训练稳定）；在HotPotQA和MuSiQue上，GARL不稳定，而PAFT在q=0.75时保持稳定，在HotPotQA上达到47.9 m@16（比GRPO高13.9）
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates why the SFT-then-RLVR training pipeline is effective for reasoning models, proposing a theoretical framework using the Tsallis $q$-logarithm to unify RLVR and supervised fine-tuning under a single loss family $J_Q$. By varying $q$ from 1 (density-estimation pole) to 0 (exploitation pole), the authors show that SFT first escapes cold start quickly but risks memorizing noise, while RLVR is robust to noise but requires longer to escape cold start. They derive two new methods, GARL and PAFT, that optimize fixed $q$ values without annotated rationales, and find that GARL at high $q$ avoids cold-start stalling on FinQA, HotPotQA, and MuSiQue, while at low $q$ it performs best on FinQA in warm start. On HotPotQA, PAFT at $q=0.75$ achieves 47.9 m@16, outperforming GRPO by 13.9 points.
该研究探讨了为何SFT后接RLVR的训练范式对推理模型有效，提出基于Tsallis $q$-对数的统一损失函数$J_Q$，将RLVR与监督微调统一于一个参数连续体中。当$q$从1（密度估计极点）降至0（探索极点）时，SFT能快速跳出冷启动但易记忆噪声，而RLVR抗噪但需更长时间跳出冷启动。作者推导出两种无需标注推理过程的新方法GARL和PAFT，分别在高$q$时避免冷启动停滞（如FinQA），在低$q$时在FinQA暖启动下表现最佳；在HotPotQA上，PAFT在$q=0.75$时稳定达到47.9 m@16，比GRPO高出13.9分。
Generative AI Meets 6G and Beyond: Diffusion Models for Semantic Communications
Authors: Hai-Long Qin, Jincheng Dai, Guo Lu, Shuo Shao, Sixian Wang, Tongda Xu, Wenjun Zhang, Ping Zhang, Khaled B. Letaief
First: 2025-11-11T16:27:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:16:30+00:00
Comments: Accepted by IEEE COMST, GitHub repository: https://github.com/qin-jingyun/Awesome-DiffComm, project page: https://qin-jingyun.github.io/Awesome-DiffComm
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Semantic communications mark a paradigm shift from bit-accurate transmission toward meaning-centric communication, essential as wireless systems approach theoretical capacity limits. The emergence of generative AI has catalyzed generative semantic communications, where receivers reconstruct content from minimal semantic cues by leveraging learned priors. Among generative approaches, diffusion models stand out for their superior generation quality, stable training dynamics, and rigorous theoretical foundations. However, the field currently lacks systematic guidance connecting diffusion techniques to communication system design, forcing researchers to navigate disparate literatures. This article provides the first comprehensive tutorial on diffusion models for generative semantic communications. We present score-based diffusion foundations and systematically review three technical pillars: conditional diffusion for controllable generation, efficient diffusion for accelerated inference, and generalized diffusion for cross-domain adaptation. In addition, we introduce an inverse problem perspective that reformulates semantic decoding as posterior inference, bridging semantic communications with computational imaging. Through analysis of human-centric, machine-centric, and agent-centric scenarios, we illustrate how diffusion models enable extreme compression while maintaining semantic fidelity and robustness. By bridging generative AI innovations with communication system design, this article aims to establish diffusion models as foundational components of next-generation wireless networks and beyond.
中文标题/摘要
标题：生成式人工智能与6G及更远：扩散模型在语义通信中的应用
语义通信标志着从比特精确传输向以意义为中心的通信范式转变，这是无线系统接近理论容量极限时的必然需求。生成式人工智能的兴起催生了生成式语义通信，其中接收端通过利用学习到的先验知识，从极少量语义线索中重建内容。在生成式方法中，扩散模型因其卓越的生成质量、稳定的训练动态和严谨的理论基础而脱颖而出。然而，当前该领域缺乏将扩散技术与通信系统设计系统性关联的指导，迫使研究者在分散的文献中摸索前行。本文首次全面系统地介绍了扩散模型在生成式语义通信中的应用。我们阐述了基于分数的扩散模型基础，并系统性回顾了三大技术支柱：条件扩散以实现可控生成、高效扩散以加速推理、通用扩散以实现跨域适应。此外，我们引入逆问题视角，将语义解码重新表述为后验推断，从而弥合语义通信与计算成像之间的鸿沟。通过分析以人为中心、以机器为中心和以智能体为中心的场景，我们展示了扩散模型如何在保持语义保真度和鲁棒性的同时实现极高压缩。通过将生成式人工智能创新与通信系统设计相结合，本文旨在确立扩散模型作为下一代无线网络及更远发展的基础组件。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to transcend bit-accurate transmission as wireless systems near capacity limits, this work explores diffusion models as a core technology for generative semantic communications. It systematically introduces score-based diffusion foundations and reviews three key technical pillars: conditional diffusion for controllable generation, efficient diffusion for faster inference, and generalized diffusion for cross-domain adaptation. The paper also frames semantic decoding as an inverse problem, linking it to computational imaging. Experimental analysis across human, machine, and agent-centric scenarios demonstrates that diffusion models enable extreme compression while preserving semantic fidelity and robustness, positioning them as foundational for 6G and beyond.
为突破无线系统逼近容量极限时比特精确传输的局限，本文将扩散模型确立为语义通信的核心技术。系统性介绍了基于分数的扩散基础，并梳理了三大关键技术支柱：条件扩散实现可控生成、高效扩散加速推理、通用扩散支持跨域适配。同时，将语义解码重构为逆问题，与计算成像建立联系。在人、机、智能体为中心的场景分析中，实验证明扩散模型可在极端压缩下保持语义保真度与鲁棒性，有望成为下一代无线网络的基础架构。
The ART of Composition: Attention-Regularized Training for Compositional Visual Grounding
Authors: Jiayun Luo, Mir Rayat Imtiaz Hossain, Pritam Sarkar, Boyang Li, Leonid Sigal
First: 2024-12-11T05:36:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:14:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance on implicit and explicit visual grounding and related tasks. However, such abilities are generally tested on simple, single-object phrases. We find that grounding performance degrades for complex, multi-object references. These limitations largely arise from training objectives that leverage image-caption alignment, where direct multi-object references are rare, the number of possible such references is theoretically large (exponential in the number of objects), and attribution is difficult. To address this, without requiring any additional annotations, we propose Compositional Attention-Regularized Training (CompART), which decomposes captions into object-centric phrases and constructs composite phrases by pairing them with conjunctions. We then introduce a composition loss that encourages the attention induced by a composite phrase to equal the sum of the attentions of its constituent phrases, promoting balanced multi-object localization. We evaluate CompART across four VLM architectures, spanning both contrastive-based and generative-based models, on four benchmarks for multi-object grounding and two VQA benchmarks for general visual understanding. CompART consistently improves grounding for both single- and multi-object references across diverse VLM architectures and datasets, and further demonstrates enhanced visual understanding, as evidenced by gains on VQA, despite not being explicitly trained for this task.
中文标题/摘要
标题：组合视觉定位的注意力正则化训练艺术
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在隐式和显式的视觉定位及相关任务中表现优异，但其能力通常仅在简单、单物体短语上进行测试。我们发现，对于复杂、多物体的参考短语，定位性能会下降。这些局限主要源于训练目标依赖图像-文本对齐，其中直接的多物体参考罕见，可能的此类参考数量理论上巨大（呈物体数量的指数级增长），且难以进行归因。为解决此问题，无需任何额外标注，我们提出组合注意力正则化训练（CompART），将图像描述分解为以物体为中心的短语，并通过搭配连词构建复合短语。随后引入组合损失，鼓励复合短语诱导的注意力等于其组成短语注意力之和，从而促进多物体定位的均衡性。我们在四种VLM架构（涵盖基于对比和生成的模型）上评估CompART，涉及四个多物体定位基准和两个通用视觉理解的VQA基准。CompART在多种VLM架构和数据集上，对单物体和多物体参考均持续提升定位性能，并进一步在VQA任务上展现出增强的视觉理解能力，尽管未明确为此任务进行训练。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the degradation of visual grounding performance on complex multi-object references due to training objectives that favor simple image-caption alignments, the authors propose CompART, a method that decomposes captions into object-centric phrases and composes them with conjunctions to simulate multi-object references without additional annotations. It introduces a composition loss that enforces attention alignment between composite phrases and the sum of their constituent attentions, promoting balanced localization. Evaluated across four VLM architectures and multiple benchmarks, CompART consistently improves grounding for both single- and multi-object references and also enhances general visual understanding on VQA tasks without explicit training for them.
鉴于视觉语言模型在单物体视觉定位任务上表现优异，但在多物体定位任务上性能下降，作者提出CompART方法，通过将图像描述分解为物体中心短语并组合成复合短语来模拟复杂参照。该方法引入组合损失，强制复合短语的注意力分布等于其组成部分注意力之和，以促进多物体的均衡定位。在四种视觉语言模型架构和六个基准数据集上的实验表明，CompART显著提升了单物体和多物体定位精度，同时在视觉问答任务上也增强了模型的通用视觉理解能力，而无需专门训练。
Distributionally-Robust Learning to Optimize
Authors: Vinit Ranjan, Jisun Park, Bartolomeo Stellato
First: 2026-05-07T17:14:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:14:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose a distributionally robust approach to learning hyperparameters for first-order methods in convex optimization. Given a dataset of problem instances, we minimize a Wasserstein distributionally robust version of the performance estimation problem (PEP) over algorithm parameters such as step sizes. Our framework unifies two extremes: as the robustness radius vanishes, we recover classical learning to optimize (L2O); as it grows, we recover worst-case optimal algorithm design via PEP. We solve the resulting problem with stochastic gradient descent, differentiating through the solution of an inner semidefinite program at each step. We prove high-probability bounds showing that the true risk of the learned algorithm is at most the in-sample L2O optimum plus a slack that shrinks with the sample size, and is no worse than the worst-case PEP bound. On unconstrained quadratic minimization, LASSO, and linear programming benchmarks, our learned algorithms achieve strong out-of-sample performance with certifiable robustness, outperforming both worst-case optimal and vanilla L2O baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分布鲁棒学习优化
我们提出了一种分布鲁棒方法，用于学习凸优化中一阶方法的超参数。给定一组问题实例数据，我们在算法参数（如步长）上最小化性能估计问题（PEP）的Wasserstein分布鲁棒版本。我们的框架统一了两个极端：当鲁棒半径趋于零时，恢复经典学习优化（L2O）；当半径增大时，恢复通过PEP实现的最坏情况最优算法设计。我们使用随机梯度下降求解该问题，并在每一步对内嵌半定规划的解进行自动微分。我们证明了高概率界，表明所学习算法的真实风险不超过样本内L2O最优值加上随样本量递减的松弛项，且不劣于最坏情况PEP界。在无约束二次最小化、LASSO和线性规划基准测试中，我们的学习算法在具有可验证鲁棒性的前提下，表现出优异的样本外性能，优于最坏情况最优和普通L2O基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to balance empirical performance and worst-case robustness in learning optimization hyperparameters, this work introduces a distributionally robust framework that minimizes a Wasserstein-regularized performance estimation problem over algorithm parameters like step sizes. The method unifies classical learning-to-optimize and worst-case PEP-based design by adjusting the robustness radius, and employs stochastic gradient descent with differentiable semidefinite programming to solve the optimization. Experimental results on quadratic minimization, LASSO, and linear programming show that the learned algorithms achieve strong out-of-sample performance with provable robustness, consistently outperforming both vanilla L2O and worst-case optimal baselines.
为在学习优化超参数时兼顾经验性能与鲁棒性，本文提出一种分布鲁棒框架，通过最小化Wasserstein正则化的性能估计问题来优化如步长等算法参数。该方法通过调节鲁棒半径，统一了经典学习优化与最坏情况PEP设计，采用随机梯度下降并结合内层半定规划的梯度计算。在无约束二次规划、LASSO和线性规划基准测试中，所学算法展现出强的样本外性能与可验证鲁棒性，优于最坏情况最优和普通L2O基线方法。
NeuroAgent: LLM Agents for Multimodal Neuroimaging Analysis and Research
Authors: Lujia Zhong, Yihao Xia, Jianwei Zhang, Shuo huang, Jiaxin Yue, Mingyang Xia, Yonggang Shi
First: 2026-05-07T17:13:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:13:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal neuroimaging analysis often involves complex, modality-specific preprocessing workflows that require careful configuration, quality control, and coordination across heterogeneous toolchains. Beyond preprocessing, downstream statistical analysis and disease classification commonly require task-specific code, evaluation protocols, and data-format conventions, creating additional barriers between raw acquisitions and reproducible scientific analysis. We present NeuroAgent, an LLM-driven agentic framework that automates key preprocessing and analysis steps for heterogeneous neuroimaging data, including sMRI, fMRI, dMRI, and PET, and supports interactive downstream analysis through natural-language queries. NeuroAgent employs a hierarchical multi-agent architecture with a feedback-driven Generate-Execute-Validate engine: agents autonomously generate executable preprocessing code, detect and recover from runtime errors, and validate output integrity. We evaluate the system on 1,470 subjects pooled across all ADNI phases (CN=1,000, AD=470), where all subjects have sMRI and tabular data, with subsets also having Tau-PET (n=469), fMRI (n=278), and DTI ($n=620$). Pipeline ablation studies across multiple LLM backends show that capable models reach up to 100% intent-parsing accuracy, with the strongest backend (Qwen3.5-27B) reaching 84.8% end-to-end preprocessing step correctness. Automated recovery limits manual intervention to edge cases where human review is required via the Human-In-The-Loop interface. For Alzheimer's Disease classification using automatically preprocessed multimodal data, our agent ensemble achieves an AUC of 0.9518 with four modalities, outperforming all single-modality baselines. These results show that NeuroAgent can reduce the manual effort required for neuroimaging preprocessing and enable end-to-end automated analysis pipelines for neuroimaging research.
