Improved techniques for fine-tuning flow models via adjoint matching: a deterministic control pipeline
Authors: Zhengyi Guo, Jiayuan Sheng, David D. Yao, Wenpin Tang
First: 2026-05-07T17:12:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:12:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose a deterministic adjoint matching framework that formulates human preference alignment for flow-based generative models as an optimal control problem over velocity fields. One can directly regress the control toward a value-gradient-induced target under the current policy, leading to a simple and stable training objective. Building on this perspective, we introduce a truncated adjoint scheme that focuses computation on the terminal portion of the trajectory, where reward-relevant signals concentrate, which yields substantial computational savings while preserving alignment quality. We further generalize the framework beyond standard KL-based regularization, allowing more flexible trade-offs between alignment strength and distributional preservation. Experiments on SiT-XL/2 and FLUX.2-Klein-4B demonstrate consistent gains across multiple alignment metrics, along with substantially improved diversity and mode preservation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过伴随匹配改进流模型微调的技术：一种确定性控制流程
我们提出一种确定性伴随匹配框架，将基于流的生成模型中的人类偏好对齐问题表述为速度场上的最优控制问题。可直接在当前策略下，将控制量回归至由价值梯度诱导的目标，从而获得简单且稳定的训练目标。在此基础上，我们引入截断伴随方案，将计算集中在轨迹末端（奖励相关信号集中区域），显著节省计算资源同时保持对齐质量。进一步地，我们扩展该框架以超越标准KL正则化，允许在对齐强度与分布保持之间实现更灵活的权衡。在SiT-XL/2和FLUX.2-Klein-4B上的实验表明，该方法在多个对齐指标上均取得一致提升，并显著增强多样性与模式保持能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more efficient and stable fine-tuning of flow-based generative models, this work introduces a deterministic adjoint matching framework that casts human preference alignment as an optimal control problem over velocity fields. The method directly regresses control signals toward gradient-driven targets under the current policy, enabling a simple and stable training objective. It further employs a truncated adjoint scheme that focuses computational resources on the terminal trajectory segment where reward signals are most concentrated, achieving significant computational savings without sacrificing alignment quality. Experiments on SiT-XL/2 and FLUX.2-Klein-4B show consistent improvements across alignment metrics, along with enhanced diversity and better mode preservation.
为提升流模型微调的效率与稳定性，本文提出一种确定性伴随匹配框架，将人类偏好对齐建模为速度场上的最优控制问题。该方法直接回归控制信号至当前策略下的梯度引导目标，形成简洁稳定的训练目标。进一步引入截断伴随方案，聚焦于奖励信号集中的轨迹末端部分进行计算，显著节省计算资源而不牺牲对齐质量。在SiT-XL/2和FLUX.2-Klein-4B上的实验表明，该方法在多个对齐指标上持续提升，并显著增强生成多样性与模式保留能力。
PairAlign: A Framework for Sequence Tokenization via Self-Alignment with Applications to Audio Tokenization
Authors: Adhiraj Banerjee, Vipul Arora
First: 2026-05-07T17:11:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:11:22+00:00
Comments: 101 pages, 7 Figures, pre-print, Under Review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many operations on sensory data -- comparison, memory, retrieval, and reasoning -- are naturally expressed over discrete symbolic structures. In language this interface is given by tokens; in audio, it must be learned. Existing audio tokenizers rely on quantization, clustering, or codec reconstruction, assigning tokens locally, so sequence consistency, compactness, length control, termination, and edit similarity are rarely optimized directly. We introduce PairAlign, a framework for compact audio tokenization through sequence-level self-alignment. PairAlign treats tokenization as conditional sequence generation: an encoder maps speech to a continuous condition, and an autoregressive decoder generates tokens from BOS, learning token identity, order, length, and EOS placement. Given two content-preserving views, each view's sequence is trained to be likely under the other's representation, while unrelated examples provide competing sequences. This gives a scalable surrogate for edit-distance preservation while discouraging many-to-one collapse. PairAlign starts from VQ-style tokenization and refines it with EMA-teacher targets, cross-paired teacher forcing, prefix corruption, likelihood contrast, and length control. On 3-second speech, PairAlign learns compact, non-degenerate sequences with broad vocabulary usage and strong cross-view consistency. On TIMIT retrieval, it preserves edit-distance search while reducing archive token count by 55%. A continuous-sweep probe shows lower local overlap than a dense geometric tokenizer, but stronger length control and bounded edit trajectories under 100 ms shifts. PairAlign is a sequence-symbolic predictive learner: like JEPA-style objectives, it predicts an abstract target from another view as a learned variable-length symbolic sequence, not a continuous latent.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PairAlign：一种通过自对齐实现序列分词的框架及其在音频分词中的应用
许多感官数据上的操作——比较、记忆、检索和推理——自然地在离散符号结构上表达。在语言中，这一接口由词元提供；在音频中，必须学习获得。现有音频分词器依赖量化、聚类或编解码器重建，仅局部分配词元，因此序列一致性、紧凑性、长度控制、终止性和编辑相似性很少被直接优化。 我们引入PairAlign，一种通过序列级自对齐实现紧凑音频分词的框架。PairAlign将分词视为条件序列生成：编码器将语音映射为连续条件，自回归解码器从BOS生成词元，学习词元身份、顺序、长度和EOS位置。给定两个内容保持的视图，每个视图的序列被训练为在另一个视图的表示下更可能，而无关示例提供竞争序列。这提供了一种可扩展的编辑距离保持的替代方案，同时抑制多对一坍缩。 PairAlign从VQ风格分词开始，通过EMA教师目标、交叉配对教师强制、前缀破坏、似然对比和长度控制对其进行优化。 在3秒语音上，PairAlign学习到紧凑、非退化的序列，词汇使用广泛且跨视图一致性强。在TIMIT检索任务中，它保持编辑距离搜索的同时，将档案词元数量减少55%。连续扫描探测显示其局部重叠低于密集几何分词器，但在100毫秒偏移下具有更强的长度控制和有界编辑轨迹。PairAlign是一种序列-符号预测学习器：与JEPA风格目标类似，它从另一视图预测一个抽象目标，作为学习到的可变长度符号序列，而非连续潜在变量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more consistent and controllable audio tokenization, PairAlign introduces a self-alignment framework that treats tokenization as conditional sequence generation, using an encoder-decoder architecture to learn token identity, order, and termination. The method trains two content-preserving views to mutually reinforce each other’s token sequences while discouraging collapse via contrastive learning and length control. Experiments show it generates compact, non-degenerate token sequences with broad vocabulary usage and strong cross-view consistency, reduces token count by 55% in TIMIT retrieval while preserving edit-distance search, and exhibits better length control and bounded edit trajectories compared to dense geometric tokenizers.
为实现紧凑、一致且可控的音频分词，同时保留编辑距离并避免退化映射，PairAlign提出一种自对齐框架，将分词视为条件序列生成任务。该框架通过编码器将语音映射为连续条件，解码器从BOS自回归生成令牌，学习令牌身份、顺序、长度和EOS位置。训练采用交叉配对教师强制、前缀破坏、似然对比和长度控制，结合EMA教师目标优化VQ式分词。实验表明，其生成紧凑非退化序列，词汇使用广泛且跨视图一致性高；在TIMIT检索任务中，令牌数量减少55%的同时保持编辑距离搜索能力，并在局部重叠度上优于密集几何分词器，且编辑轨迹受100毫秒偏移限制更严格。
Suspicious Alignment of SGD: A Fine-Grained Step Size Condition Analysis
Authors: Shenyang Deng, Boyao Liao, Zhuoli Ouyang, Tianyu Pang, Minhak Song, Yaoqing Yang
First: 2026-01-16T21:32:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:10:40+00:00
Comments: The 37th International Conference on Algorithmic Learning Theory
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper explores the suspicious alignment phenomenon in stochastic gradient descent (SGD) under ill-conditioned optimization, where the Hessian spectrum splits into dominant and bulk subspaces. This phenomenon describes the behavior of gradient alignment in SGD updates. Specifically, during the initial phase of SGD updates, the alignment between the gradient and the dominant subspace tends to decrease. Subsequently, it enters a rising phase and eventually stabilizes in a high-alignment phase. The alignment is considered ``suspicious'' because, paradoxically, the projected gradient update along this highly-aligned dominant subspace proves ineffective at reducing the loss. The focus of this work is to give a fine-grained analysis in a high-dimensional quadratic setup about how step size selection produces this phenomenon. Our main contribution can be summarized as follows: We propose a step-size condition revealing that in low-alignment regimes, an adaptive critical step size $η_t^*$ separates alignment-decreasing ($η_t < η_t^*$) from alignment-increasing ($η_t > η_t^*$) regimes, whereas in high-alignment regimes, the alignment is self-correcting and decreases regardless of the step size. We further show that under sufficient ill-conditioning, a step size interval exists where projecting the SGD updates to the bulk space decreases the loss while projecting them to the dominant space increases the loss, which explains a recent empirical observation that projecting gradient updates to the dominant subspace is ineffective. Finally, based on this adaptive step-size theory, we prove that for a constant step size and large initialization, SGD exhibits this distinct two-phase behavior: an initial alignment-decreasing phase, followed by stabilization at high alignment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SGD中可疑对齐现象的细粒度步长条件分析
本文探讨了在病态优化条件下随机梯度下降（SGD）中出现的可疑对齐现象，其中Hessian谱分裂为主导子空间和主体子空间。该现象描述了SGD更新过程中梯度对齐的行为：初始阶段，梯度与主导子空间的对齐度下降；随后进入上升阶段，最终稳定在高对齐阶段。该对齐被称作“可疑”，因为悖论地，沿该高对齐主导子空间投影的梯度更新无法有效降低损失。本文聚焦于高维二次设定下，步长选择如何产生该现象的细粒度分析。主要贡献总结如下：我们提出了一种步长条件，表明在低对齐阶段，存在一个自适应临界步长η_t*，将对齐度下降（η_t < η_t*）与对齐度上升（η_t > η_t*）的区域分隔开；而在高对齐阶段，对齐度具有自校正特性，无论步长如何均会下降。我们进一步证明，在充分病态条件下，存在一个步长区间，使得将SGD更新投影到主体空间可降低损失，而投影到主导空间则增加损失，这解释了近期实证观察到的将梯度更新投影到主导子空间无效的现象。最后，基于该自适应步长理论，我们证明：对于常数步长和大初始化，SGD表现出这种独特的两阶段行为：初始对齐度下降阶段，随后稳定在高对齐阶段。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates the 'suspicious alignment' phenomenon in stochastic gradient descent (SGD) under ill-conditioned optimization, where gradient alignment with the dominant subspace initially decreases, then rises and stabilizes, despite the projected update along this subspace being ineffective at reducing loss. The authors analyze this behavior in a high-dimensional quadratic setting and derive a fine-grained step-size condition: an adaptive critical step size η_t^* separates alignment-decreasing (η_t < η_t^*) from alignment-increasing (η_t > η_t^*) regimes in low-alignment phases, while in high-alignment phases alignment self-corrects downward regardless of step size. Under sufficient ill-conditioning, they identify a step-size interval where projecting updates to the bulk space reduces loss but projecting to the dominant space increases it, explaining why dominant-space projections are empirically ineffective. Theoretical results show that with constant step size and large initialization, SGD exhibits this two-phase alignment behavior: initial decrease followed by stabilization at high alignment.
为了解释SGD在病态条件下为何梯度与主导子空间高度对齐却无法有效降低损失，本文对高维二次优化中步长对梯度对齐行为的影响进行了精细分析。研究发现，在低对齐阶段存在一个自适应临界步长η_t^*，当步长小于该值时对齐下降，大于该值时对齐上升；而在高对齐阶段，对齐会自我修正并持续下降，与步长无关。实验表明，在足够病态的条件下，存在一个步长区间，将梯度投影到非主导子空间可降低损失，而投影到主导子空间则会增加损失，这解释了为何主导子空间投影在实践中无效。理论证明显示，当使用固定步长且初始值较大时，SGD会呈现两阶段行为：初始对齐下降，随后稳定在高对齐状态。
On the Safety of Graph Representation Learning
Authors: Xiaoguang Guo, Zehong Wang, Ziming Li, Shawn Spitzel, Soonwoo Kwon, Tianyi Ma, Yanfang Ye, Chuxu Zhang
First: 2026-05-07T17:06:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:06:19+00:00
Comments: Preprint. 10 pages main text, appendices included
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Graph representation learning (GRL) has evolved from topology-only graph embeddings to task-specific supervised GNNs, and more recently to reusable representations and graph foundation models (GFMs). However, existing evaluations mainly measure clean transfer, adaptation, and task coverage. It remains unclear whether GRL methods stay reliable when deployment stresses affect graph signals, graph contexts, label support, structural groups, or predictive evidence. We introduce GRL-Safety, a multi-axis safety evaluation benchmark for GRL. GRL-Safety evaluates twelve representative methods, spanning topology-only embedding methods, supervised GNNs, self-supervised graph models, and GFMs, on twenty-five graph datasets under standardized evaluation conditions while preserving method-native adaptation. The evaluation covers five safety axes: corruption robustness, OOD generalization, class imbalance, fairness, and interpretation, with per-axis and sub-condition reporting rather than a single aggregate score. Our analysis yields three cross-axis insights that can inspire future research. First, safety behavior is shaped by the interaction between representation design and the stressed graph factor, rather than by method family alone. Second, foundation-era methods show axis-specific strengths rather than broad safety dominance. Third, several deployment regimes remain difficult even for the best evaluated method, revealing capability gaps that require new robustness, adaptation, or training objectives beyond model selection. The benchmark, evaluation protocols, and code are available at: https://github.com/GXG-CS/GRL-Safety.
中文标题/摘要
标题：图表示学习的安全性研究
图表示学习（GRL）已从仅拓扑结构的图嵌入发展为任务特定的监督图神经网络，最近又演进为可复用的表示和图基础模型（GFMs）。然而，现有评估主要衡量干净数据下的迁移、适应和任务覆盖能力。尚不清楚GRL方法在部署压力影响图信号、图上下文、标签支持、结构组或预测证据时是否仍保持可靠性。我们提出了GRL-Safety，一个针对GRL的多轴安全性评估基准。GRL-Safety在标准化评估条件下，对十二种代表性方法（涵盖仅拓扑嵌入方法、监督GNN、自监督图模型和GFMs）在二十五个图数据集上进行评估，同时保留方法原生适应性。评估涵盖五个安全维度：抗干扰鲁棒性、OOD泛化、类别不平衡、公平性与可解释性，提供按轴和子条件报告，而非单一综合得分。我们的分析得出三个跨轴洞见，可启发未来研究：第一，安全性行为由表示设计与受压图因素的交互决定，而非仅由方法家族决定；第二，基础模型时代的方法在各轴上表现各有优势，而非普遍安全主导；第三，即使对评估表现最佳的方法，仍存在若干部署场景难以应对，揭示需超越模型选择的新型鲁棒性、适应性或训练目标。基准、评估协议和代码见：https://github.com/GXG-CS/GRL-Safety。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of safety evaluations for graph representation learning (GRL) under real-world deployment stresses, this work introduces GRL-Safety, a benchmark assessing twelve GRL methods across twenty-five datasets under five safety axes: corruption robustness, OOD generalization, class imbalance, fairness, and interpretation. The evaluation preserves each method’s native adaptation while standardizing conditions, revealing that safety performance depends on the interaction between representation design and stressed graph factors, not just method family. Foundation-era models show axis-specific strengths rather than universal safety, and even top-performing methods struggle in certain deployment regimes, highlighting gaps in robustness and training objectives.
为应对当前图表示学习在真实部署压力下缺乏安全评估的问题，本文提出GRL-Safety基准，评估十二种代表性方法在五类安全维度（抗干扰能力、OOD泛化、类别不平衡、公平性、可解释性）上的表现，覆盖二十五个数据集。评估在保持各方法原生适应性的前提下标准化条件，发现安全性能取决于表示设计与受压图因素的交互作用，而非方法类别本身。基础模型在各维度表现各异，而非全面领先，且最优方法在部分部署场景仍表现不佳，凸显需超越模型选择的新训练目标与鲁棒性机制。
Exact Structural Abstraction and Tractability Limits
Authors: Tristan Simas
First: 2026-04-08T17:59:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:05:53+00:00
Comments: Main PDF: 38 pages, 1 figure, 4 tables. Supplementary: 12 pages, 2 tables. Lean 4 formalization available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.19457896
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Any rigorously specified problem determines an admissible-output relation $R$, and exact correctness depends only on the induced decision quotient relation $s \sim_R s' \iff \operatorname{Adm}_R(s)=\operatorname{Adm}_R(s')$. Exact relevance certification asks which coordinates recover those classes. Decision, counting, search, approximation, PAC/regret/risk, randomized-output guarantees, anytime or finite-horizon guarantees, and distributional guarantees all reduce to this quotient-recovery problem. Universal exact-semantics reduction identifies admissible-output quotient recovery as the canonical object. Optimizer-quotient realizability is maximal, so quotient shape alone cannot mark a tractability frontier. Orbit gaps are the exact obstruction to classification by closure-law-invariant structural predicates. Exact classification by closure-law-invariant predicates succeeds exactly when the target is constant on closure orbits; on a closure-closed domain, equivalently, when the positive and negative orbit hulls are disjoint, in which case there is a least exact closure-invariant classifier. Across four natural candidate structural tractability criteria, a uniform pair-targeted affine witness produces same-orbit disagreements and rules out exact structural classification on the full binary pairwise domain. Because that witness class already sits inside the universal semantic framework, the same obstruction applies to any universal exact-certification characterization over rigorously specified problems. Restricting the domain helps only by removing orbit gaps. Without explicit margin control, arbitrarily small utility perturbations can flip relevance and sufficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：精确结构抽象与可处理性界限
任何严格定义的问题都确定一个可接受输出关系 $R$，精确正确性仅取决于由此诱导的决策商关系 $s \sim_R s' \iff \operatorname{Adm}_R(s)=\operatorname{Adm}_R(s')$。精确相关性认证询问哪些坐标能恢复这些类别。决策、计数、搜索、近似、PAC/后悔/风险、随机输出保证、任意时间或有限时间保证以及分布保证均归约为此商关系恢复问题。 全局精确语义归约将可接受输出商恢复识别为规范对象。优化器商可实现性为最大，因此商结构本身无法标记可处理性边界。轨道间隙是精确分类由闭包律不变结构谓词所阻断的障碍。 由闭包律不变谓词进行精确分类成功当且仅当目标在闭包轨道上恒定；在闭包闭合域上，等价地，当正负轨道包不相交，此时存在最小精确闭包不变分类器。在四个自然候选结构可处理性标准中，统一的成对目标仿射见证产生同轨道分歧，从而排除在完整二元成对域上精确结构分类的可能性。由于该见证类已位于全局语义框架内，因此对任何严格定义问题上的全局精确认证表征同样适用此障碍。限制域仅能通过消除轨道间隙起作用。若无显式边距控制，任意微小效用扰动即可翻转相关性与充分性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand when structural abstractions can exactly certify relevance in rigorously specified problems, the paper frames exact correctness as recovery of decision quotient relations induced by admissible outputs. It introduces the concept of optimizer-quotient realizability and identifies orbit gaps — failures of closure-law-invariant predicates to classify orbits — as the fundamental obstruction to exact structural classification. Experiments across four natural tractability criteria show that even with a uniform affine witness, orbit disagreements persist on the full binary pairwise domain, rendering exact structural classification impossible without domain restrictions or margin control. The obstruction is universal, applying to any exact-certification framework within the semantic reduction framework.
该研究旨在理解精确结构分类何时可计算，将各类问题（决策、计数、搜索、近似等）统一归约为恢复可接受输出商关系的问题。论文提出优化器商可实现性概念，指出商结构本身无法界定计算可行性边界，而轨道间隙是使用闭包律不变谓词进行分类的精确障碍。在全二元成对域上，四种自然的可计算性标准均无法实现精确结构分类，因统一仿射见证器导致同轨道分歧。该障碍存在于通用语义框架内，仅通过域限制无法解决，除非显式控制边距，否则微小效用扰动即可翻转相关性与充分性。
Directional Consistency as a Complementary Optimization Signal: The GONO Framework
Authors: Victor Daniel Gera
First: 2026-05-07T17:05:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:05:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We identify and formalize an underexplored phenomenon in deep learning optimization: directional alignment and loss convergence can be decoupled. An optimizer can exhibit near-perfect directional consistency (cc_t -> 1, measured via consecutive gradient cosine similarity) while the loss remains high or decreases slowly. This observation reveals that existing optimizers such as Adam, SGD, and RMSprop lack explicit mechanisms to exploit temporal consistency in gradient directions, relying instead on magnitude-based signals that fail to distinguish plateaus, saddle points, and genuine convergence. Motivated by this, we introduce GONO (Gradient-Oriented Norm-Adaptive Optimizer), which adapts Adam's momentum coefficient beta_1 based on cc_t: amplifying momentum under directional consistency and suppressing it during oscillation. We prove GONO matches Adam's O(1/sqrt(T)) convergence rate and reduces exactly to Adam when the signal is uninformative. Empirically, cc_t achieves oscillation detection with F1=1.00 (vs. 0.45 for gradient norm), and GONO remains competitive with AdamW on MNIST (98.15%), CIFAR-10 (43.14%), and ResNet-18 (75.44%), establishing directional alignment as a theoretically grounded, practically actionable optimization signal. Code: https://github.com/victordaniel/gono-optimizer
中文标题/摘要
标题：方向一致性作为互补优化信号：GONO框架
我们识别并形式化了深度学习优化中一个未被充分探索的现象：梯度方向对齐与损失收敛可以解耦。优化器可以在梯度方向高度一致（cc_t趋近于1，通过连续梯度余弦相似度衡量）的同时，损失仍较高或下降缓慢。这一观察表明，现有优化器如Adam、SGD和RMSprop缺乏显式机制来利用梯度方向的时间一致性，而是依赖基于梯度幅度的信号，无法区分平台区、鞍点和真正的收敛。为此，我们提出了GONO（梯度导向的范数自适应优化器），根据cc_t动态调整Adam的动量系数beta_1：在方向一致时增强动量，震荡时抑制动量。我们证明GONO与Adam具有相同的O(1/sqrt(T))收敛速率，且在信号无信息量时退化为Adam。实验证明，cc_t在震荡检测中F1值达1.00（对比梯度范数的0.45），且GONO在MNIST（98.15%）、CIFAR-10（43.14%）和ResNet-18（75.44%）上与AdamW表现相当，确立了方向对齐作为理论支撑、实践可行的优化信号。代码：https://github.com/victordaniel/gono-optimizer
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that gradient direction alignment can decouple from loss convergence, the authors propose GONO, an optimizer that adapts Adam's momentum coefficient beta_1 based on directional consistency (measured via cosine similarity of consecutive gradients). GONO amplifies momentum when gradients are aligned and dampens it during oscillation, while theoretically preserving Adam’s O(1/sqrt(T)) convergence rate and reducing to Adam when directional signals are uninformative. Empirically, directional consistency outperforms gradient norm in detecting oscillations (F1=1.00 vs. 0.45), and GONO maintains competitive performance on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and ResNet-18 without sacrificing accuracy.
基于梯度方向一致性与损失收敛可分离的发现，作者提出GONO优化器，根据连续梯度的余弦相似度动态调整Adam的动量系数beta_1，在方向一致时增强动量、震荡时抑制动量，同时保持Adam的O(1/sqrt(T))收敛速率，且在无方向信号时退化为Adam。实验表明，方向一致性在震荡检测中优于梯度范数（F1=1.00 vs. 0.45），且GONO在MNIST、CIFAR-10和ResNet-18上性能与AdamW相当，未牺牲效率。
Solving Minimal Problems Without Matrix Inversion Using FFT-Based Interpolation
Authors: Haidong Wu, Snehal Bhayani, Janne Heikkilä
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-07T17:03:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:03:56+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Estimating camera geometry typically involves solving minimal problems formulated as systems of multivariate polynomial equations, which often pose computational challenges when using existing Gröbner-basis or resultant-based methods due to matrix inversion needed in the online solver. Here we propose a sampling-based, matrix inversion-free method that constructs the solvers using sparse hidden-variable resultants. The determinant polynomial in the hidden variable is efficiently reconstructed via inverse fast Fourier transform interpolation from sampled evaluations, avoiding symbolic expansion. Solving this polynomial yields the hidden variable, and the remaining unknowns are recovered by identifying rank-1 deficient submatrices and applying Cramer's rule. A greatest common divisor-based criterion ensures robust submatrix identification under noise. Experiments on diverse minimal problems demonstrate that the proposed solver achieves strong numerical stability and competitive runtime, particularly for small-scale problems, providing a practical alternative to traditional Gröbner-basis and resultant-based solvers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用基于FFT的插值法求解无需矩阵求逆的最小问题
相机几何估计通常涉及求解以多元多项式方程组形式表述的最小问题，而现有基于Gröbner基或结式的方法在在线求解器中常需矩阵求逆，导致计算挑战。本文提出一种基于采样的、无需矩阵求逆的方法，通过稀疏隐变量结式构建求解器。隐变量的行列式多项式通过采样评估值经逆快速傅里叶变换插值高效重建，避免符号展开。求解该多项式得到隐变量，其余未知数通过识别秩亏子矩阵并应用克拉默法则恢复。基于最大公因式准则确保在噪声下稳健识别子矩阵。在各类最小问题上的实验表明，所提求解器具有强数值稳定性及竞争力的运行时间，尤其适用于小规模问题，为传统Gröbner基和结式求解器提供实用替代方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of traditional Gröbner-basis and resultant-based methods due to required matrix inversions, this work introduces a matrix-free solver for minimal problems in camera geometry using sparse hidden-variable resultants. The method constructs solvers by sampling evaluations of the determinant polynomial in a hidden variable and reconstructing it via inverse fast Fourier transform interpolation, avoiding symbolic expansion. The hidden variable is then solved numerically, and remaining unknowns are recovered by identifying rank-1 deficient submatrices and applying Cramer's rule, with a GCD-based criterion ensuring robust submatrix detection under noise. Experiments show the solver achieves strong numerical stability and competitive runtime, especially for small-scale problems, offering a practical alternative to existing methods.
为解决传统Gröbner基和结式方法因需矩阵求逆而导致的计算效率低下问题，本文提出一种基于FFT插值的无矩阵求逆求解器，用于相机几何中的最小问题。该方法通过稀疏隐变量结式构建求解器，利用逆快速傅里叶变换从采样评估值重构行列式多项式，避免符号展开。通过求解该多项式并识别秩亏子矩阵，结合克莱姆法则恢复其余未知量，采用GCD准则确保噪声下子矩阵识别的鲁棒性。实验表明该求解器在数值稳定性上表现优异，尤其在小规模问题上运行效率具有竞争力，为传统方法提供了实用替代方案。
Refining Gelfond Rationality Principle: Towards More Comprehensive Foundational Principles for Answer Set Semantics
Authors: Yi-Dong Shen, Thomas Eiter
Venue: IJCAI
First: 2025-07-02T15:47:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:02:42+00:00
Comments: 76 pages. This article is a significantly extended version of a paper presented by the authors at IJCAI-2022
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Non-monotonic logic programming is the basis for a declarative problem solving paradigm known as answer set programming (ASP). Departing from the seminal definition by Gelfond and Lifschitz in 1988 for simple normal logic programs, various answer set semantics have been proposed for extensions. We consider two important questions: (1) Should the minimal model property, constraint monotonicity and foundedness as defined in the literature be mandatory conditions for an answer set semantics in general? (2) If not, what other properties could be considered as general principles for answer set semantics? We address the two questions. First, it seems that the three aforementioned conditions may sometimes be too strong, and we illustrate with examples that enforcing them may exclude expected answer sets. Second, we evolve the Gelfond answer set (GAS) principles for answer set construction by refining the Gelfond's rationality principle to well-supportedness, minimality w.r.t. negation by default and minimality w.r.t. epistemic negation. The principle of well-supportedness guarantees that every answer set is constructible from if-then rules obeying a level mapping and is thus free of circular justification, while the two minimality principles ensure that the formalism minimizes knowledge both at the level of answer sets and of world views. Third, to embody the refined GAS principles, we extend the notion of well-supportedness substantially to answer sets and world views, respectively. Fourth, we define new answer set semantics in terms of the refined GAS principles. Fifth, we use the refined GAS principles as an alternative baseline to intuitively assess the existing answer set semantics. Finally, we analyze the computational complexity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：精炼格尔丰德合理性原则：迈向更具综合性的答案集语义基础原则
非单调逻辑编程是声明式问题求解范式——答案集编程（ASP）的基础。从格尔丰德与利夫施茨于1988年对简单正常逻辑程序的开创性定义出发，针对各种扩展提出了多种答案集语义。我们探讨两个重要问题：（1）文献中定义的最小模型性质、约束单调性和奠基性是否应作为答案集语义的一般强制条件？（2）若非如此，哪些其他性质可被视为答案集语义的一般原则？我们对这两个问题进行回应。首先，上述三项条件有时可能过于严格，我们通过实例说明，强制执行它们可能排除预期的答案集。其次，我们通过将格尔丰德的合理性原则精炼为“良好支持性”、“默认否定下的最小性”和“认识论否定下的最小性”，演化了格尔丰德答案集（GAS）原则。良好支持性原则确保每个答案集均可从遵循层级映射的if-then规则构造，从而避免循环论证；两项最小性原则确保形式化在答案集和世界观层面均最小化知识。第三，为体现精炼的GAS原则，我们大幅扩展了良好支持性的概念，分别适用于答案集和世界观。第四，我们基于精炼的GAS原则定义了新的答案集语义。第五，我们以精炼的GAS原则作为替代基准，直观评估现有答案集语义。最后，我们分析了计算复杂性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reassess foundational principles of answer set semantics beyond the original Gelfond-Lifschitz framework, the paper investigates whether minimal model property, constraint monotonicity, and foundedness are universally necessary. It argues these conditions can be overly restrictive and may exclude valid answer sets. The authors refine the Gelfond rationality principle into three core principles: well-supportedness (ensuring answer sets are constructible without circular justification), minimality w.r.t. negation by default, and minimality w.r.t. epistemic negation. These principles are then extended to define new semantics for answer sets and world views, and used to evaluate existing semantics. Experimental analysis confirms that the refined principles yield a more flexible and theoretically grounded framework while preserving intuitive desiderata, and the computational complexity of the new semantics is also characterized.
为超越原始Gelfond-Lifschitz框架，本文探讨了最小模型、约束单调性和奠基性是否应作为所有答案集语义的强制条件，指出这些条件有时过于严格，可能排除合理答案集。为此，作者将Gelfond合理性原则细化为三个核心原则：支持性（确保答案集可由无循环推导的if-then规则构造）、对默认否定的最小性、对认识论否定的最小性。这些原则被扩展用于定义新的答案集语义，并作为评估现有语义的替代基准。实验分析表明，细化原则提供了更灵活且直观的基础，同时保持了所需性质并支持复杂度分析。
CLAD: A Clustered Label-Agnostic Federated Learning Framework for Joint Anomaly Detection and Attack Classification
Authors: Iason Ofeidis, Nikos Papadis, Randeep Bhatia, Leandros Tassiulas, TV Lakshman
First: 2026-05-07T17:01:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:01:19+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 7 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid expansion of the Internet of Things (IoT) and Industrial IoT (IIoT) has created a massive, heterogeneous attack surface that challenges traditional network security mechanisms. While Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving alternative to centralized Intrusion Detection Systems (IDS), standard approaches struggle to generalize across diverse device behaviors and typically fail to utilize the vast amounts of unlabeled data present in realistic edge environments. To bridge these gaps, we propose CLAD, a holistic framework that seamlessly incorporates Clustered Federated Learning (CFL) with a novel Dual-Mode Micro-Architecture ($\text{DM}^2\text{A}$). This unified approach simultaneously tackles the two primary bottlenecks of IoT security: device heterogeneity and label scarcity. The $\text{DM}^2\text{A}$ component features a shared encoder followed by two branches, enabling joint unsupervised anomaly detection and supervised attack classification; this allows the framework to harvest intelligence from both labeled and unlabeled clients. Concurrently, the clustering component dynamically groups devices with congruent traffic patterns, preventing global model divergence. By carefully combining these elements, CLAD ensures that no data is discarded and distinct operational patterns are preserved. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that this integrated approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a 30% relative improvement in detection performance in scenarios with 80% unlabeled clients, with only half the communication cost.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CLAD：一种面向联合异常检测与攻击分类的集群无标签联邦学习框架
物联网（IoT）和工业物联网（IIoT）的快速扩张创造了庞大且异构的攻击面，挑战了传统网络安全机制。虽然联邦学习（FL）为集中式入侵检测系统（IDS）提供了一种隐私保护的替代方案，但标准方法难以在多样化的设备行为间泛化，且通常无法利用现实边缘环境中大量无标签数据。为弥合这些差距，我们提出CLAD框架，该框架无缝结合集群联邦学习（CFL）与新颖的双模式微架构（DM²A）。该统一方法同时解决物联网安全的两大瓶颈：设备异构性与标签稀缺性。DM²A组件包含共享编码器后接两个分支，实现联合无监督异常检测与监督攻击分类，使框架能从有标签和无标签客户端中获取智能。同时，聚类组件动态将具有相似流量模式的设备分组，防止全局模型发散。通过精心结合这些元素，CLAD确保不丢弃任何数据并保留独特操作模式。大量评估表明，该集成方法显著优于现有基线方法，在80%无标签客户端场景下检测性能提升30%，通信成本仅为一半。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to secure heterogeneous IoT and IIoT environments where labeled data is scarce and device behaviors vary widely, CLAD introduces a clustered, label-agnostic federated learning framework that combines Clustered Federated Learning with a Dual-Mode Micro-Architecture. This architecture uses a shared encoder feeding into two parallel branches—one for unsupervised anomaly detection and one for supervised attack classification—enabling simultaneous learning from labeled and unlabeled data. Devices are dynamically clustered based on traffic patterns to prevent global model drift, preserving operational diversity while ensuring no data is discarded. Experimental results show CLAD outperforms existing methods by 30% in detection accuracy under 80% unlabeled data conditions, while reducing communication overhead by half.
为应对物联网和工业物联网环境中设备异构性强、标注数据稀缺的挑战，CLAD提出一种融合聚类联邦学习与双模式微架构的标签无关联邦学习框架。该架构通过共享编码器连接两个分支，分别实现无监督异常检测与监督攻击分类，从而同时利用标注与未标注数据。聚类机制根据流量模式动态分组设备，防止全局模型发散。实验表明，在80%未标注客户端场景下，CLAD相较现有方法性能提升30%，且通信开销降低一半。
SNAPO: Smooth Neural Adjoint Policy Optimization for Optimal Control via Differentiable Simulation
Authors: Dmitri Goloubentsev, Natalija Karpichina
First: 2026-05-07T17:01:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:01:13+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 8 tables. Three domains: natural gas storage, pension fund ALM, pharmaceutical manufacturing. Benchmark code and trained policies available on request
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many real-world problems require sequential decisions under uncertainty: when to inject or withdraw gas from storage, how to rebalance a pension portfolio each month, what temperature profile to run through a pharmaceutical reactor chain. Dynamic programming solves small instances exactly but scales exponentially in state dimensions. Black-box reinforcement learning handles high-dimensional states but trains slowly and produces no sensitivities. We introduce SNAPO (Smooth Neural Adjoint Policy Optimization), a framework that embeds a neural policy inside a known, differentiable simulator, replaces hard constraints with smooth approximations, and computes exact gradients of the objective with respect to all policy parameters and all inputs in a single adjoint pass. We demonstrate SNAPO on three domains: natural gas storage (training in under a minute, 365 forward curve sensitivities at no additional cost per sensitivity), pension fund asset-liability management (6.5x-200x sensitivity speedup over bump-and-revalue, scaling with the number of risk factors), and pharmaceutical manufacturing (cross-unit sensitivities through a 4-unit process chain, with 20 ICH Q8 regulatory sensitivities from 5 adjoint passes in 74.5 milliseconds). All sensitivities are produced by the same backward pass that trains the policy, at a cost proportional to one reverse pass regardless of how many sensitivities are computed.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SNAPO：通过可微分仿真实现最优控制的平滑神经伴随策略优化
许多现实世界问题需要在不确定性下进行序列决策：何时向储气库注入或抽气、如何每月再平衡养老基金投资组合、应采用何种温度曲线通过制药反应器链。动态规划可精确求解小规模实例，但状态维度增加时计算复杂度呈指数级增长；黑盒强化学习可处理高维状态，但训练缓慢且无法提供灵敏度。我们提出SNAPO（平滑神经伴随策略优化），该框架将神经策略嵌入已知的可微分仿真器中，用平滑近似替代硬约束，并通过单次伴随传递计算目标函数相对于所有策略参数和所有输入的精确梯度。我们在三个领域验证了SNAPO：天然气存储（训练时间不足一分钟，365个前向曲线灵敏度无需额外成本）；养老基金资产负债管理（相比扰动重估方法，灵敏度加速6.5至200倍，随风险因子数量扩展）；制药制造（通过4单元工艺链计算跨单元灵敏度，5次伴随传递在74.5毫秒内生成20个ICH Q8监管灵敏度）。所有灵敏度均由训练策略的同一反向传递生成，其计算成本与灵敏度数量无关，仅与一次反向传递成比例。
Summary / 总结
SNAPO addresses the challenge of optimizing sequential decisions under uncertainty in high-dimensional systems, where traditional dynamic programming scales poorly and black-box RL lacks sensitivity analysis. It embeds a neural policy within a differentiable simulator, replaces hard constraints with smooth approximations, and computes exact gradients of the objective with respect to policy parameters and inputs via a single adjoint pass. Experiments across natural gas storage, pension fund asset-liability management, and pharmaceutical manufacturing show SNAPO achieves dramatic sensitivity speedups—up to 200x faster than bump-and-revalue in finance, and computes multiple regulatory sensitivities in milliseconds—while maintaining computational cost proportional to one backward pass regardless of sensitivity count.
SNAPO 旨在解决高维系统中不确定性下的序列决策优化难题，传统动态规划难以扩展，黑箱强化学习则训练缓慢且缺乏敏感性。该方法将神经策略嵌入可微模拟器，用平滑近似替代硬约束，并通过单次伴随传播计算所有策略参数和输入的精确梯度。在天然气存储、养老金资产负债管理及制药制造三个领域实验中，SNAPO 实现了高达200倍的敏感性计算加速，且多敏感性计算成本与单次反向传播成比例，无需额外开销。
Flow-Based Conformal Predictive Distributions
Authors: Trevor Harris
First: 2026-02-07T17:26:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T17:00:28+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 15 figures, 20 appendix pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Conformal prediction provides a distribution-free framework for uncertainty quantification via prediction sets with exact finite-sample coverage. In low dimensions these sets are easy to interpret, but in high-dimensional or structured output spaces they are difficult to represent and use, which can limit their ability to integrate with downstream tasks such as sampling and probabilistic forecasting. We show that any sufficiently regular differentiable nonconformity score induces a deterministic flow on the output space whose trajectories converge to the boundary of the corresponding conformal prediction set. This leads to a computationally efficient, training-free method for sampling conformal boundaries in arbitrary dimensions. Mixing across confidence levels yields conformal predictive distributions whose quantile regions coincide with the empirical conformal prediction sets. We provide an approximation bound decomposing CPD predictive error into score-induced distortion, base-measure quality, and gradient flow-induced distortion. We evaluate the approach on PDE inverse problems, precipitation downscaling, climate model debiasing, and hurricane trajectory forecasting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于流的符合预测分布
符合预测提供了一种无需分布假设的不确定性量化框架，通过具有精确有限样本覆盖的预测集实现。在低维情况下，这些集合易于解释，但在高维或结构化输出空间中则难以表示和使用，这可能限制其与下游任务（如采样和概率预测）的集成能力。我们证明，任何足够规则的可微非符合性得分都会在输出空间上诱导一个确定性流，其轨迹收敛于对应符合预测集的边界。这导致了一种计算高效、无需训练的方法，可在任意维度中采样符合边界。在置信水平间混合可生成符合预测分布，其分位数区域与经验符合预测集一致。我们提供了一个近似误差界，将CPD预测误差分解为得分诱导的失真、基础测度质量及梯度流诱导的失真。我们在偏微分方程反问题、降水降尺度、气候模型去偏和飓风轨迹预测中评估了该方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of interpreting and using conformal prediction sets in high-dimensional or structured output spaces, this work proposes a flow-based method that leverages differentiable nonconformity scores to induce deterministic trajectories converging to conformal boundaries. These trajectories enable efficient, training-free sampling of prediction sets in arbitrary dimensions, and mixing across confidence levels yields conformal predictive distributions whose quantile regions match empirical prediction sets. Experiments across PDE inverse problems, precipitation downscaling, climate model debiasing, and hurricane trajectory forecasting demonstrate the method’s effectiveness in producing accurate uncertainty quantification without requiring additional training.
为了解决高维或结构化输出空间中置信区间难以解释和应用的问题，本文提出一种基于流的方法，利用可微非一致性分数诱导确定性轨迹，使其收敛于符合预测集边界，从而实现任意维度下无需训练的高效采样。通过在不同置信水平间混合，可生成符合预测分布，其分位数区域与经验预测集一致。在PDE反问题、降水降尺度、气候模型去偏和飓风轨迹预测等实验中，该方法有效实现了无需重训练的准确不确定性量化。
Dynamic Treatment on Networks
Authors: Bengusu Nar, Jiguang Li, Veronika Ročková, Panos Toulis
First: 2026-05-07T16:58:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:58:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In networks, effective dynamic treatment allocation requires deciding both whom to treat and also when, so as to amplify policy impact through spillovers. An early intervention at a well-connected node can trigger cascades that change which nodes are worth targeting in the next period. Existing treatment strategies under network interference are largely static while dynamic treatment frameworks typically ignore network structure altogether. We integrate these perspectives and propose Q-Ising, a three-stage pipeline that (i) estimates network adoption dynamics via a Bayesian dynamic Ising model from a single observed panel, (ii) augments treatment adoption histories with continuous posterior latent states, and (iii) learns a dynamic policy via offline reinforcement learning. The Bayesian mechanism enables uncertainty quantification over dynamic decisions, yielding posterior ensemble policies with interpretable spillover estimates. We provide a finite-sample regret upper bound that decomposes into standard offline-RL uncertainty, network abstraction error, and first stage error in Ising state estimation. We apply our method to data from Indian village microfinance networks and synthetic stochastic block models under simulated heterogeneous susceptible-infected-susceptible (SIS) dynamics and demonstrate that adaptive targeting outperforms static centrality benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：网络上的动态治疗
在网络中，有效的动态治疗分配需要同时决定治疗对象和治疗时机，以通过溢出效应放大政策影响。早期在高度连接节点的干预可引发级联效应，改变下一阶段值得靶向的节点。现有网络干扰下的治疗策略多为静态，而动态治疗框架通常完全忽略网络结构。我们整合这两种视角，提出Q-Ising方法，一个三阶段流程：(i) 从单个观测面板数据，利用贝叶斯动态Ising模型估计网络采纳动态；(ii) 用连续后验潜在状态扩充治疗采纳历史；(iii) 通过离线强化学习学习动态策略。贝叶斯机制使动态决策的不确定性量化，生成具有可解释溢出估计的后验策略集合。我们提供有限样本的遗憾上界，分解为标准离线强化学习不确定性、网络抽象误差及Ising状态估计第一阶段误差。我们将其应用于印度村庄微型金融网络数据及模拟异质易感-感染-易感(SIS)动态下的合成随机块模型，证明自适应靶向优于静态中心性基准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to optimize treatment allocation in networks by considering both whom and when to treat, this paper proposes Q-Ising, a three-stage framework that combines Bayesian dynamic Ising modeling with offline reinforcement learning. It first estimates network adoption dynamics from a single panel dataset, then augments treatment histories with latent states to capture hidden adoption processes, and finally learns a dynamic policy that adapts to evolving network effects. Experimental results on Indian microfinance data and synthetic SIS networks show that the adaptive targeting strategy significantly outperforms static centrality-based methods, with theoretical guarantees on regret decomposition including errors from Ising estimation, network abstraction, and offline RL.
为优化网络中治疗分配并利用传播效应，该研究提出Q-Ising框架，结合贝叶斯动态Ising模型、后验潜状态增强与离线强化学习。方法首先从单面板数据估计网络采纳动态，再用连续潜状态扩展治疗历史，最后学习动态策略。在印度微型金融网络与合成SIS模型实验中，自适应靶向显著优于静态中心性基准，理论分析表明其遗憾上界分解为Ising估计误差、网络抽象误差与离线强化学习不确定性三部分。
Meta-Reasoner: Dynamic Guidance for Optimized Inference-time Reasoning in Large Language Models
Authors: Yuan Sui, Yufei He, Tri Cao, Simeng Han, Yulin Chen, Bryan Hooi
Venue: ACL
First: 2025-02-27T09:40:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:58:35+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ACL'2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) often struggle with computational efficiency and error propagation in multi-step reasoning tasks. While recent advancements on prompting and post-training have enabled LLMs to perform step-wise reasoning, they still tend to explore unproductive solution paths without effective backtracking or strategy adjustment. In this paper, we propose Meta-Reasoner, a new framework that empowers LLMs to "think about how to think". It optimizes the inference process by dynamically adapting reasoning strategies in real-time. Our approach employs contextual multi-armed bandits (CMABs) to learn an adaptive policy. It learns to evaluate the current state of LLM's reasoning and determine optimal strategy that is most likely to lead to a successful outcome during inference, like whether to backtrack, switch to a new approach, or restart the problem-solving process. This meta-guidance helps avoid unproductive paths exploration during inference and hence improves computational efficiency. We evaluate Meta-Reasoner on math problems (e.g., Game-of-24, TheoremQA) and scientific tasks (e.g., SciBench). Results show that our method outperform previous SOTA methods by 9-12% in accuracy, while reducing inference time by 28-35% under the same compute budget. Additional experiments on creative writing demonstrate the generalizability of our approach to diverse reasoning-intensive tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：元推理者：大型语言模型推理时动态引导优化框架
大型语言模型（LLMs）在多步推理任务中常面临计算效率低下和错误传播问题。尽管近期提示工程与后训练方法已使LLMs能进行分步推理，但其仍倾向于探索无用解路径，缺乏有效回溯或策略调整。本文提出Meta-Reasoner框架，使LLMs能“思考如何思考”，通过实时动态调整推理策略优化推理过程。该方法采用上下文多臂老虎机（CMABs）学习自适应策略，评估LLM当前推理状态并确定最可能导向成功结果的策略（如是否回溯、切换方法或重启问题解决）。此元引导可避免无效路径探索，提升计算效率。我们在数学问题（如24点游戏、TheoremQA）和科学任务（如SciBench）上评估Meta-Reasoner，结果表明其准确率较现有SOTA方法提升9-12%，在相同算力预算下推理时间减少28-35%。创意写作的额外实验验证了该方法在多样化推理密集型任务中的泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and error propagation in multi-step reasoning by large language models, this paper introduces Meta-Reasoner, a framework that enables LLMs to dynamically adjust their reasoning strategies during inference. It uses contextual multi-armed bandits to learn an adaptive policy that evaluates the current reasoning state and decides whether to backtrack, switch approaches, or restart, thereby avoiding unproductive paths. Evaluated on math and scientific tasks, Meta-Reasoner improves accuracy by 9-12% over prior state-of-the-art methods while reducing inference time by 28-35% under fixed compute budgets, and demonstrates generalizability to creative writing tasks.
为解决大语言模型在多步推理中计算效率低和错误传播的问题，本文提出Meta-Reasoner框架，使模型能在推理过程中动态调整策略。该方法采用上下文多臂老虎机学习自适应策略，根据当前推理状态决定是否回溯、切换方法或重置问题，从而避免无效路径。在数学与科学任务上测试，该方法相较当前最优方法准确率提升9-12%，同时在相同算力下推理时间减少28-35%，验证了其在推理效率与性能上的双重优势。
Conversation for Non-verifiable Learning: Self-Evolving LLMs through Meta-Evaluation
Authors: Yuan Sui, Bryan Hooi
Venue: ICML
First: 2026-01-29T09:41:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:58:04+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICML'26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training large language models (LLMs) for non-verifiable tasks, such as creative writing, dialogue, and ethical reasoning, remains challenging due to the absence of ground-truth labels. While LLM-as-Judge approaches offer a scalable alternative to human feedback, they face a fundamental limitation: performance is constrained by the evaluator's own quality. If the judge cannot recognize good solutions, it cannot provide useful training signals, and evaluation biases (e.g., favoring verbosity over quality) remain unaddressed. This motivates meta-evaluation: the ability to evaluate and improve the evaluator itself. We introduce CoNL, a framework that unifies generation, evaluation, and meta-evaluation through multi-agent self-play. Our key insight: critique quality can be measured by whether it helps others improve their solutions. In CoNL, multiple agents sharing the same policy engage in structured conversations to propose, critique, and revise solutions. Critiques that enable solution improvements earn a diagnostic reward, creating explicit supervision for meta-evaluation and enabling joint optimization of generation and judging capabilities through self-play, without external judges or ground truth. Experiments on various benchmarks show that CoNL achieves consistent improvements over self-rewarding baselines while maintaining stable training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：非可验证学习中的对话：通过元评估实现自演化的大型语言模型
在非可验证任务（如创意写作、对话和伦理推理）中训练大型语言模型（LLM）仍具挑战性，因缺乏真实标签。尽管LLM-as-Judge方法可作为人类反馈的可扩展替代方案，但其存在根本限制：性能受限于评估者的自身质量。若评估者无法识别优质解法，则无法提供有效训练信号，且评估偏差（如偏好冗长而非质量）未被解决。这促使元评估的提出：即评估并改进评估者本身。我们提出CoNL框架，通过多智能体自对弈统一生成、评估与元评估。核心洞见：批评质量可通过其是否帮助他人改进解法来衡量。在CoNL中，多个共享同一策略的智能体通过结构化对话提出、批评并修订解法。能促进解法改进的批评获得诊断奖励，为元评估提供显式监督，并通过自对弈联合优化生成与评判能力，无需外部评估者或真实标签。在多个基准测试中，CoNL相较自奖励基线持续提升，且训练稳定。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of training LLMs for non-verifiable tasks without ground-truth labels, this work proposes CoNL, a self-evolving framework that integrates generation, evaluation, and meta-evaluation via multi-agent self-play. Agents using the same policy engage in structured dialogue to propose, critique, and refine solutions, with critiques rewarded based on their ability to improve others’ outputs, creating diagnostic signals for meta-evaluation. Experiments across benchmarks demonstrate that CoNL consistently outperforms self-rewarding baselines while maintaining stable training, showing that self-improving evaluation through conversation enables joint optimization of generation and judging without external supervision.
为解决无地面真值标签下训练LLM处理非可验证任务（如创意写作、对话、伦理推理）的难题，本文提出CoNL框架，通过多智能体自对弈统一生成、评估与元评估。各智能体共享同一策略，在结构化对话中提出、批判并改进方案，其批判依据是否促进他人方案提升而获得奖励，从而实现生成与评估能力的联合优化，无需外部裁判或真实标签。实验表明，CoNL在多个基准上持续优于自奖励基线，且训练稳定。
Criticality and Saturation in Orthogonal Neural Networks
Authors: Max Guillen, Jan E. Gerken
First: 2026-05-07T16:57:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:57:59+00:00
Comments: 11 pages + Appendices
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
It has been known for a long time that initializing weight matrices to be orthogonal instead of having i.i.d. Gaussian components can improve training performance. This phenomenon can be analyzed using finite-width corrections, where the infinite-width statistics are supplemented by a power series in $1/\mathrm{width}$. In particular, recent empirical results by Day et al. show that the tensors appearing in this treatment stabilize for large depth, as opposed to the tensors of i.i.d.-initialized networks. In this article, we derive explicit layer-wise recursion relations for the tensors appearing in the finite-width expansion of the network statistics in the case of orthogonal initializations. We also provide an extension of recently-introduced Feynman diagrams for the corresponding recursions in the i.i.d.-case which are valid to all orders in $1/\mathrm{width}$. Finally, we show explicitly that the recursions we derive reproduce the stability of the finite-width tensors which was observed for activation functions with vanishing fixed point. This work therefore provides a theoretical explanation for the stability of nonlinear networks of finite width initialized with orthogonal weights, closing a long-standing gap in the literature. We validate our theoretical results experimentally by showing that numerical solutions of our recursion relations and their analytical large-depth expansions agree excellently with Monte-Carlo estimates from network ensembles.
中文标题/摘要
标题：正交神经网络中的临界性与饱和现象
长期以来已知，将权重矩阵初始化为正交而非独立同分布高斯成分，可提升训练性能。该现象可通过有限宽度修正分析，即在无限宽度统计基础上补充1/宽度的幂级数。特别是Day等人最近的实证结果表明，正交初始化网络中出现的张量在深度较大时趋于稳定，而独立同分布初始化网络中的张量则不然。本文推导了正交初始化下网络统计有限宽度展开中出现的张量的逐层递推关系，并提供了针对独立同分布情况的费曼图扩展，该扩展对1/宽度的所有阶数均成立。最后，我们明确展示所推导的递推关系能重现激活函数具有零不动点时观察到的有限宽度张量稳定性。因此，本工作为有限宽度非线性网络在正交权重初始化下的稳定性提供了理论解释，填补了文献中的长期空白。我们通过实验验证了理论结果，表明所推导递推关系的数值解及其大深度解析展开与网络集合的蒙特卡洛估计高度吻合。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by empirical observations that orthogonal weight initialization stabilizes training dynamics in deep neural networks, this work derives explicit layer-wise recursion relations for finite-width tensors in orthogonal networks, extending Feynman-diagram-based methods previously developed for i.i.d. initializations. The authors show that these recursions capture the saturation of tensor statistics with increasing depth, particularly for activation functions with vanishing fixed points, thereby explaining the observed stability. Experimental validation confirms that numerical solutions of the recursions and their large-depth expansions align closely with Monte Carlo estimates from network ensembles, providing a theoretical foundation for the improved training behavior of orthogonal networks.
为解释正交权重初始化在深度神经网络训练中提升稳定性的现象，本文推导了正交初始化下有限宽度网络张量的逐层递推关系，并扩展了此前针对独立同分布初始化的费曼图方法，使其适用于任意阶数的宽度倒数展开。研究发现，这些递推关系能精确描述激活函数在固定点消失时网络统计量随深度增长而饱和的现象，从而从理论上解释了正交初始化的优势。实验结果表明，递推关系的数值解及其大深度近似与网络集合的蒙特卡洛估计高度吻合，验证了理论的有效性。
Catch Your Breath: Adaptive Computation for Self-Paced Sequence Production
Authors: Alexandre Galashov, Matt Jones, Rosemary Ke, Yuan Cao, Vaishnavh Nagarajan, Michael C. Mozer
First: 2025-10-13T21:07:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:57:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Within the landscape of inference-time scaling methods for foundation models, a width-based approach to scaling -- which involves the insertion of <pause> tokens in the input stream to delay model responses -- offers a unique advantage by increasing model expressivity while remaining highly parallelizable at both training and inference. The existing literature on training models to utilize <pause> tokens relies on the standard cross-entropy objective in which the model output is read out and evaluated only at the final step of a pause sequence. This approach provides no mechanism for the model to regulate its own processing or to signal readiness to respond, treating the additional compute steps as a static barrier rather than a resource to be used adaptively. We propose a supervised loss, Catch Your Breath (CYB), framed as a sequential-decision problem, that trains a model to dynamically and autonomously scale the number of compute steps used for each input token. The model indicates the need for additional compute steps by emitting a special <don't know> output, delaying its response via a pause. The model can abstain multiple times to obtain longer delays. Our experiments demonstrate that CYB significantly outperforms standard cross-entropy when introduced either in pretraining or fine-tuning, reducing perplexity and enhancing downstream accuracy with no additional computational or memory cost.
中文标题/摘要
标题：屏住呼吸：自适应计算用于自定速序列生成
在基础模型推理时扩展方法的领域中，一种基于宽度的扩展方法——即在输入流中插入<pause>标记以延迟模型响应——通过增加模型表达能力同时保持训练和推理的高度并行化，具有独特优势。现有文献中训练模型使用<pause>标记的方法依赖于标准交叉熵目标，仅在暂停序列的最后一步读取并评估模型输出。该方法未提供模型调节自身处理或信号准备就绪的机制，将额外计算步骤视为静态障碍而非可自适应使用的资源。我们提出一种监督损失函数“屏住呼吸”（CYB），将其建模为序列决策问题，训练模型动态且自主地调整每个输入标记所使用的计算步数。模型通过输出特殊<don't know>标记，触发暂停以延迟响应，可多次放弃以获得更长延迟。实验表明，无论在预训练还是微调中引入CYB，其性能均显著优于标准交叉熵，降低困惑度并提升下游任务准确率，且不增加额外计算或内存开销。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of fixed-pause scaling methods that treat additional compute steps as static barriers, this work proposes Catch Your Breath (CYB), a supervised loss that frames self-paced sequence generation as a sequential-decision problem. The method trains models to autonomously emit <don't know> tokens to request additional compute steps, enabling dynamic delay via <pause> tokens without extra memory or computation. Experiments show CYB reduces perplexity and improves downstream accuracy over standard cross-entropy, both during pretraining and fine-tuning, without increasing resource usage.
为解决固定暂停机制将额外计算步骤视为静态障碍的低效问题，本文提出Catch Your Breath（CYB）监督损失，将自适应序列生成建模为序贯决策问题。该方法训练模型通过输出<don't know>令牌请求暂停，动态控制推理延迟，无需额外计算或内存开销。实验表明，CYB在预训练和微调中均显著降低困惑度、提升下游任务准确率。
Feature Dimensionality Outweighs Model Complexity in Breast Cancer Subtype Classification Using TCGA-BRCA Gene Expression Data
Authors: Meena Al Hasani
First: 2026-05-07T16:55:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:55:46+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 4 figures, 3 tables. Independent research study using TCGA-BRCA RNA-seq data
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate classification of breast cancer subtypes from gene expression data is critical for diagnosis and treatment selection. However, such datasets are characterized by high dimensionality and limited sample size, posing challenges for machine learning models. In this study, we evaluate the impact of model complexity and feature selection on subtype classification performance using TCGA-BRCA gene expression data. Logistic regression, random forest, and support vector machine (SVM) models were trained using varying numbers of highly variable genes (50 to 20,518). Performance was evaluated using stratified 5-fold cross-validation and assessed with accuracy and macro F1 score. While all models achieved high accuracy, macro F1 analysis revealed substantial differences in subtype-level performance. Logistic regression demonstrated the most stable and balanced performance across subtypes, including improved detection of rare classes. Random forest underperformed on minority subtypes despite strong overall accuracy, while SVM showed sensitivity to feature dimensionality. These findings highlight the importance of model simplicity, evaluation metrics, and feature selection in high-dimensional biological classification tasks.

标题：使用TCGA-BRCA基因表达数据进行乳腺癌亚型分类时，特征维度优于模型复杂度
从基因表达数据中准确分类乳腺癌亚型对诊断和治疗选择至关重要。然而，此类数据集具有高维度和样本量有限的特点，给机器学习模型带来挑战。本研究利用TCGA-BRCA基因表达数据，评估了模型复杂度和特征选择对亚型分类性能的影响。采用逻辑回归、随机森林和支持向量机（SVM）模型，在不同数量的高变基因（50至20,518个）下进行训练。性能通过分层5折交叉验证评估，使用准确率和宏F1分数衡量。尽管所有模型均达到高准确率，但宏F1分析揭示了亚型层面的显著差异。逻辑回归在各亚型中表现最稳定且均衡，包括对罕见类别的检测能力提升。随机森林虽整体准确率高，但在少数亚型上表现不佳；SVM则对特征维度敏感。这些发现强调了在高维生物分类任务中，模型简洁性、评估指标和特征选择的重要性。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how model complexity and feature dimensionality affect breast cancer subtype classification using TCGA-BRCA gene expression data, motivated by the challenge of high dimensionality and small sample sizes in biological datasets. While all models achieved high overall accuracy, logistic regression consistently outperformed the others in subtype-level balance, especially for rare classes, whereas random forest showed poor performance on minority subtypes despite strong overall accuracy, and SVM was sensitive to feature dimensionality. Contrastive Image-Metadata Pre-Training for Materials Transmission Electron Microscopy
Authors: Georgia Channing, Debora Keller, Marta D. Rossell, Philip Torr, Stig Helveg, Henrik Eliasson
First: 2026-04-27T18:42:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:55:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The transmission electron microscope facilitates the highest-resolution imaging of any instrument ever created, and its limiting factor is no longer spatial resolution but dose efficiency. Low electron doses avoid sample damage but produce noisy images for which, unlike in classical computer vision, there is no ground truth. Autonomous materials experimentation poses a related problem, since closed-loop instruments need representations grounded in the microscope state at acquisition. Both demand representations grounded in how an image was acquired. We release 7,330 paired high-angle annular dark-field scanning-TEM (HAADF-STEM) images and their seven-dimensional acquisition metadata, and propose Contrastive Image-Metadata Pre-training (CIMP), a CLIP-style encoder that aligns the two modalities and reaches 84.4% Top-1 cross-modal retrieval on a held-out split. All seven parameters are individually recoverable from the frozen visual embedding through a linear probe, and we use the embedding to condition a metadata-conditioned style-transfer model that re-renders experimental images under different acquisition parameters. Virtually scaling dwell time and beam current of low-dose images turns this model into a physics-informed denoiser; in a blind user study, experimental microscopists prefer it over the current state-of-the-art denoiser for STEM imagery on 70.2% of trials.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于材料透射电子显微镜的对比图像-元数据预训练
透射电子显微镜是迄今为止分辨率最高的成像仪器，其限制因素已从空间分辨率转变为剂量效率。低电子剂量可避免样品损伤，但会产生噪声图像，而与经典计算机视觉不同，这些图像没有真实标签。自主材料实验也面临类似问题，因为闭环仪器需要基于显微镜采集状态的表示。两者均要求表示方式与图像采集方式相关。我们发布了7,330对高角度环形暗场扫描透射电子显微镜（HAADF-STEM）图像及其七维采集元数据，并提出对比图像-元数据预训练（CIMP），这是一种CLIP风格的编码器，对齐两种模态，在保留集上实现84.4%的跨模态检索Top-1准确率。所有七个参数均可通过冻结的视觉嵌入经线性探针单独恢复，我们利用该嵌入条件化一个元数据引导的风格迁移模型，以不同采集参数重新渲染实验图像。通过虚拟扩展低剂量图像的驻留时间和束流强度，该模型可转化为物理信息引导的去噪器；在盲测用户研究中，70.2%的实验显微镜专家更偏好该模型而非当前STEM图像去噪领域的最先进方法。

Motivated by the need for robust image representations in low-dose transmission electron microscopy where ground truth is absent, this work introduces Contrastive Image-Metadata Pre-training (CIMP), a CLIP-inspired model that aligns image and acquisition metadata modalities. 为了解决低剂量透射电子显微镜成像中缺乏真实标签的问题，该研究提出对比图像-元数据预训练（CIMP）方法，通过CLIP风格的编码器对图像与采集元数据进行对齐。在7,330组HAADF-STEM图像及其七维采集元数据上训练后，CIMP在跨模态检索中达到84.4%的Top-1准确率。冻结的视觉嵌入可经线性探针精确恢复所有采集参数，并用于条件化风格迁移模型，实现不同采集参数下的图像重渲染。当用于虚拟调整驻留时间和束流电流以降噪低剂量图像时，该模型在盲测中优于当前最先进去噪器，70.2%的实验人员更偏好其结果。
Mirror Descent-Ascent for mean-field min-max problems
Authors: Razvan-Andrei Lascu, Mateusz B. Majka, Łukasz Szpruch
First: 2024-02-12T22:52:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:54:55+00:00
Comments: 57 pages; substantially revised version with improved presentation, re-worked main theorems, and added numerical experiments
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study two variants of the mirror descent-ascent (MDA) algorithm for solving min-max problems on the space of measures: simultaneous and alternating. We work under assumptions of convexity-concavity and relative smoothness of the payoff function with respect to a suitable Bregman divergence, defined on the space of measures via flat derivatives. We establish non-asymptotic convergence rates to mixed Nash equilibria, measured in the Nikaidô-Isoda error, proving an $\mathcal{O}(N^{-1/2})$ rate for simultaneous MDA and an improved $\mathcal{O}(N^{-2/3})$ rate for alternating MDA. The main technical contribution is an infinite-dimensional dual space analysis that relates Bregman divergences on measures to dual Bregman divergences on spaces of bounded continuous functions, allowing us to control asymmetric commutator terms created by alternating updates. The results substantially generalize prior analyses restricted to bilinear objectives and also apply to nonlinear convex-concave problems on measure spaces, thereby providing a unified theoretical foundation for MDA in mean-field min-max optimization.

标题：测度空间上均值场极小-极大问题的镜像下降-上升算法
我们研究了镜像下降-上升（MDA）算法的两种变体——同步与交替更新，用于解决测度空间上的极小-极大问题。在假设收益函数关于某合适Bregman散度（通过平坦导数在测度空间上定义）满足凸-凹性与相对光滑性条件下，我们建立了收敛至混合纳什均衡的非渐近收敛速率，以Nikaidô-Isoda误差度量，证明同步MDA的速率为$\mathcal{O}(N^{-1/2})$，交替MDA的速率改进为$\mathcal{O}(N^{-2/3})$。主要技术贡献是无限维对偶空间分析，将测度空间上的Bregman散度与有界连续函数空间上的对偶Bregman散度关联起来，从而控制交替更新产生的非对称交换子项。结果显著推广了此前仅限于双线性目标的分析，同时适用于测度空间上的非线性凸-凹问题，为均值场极小-极大优化中的MDA提供了统一的理论基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient algorithms in mean-field min-max optimization, this paper analyzes two variants of mirror descent-ascent (MDA) for solving problems over measure spaces, assuming convex-concave structure and relative smoothness with respect to a Bregman divergence. The authors derive non-asymptotic convergence rates to mixed Nash equilibria measured by Nikaidô-Isoda error, showing an O(N^{-1/2}) rate for simultaneous MDA and an improved O(N^{-2/3}) rate for alternating MDA. The key methodological advance involves an infinite-dimensional dual space analysis that links Bregman divergences on measures to dual divergences on bounded continuous functions, enabling control of asymmetric commutator terms in alternating updates. 为解决测度空间上的均值场极小-极大优化问题，本文提出并分析了两种镜像下降-上升（MDA）算法变体：同步与交替更新。方法基于凸-凹结构与相对于Bregman散度的相对平滑性，并通过无限维对偶空间分析处理交替更新中产生的不对称交换子项。实验结果验证了理论收敛率：同步MDA收敛率为$\mathcal{O}(N^{-1/2})$，交替MDA收敛率更优，为$\mathcal{O}(N^{-2/3})$，且该方法适用于非线性凸-凹问题，超越了以往仅限于双线性目标的分析框架。
Optimal Counterfactual Search in Tree Ensembles: A Study Across Modeling and Solution Paradigms
Authors: Awa Khouna, Youssouf Emine, Julien Ferry, Thibaut Vidal
First: 2026-05-07T16:54:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:54:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Trust in counterfactual explanations depends critically on whether their recommended changes are truly minimal: suboptimal explanations may vastly overshoot the actual changes needed to alter a decision, and heuristic errors can affect individuals unevenly, giving some users relevant recourse while assigning others unnecessarily costly recommendations. Consequently, we study the problem of computing optimal counterfactual explanations for tree ensembles under plausibility and actionability constraints. This is a combinatorial problem: for a fixed model, counterfactual search boils down to selecting consistent branching decisions and threshold-defined regions under a distance objective. We exploit this structure through CPCF, a constraint programming (CP) formulation in which numerical features are encoded as interval domains induced by split thresholds, while discrete features retain native finite-domain representations. This yields a compact finite-domain formulation that supports multiple distance objectives without continuous split-boundary search. We then place CPCF in a broader comparison across mathematical programming paradigms: we extend a maximum Boolean satisfiability (MaxSAT) formulation, originally designed for hard-voting random forests, to soft-voting ensembles, and compare against the current state-of-the-art mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) optimal approach. Across ten datasets and three types of tree ensembles, we analyze scalability, anytime performance, and sensitivity to distance metrics. We observe that CP achieves the best overall performance. More importantly, our results identify regimes in which the specific strengths of each paradigm make it best suited: CP is most versatile overall, MaxSAT handles hard-voting ensembles particularly well, and MILP remains competitive in amortized inference settings with a moderate number of split levels.
中文标题/摘要
标题：树集成中最优反事实搜索：跨建模与求解范式的实证研究
对反事实解释的信任程度取决于其推荐的改变是否真正最小：次优解释可能远超实际所需改变，启发式误差会不均衡影响个体，使部分用户获得相关救济，而另一些用户则被赋予不必要的高成本建议。因此，我们研究在合理性与可操作性约束下，为树集成计算最优反事实解释的问题。这是一个组合优化问题：对于固定模型，反事实搜索等价于在距离目标下选择一致的分支决策和阈值定义区域。我们通过CPCF（约束规划）公式利用该结构，其中数值特征编码为由分割阈值诱导的区间域，离散特征保留原生有限域表示。这产生了一个紧凑的有限域公式，支持多种距离目标，无需连续分割边界搜索。随后，我们将CPCF置于更广泛的数学规划范式比较中：我们将最初为硬投票随机森林设计的最大布尔可满足性（MaxSAT）公式扩展至软投票集成，并与当前最先进的混合整数线性规划（MILP）最优方法进行比较。在十个数据集和三种树集成类型上，我们分析了可扩展性、任意时间性能及对距离度量的敏感性。我们观察到CP整体表现最佳。更重要的是，我们的结果识别出各范式最适用的场景：CP总体最灵活，MaxSAT特别擅长处理硬投票集成，而MILP在分割层级适中、需批量推理的场景中仍具竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for minimal and equitable counterfactual explanations in tree ensembles, this study proposes CPCF, a constraint programming formulation that encodes numerical features via interval domains derived from split thresholds and discrete features via native finite domains, enabling efficient optimization without continuous search. Evaluated against MaxSAT (extended for soft-voting ensembles) and MILP across ten datasets and three ensemble types, CPCF consistently outperforms alternatives in overall scalability and anytime performance, while revealing that CP excels broadly, MaxSAT suits hard-voting models, and MILP remains viable for amortized inference with moderate split levels.
为提升树集成模型中反事实解释的最小化与公平性，本文提出CPCF约束规划框架，通过将数值特征编码为由分割阈值诱导的区间域，保留离散特征的原生有限域表示，避免连续边界搜索，实现高效优化。在十组数据与三类集成模型上，与扩展至软投票的MaxSAT及当前最优的MILP方法对比，CPCF整体表现最佳，且揭示CP框架最通用，MaxSAT对硬投票森林最有效，MILP在中等分割层级的批量推理场景中仍具竞争力。
Dual Cross-Attention Siamese Transformer for Rectal Tumor Regrowth Assessment in Watch-and-Wait Endoscopy
Authors: Jorge Tapias Gomez, Despoina Kanata, Aneesh Rangnekar, Christina Lee, Julio Garcia-Aguilar, Joshua Jesse Smith, Harini Veeraraghavan
Venue: ISBI 2026
First: 2025-12-03T15:34:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:52:47+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ISBI 2026 conference (6 pages, 5 figures, 1 table)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Increasing evidence supports watch-and-wait (WW) surveillance for patients with rectal cancer who show clinical complete response (cCR) at restaging following total neoadjuvant treatment (TNT). However, objectively accurate methods to early detect local regrowth (LR) from follow-up endoscopy images during WW are essential to manage care and prevent distant metastases. Hence, we developed a Siamese Swin Transformer with Dual Cross-Attention (SSDCA) to combine longitudinal endoscopic images at restaging and follow-up and distinguish cCR from LR. SSDCA leverages pretrained Swin transformers to extract domain agnostic features and enhance robustness to imaging variations. Dual cross attention is implemented to emphasize features from the two scans without requiring any spatial alignment of images to predict response. SSDCA as well as Swin-based baselines were trained using image pairs from 135 patients and evaluated on a held-out set of image pairs from 62 patients. SSDCA produced the best balanced accuracy (81.76\% $\pm$ 0.04), sensitivity (90.07\% $\pm$ 0.08), and specificity (72.86\% $\pm$ 0.05). Robustness analysis showed stable performance irrespective of artifacts including blood, stool, telangiectasia, and poor image quality. UMAP clustering of extracted features showed maximal inter-cluster separation (1.45 $\pm$ 0.18) and minimal intra-cluster dispersion (1.07 $\pm$ 0.19) with SSDCA, confirming discriminative representation learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于观察等待内镜检查中直肠肿瘤复发评估的双交叉注意力孪生Transformer
越来越多的证据支持对接受全新辅助治疗（TNT）后临床完全缓解（cCR）的直肠癌患者实施观察等待（WW）监测。然而，在WW期间，从随访内镜图像中客观准确地早期检测局部复发（LR）对于管理治疗和预防远处转移至关重要。为此，我们开发了一种双交叉注意力孪生Swin Transformer（SSDCA），结合再分期和随访的纵向内镜图像，区分cCR与LR。SSDCA利用预训练的Swin Transformer提取领域无关特征，增强对成像变异的鲁棒性。通过双交叉注意力机制，无需图像空间对齐即可强调两幅图像的特征以预测治疗反应。SSDCA与基于Swin的基线模型均在135名患者的图像对上训练，并在62名患者的独立测试集上评估。SSDCA在平衡准确率（81.76% ± 0.04）、灵敏度（90.07% ± 0.08）和特异性（72.86% ± 0.05）上表现最佳。稳健性分析表明，即使存在血液、粪便、毛细血管扩张和图像质量差等伪影，其性能依然稳定。对提取特征进行UMAP聚类显示，SSDCA实现了最大类间分离（1.45 ± 0.18）和最小类内离散度（1.07 ± 0.19），证实了其判别性表征学习能力。

Motivated by the need for accurate early detection of rectal tumor regrowth in watch-and-wait endoscopy patients post-neoadjuvant therapy, this study introduces SSDCA—a Siamese Swin Transformer with Dual Cross-Attention—to analyze paired endoscopic images from restaging and follow-up visits. 为解决直肠癌患者接受新辅助治疗后临床完全缓解（cCR）阶段随访内镜检查中肿瘤复发的早期准确识别问题，本研究提出SSDCA（双交叉注意力双分支Swin Transformer）模型，通过结合初诊与随访内镜图像对，无需图像配准即可提取判别性特征。模型基于预训练Swin Transformer提取跨域鲁棒特征，并通过双交叉注意力机制强化图像对间的差异表达。在135例训练患者和62例验证患者图像对上测试，SSDCA表现最优，平衡准确率81.76%，敏感性90.07%，特异性72.86%，优于Swin基线模型。鲁棒性分析显示其在血迹、粪便、毛细血管扩张及图像质量差等干扰下仍保持稳定性能，UMAP聚类结果显示其特征空间具有最大类间分离度（1.45）与最小类内离散度（1.07），验证了其判别性表征能力。
Switchcodec: Adaptive residual-expert sparse quantization for high-fidelity neural audio coding
Authors: Xiangbo Wang, Wenbin Jiang, Jin Wang, Yubo You, Sheng Fang, Fei Wen
First: 2026-01-28T08:26:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:51:49+00:00
Comments: This manuscript contains critical errors in the experimental parameter settings and partial algorithm derivation in Section 3 and Section 4, which will lead to inaccurate conclusion interpretation. We need to withdraw the paper for comprehensive revision, re-calculation and experimental verification, and will resubmit after full correction
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent neural audio compression models often rely on residual vector quantization for high-fidelity coding, but using a fixed number of per-frame codebooks is suboptimal for the wide variability of audio content-especially for signals that are either very simple or highly complex. To address this limitation, we propose SwitchCodec, a neural audio codec based on Residual Experts Vector Quantization (REVQ). REVQ combines a shared quantizer with dynamically routed expert quantizers that are activated according to the input audio, decoupling bitrate from codebook capacity and improving compression efficiency. This design ensures full training and utilization of each quantizer. In addition, a variable-bitrate mechanism adjusts the number of active expert quantizers at inference, enabling multi-bitrate operation without retraining. Experiments demonstrate that SwitchCodec surpasses existing baselines on both objective metrics and subjective listening tests.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SwitchCodec：基于自适应残差专家稀疏量化实现高保真神经音频编码
近期神经音频压缩模型常依赖残差向量量化以实现高保真编码，但采用固定帧数码本数量对音频内容的广泛变异性（尤其是极简或极复杂信号）并不理想。为解决此问题，我们提出SwitchCodec，一种基于残差专家向量量化（REVQ）的神经音频编码器。REVQ结合共享量化器与根据输入音频动态路由的专家量化器，将码率与码本容量解耦，提升压缩效率。该设计确保每个量化器的充分训练与利用。此外，可变码率机制在推理时调整激活专家量化器数量，实现无需重训练的多码率操作。实验表明，SwitchCodec在客观指标与主观听感测试中均优于现有基线。

This architecture combines a shared quantizer with adaptive expert quantizers, enabling full utilization of each component during training and variable-bitrate operation at inference without retraining. Experimental results show SwitchCodec outperforms existing baselines on both objective metrics and subjective listening tests, though the paper contains critical errors in experimental settings and derivations that necessitate withdrawal for revision.
为解决固定码本残差向量量化在神经音频压缩中的效率不足问题，SwitchCodec提出基于残差专家向量量化（REVQ）的动态量化框架，结合共享量化器与根据输入音频内容动态激活的专家量化器，实现码本容量与码率解耦，并通过动态路由确保每个量化器充分训练与利用。此外，变码率机制支持推理时多码率操作而无需重新训练。实验表明，SwitchCodec在客观指标与主观听感测试中均优于现有基线，但论文存在实验参数设置与推导关键错误，需撤回修订后重新提交。
Importance-Guided Basis Selection for Low-Rank Decomposition of Large Language Models
Authors: Daniel Agyei Asante, Ernie Chang, Yang Li
First: 2026-05-02T22:35:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:51:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Low-rank decomposition is a compelling approach for compressing large language models, but its effectiveness hinges on selecting which singular-vector bases to retain for a target task. Existing methods such as Basel adapt singular-value coefficients on downstream data and prune bases with small re-learned magnitudes, a heuristic that can be misaligned with task performance because it ignores the local geometry of the loss landscape. We present Basis Selection with Importance (BSI), a principled low-rank compression framework that ranks and prunes bases by directly estimating the expected loss increase incurred when each basis is removed. BSI derives a derivative-based importance score from a second-order Taylor expansion of the task loss with respect to singular values, combining first-order sensitivity and second-order curvature to quantify pruning impact. To make this criterion practical for LLMs, we develop an efficient Hessian-diagonal estimator by adapting the Hutchinson randomized-probing method to loss curvature with symmetric parameter perturbations. We provide a comprehensive theoretical analysis, including loss-increase bounds under basis pruning, explicit propagation of Hessian-diagonal estimation error into these bounds, variance characterization tied to the Hessian spectrum, high-probability sample-complexity guarantees for achieving a target estimation accuracy, and guidance on perturbation intensity. Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that BSI consistently outperforms state-of-the-art low-rank decomposition baselines, with especially strong improvements under deep compression.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于重要性引导的基选择用于大语言模型的低秩分解
低秩分解是压缩大语言模型的一种有吸引力的方法，但其有效性取决于为特定任务选择保留哪些奇异向量基。现有方法如Basel通过在下游数据上适应奇异值系数，并剪枝重学幅度较小的基，这是一种启发式方法，可能与任务性能不一致，因为它忽略了损失景观的局部几何结构。我们提出了重要性引导的基选择（BSI），这是一种原理性的低秩压缩框架，通过直接估计移除每个基所导致的预期损失增加来对基进行排序和剪枝。BSI从任务损失关于奇异值的二阶泰勒展开中推导出基于导数的重要性分数，结合一阶敏感性和二阶曲率以量化剪枝影响。为使该准则适用于大语言模型，我们通过将Hutchinson随机探针方法适应于对称参数扰动下的损失曲率，开发了一种高效的Hessian对角线估计器。我们提供了全面的理论分析，包括基剪枝下的损失增加界、Hessian对角线估计误差对这些界的具体传播、与Hessian谱相关的方差特征、实现目标估计精度的高概率样本复杂度保证，以及扰动强度的指导。在数学推理基准上的大量实验表明，BSI在各项指标上持续优于现有最先进的低秩分解基线方法，尤其在深度压缩时表现尤为突出。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve task-specific compression of large language models via low-rank decomposition, this work introduces Basis Selection with Importance (BSI), which selects and prunes singular-vector bases by estimating the expected loss increase from removing each basis. BSI computes an importance score using a second-order Taylor expansion of the task loss, combining first-order sensitivity and second-order curvature to quantify pruning impact. To enable efficient computation, it adapts the Hutchinson method to estimate the diagonal of the Hessian via symmetric parameter perturbations. Theoretical analysis provides bounds on loss increase, error propagation, variance, and sample complexity. Experiments show BSI consistently outperforms existing baselines, especially under deep compression, achieving higher accuracy on mathematical reasoning benchmarks.
为提升大语言模型低秩压缩中对任务相关奇异向量基的选择效果，本文提出基于重要性基选择（BSI）方法，通过任务损失的二阶泰勒展开估计移除每个基所导致的预期损失增加，结合一阶敏感度与二阶曲率计算导数重要性得分，实现有原则的剪枝。为在大模型中高效估计Hessian对角线，该方法改进Hutchinson方法，采用对称参数扰动。理论分析给出了剪枝损失上界、误差传播、方差特性及采样复杂度保证。在数学推理基准上的实验表明，BSI在各类压缩深度下均显著优于现有基线方法。
Coordination Matters: Evaluation of Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Maria Ana Cardei, Matthew Landers, Afsaneh Doryab
First: 2026-05-07T16:50:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:50:53+00:00
Comments: 27 pages. Submitted and under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) benchmarks commonly emphasize aggregate outcomes such as return, success rate, or completion time. While essential, these metrics often fail to reveal how agents coordinate, particularly in settings where agents, tasks, and joint assignment choices scale combinatorially. We propose a coordination-aware evaluation perspective that supplements return with process-level diagnostics. We instantiate this perspective using STAT, a controlled commitment-constrained spatial task-allocation testbed that systematically varies agents, tasks, and environment size while holding observation access and task rules fixed. We evaluate six representative value-based MARL methods across varying levels of centralization. Our results show that similar return trends can reflect distinct coordination mechanisms, including differences in redundant assignment, assignment diversity, and task-completion efficiency. We find that in commitment-constrained task allocation, performance under scale is shaped not only by nominal action-space size, but also by assignment pressure, sparse decision opportunities, and redundant choices among interdependent agents. Our findings motivate coordination-aware evaluation as a necessary complement to return-based benchmarking for cooperative MARL.
中文标题/摘要
标题：协调至关重要：合作多智能体强化学习的评估
合作多智能体强化学习（MARL）基准通常强调聚合结果，如回报、成功率或完成时间。虽然这些指标至关重要，但往往无法揭示智能体如何协调，特别是在智能体、任务和联合分配选择呈组合式扩展的场景中。我们提出一种协调感知的评估视角，通过过程级诊断补充回报指标。我们使用STAT（受约束承诺的空间任务分配测试平台）来实例化该视角，该平台在固定观测权限和任务规则的前提下，系统性地变化智能体数量、任务数量和环境规模。我们在不同集中化水平下评估六种代表性基于价值的MARL方法。结果表明，相似的回报趋势可能反映不同的协调机制，包括冗余分配、分配多样性及任务完成效率的差异。我们发现，在承诺约束的任务分配中，性能随规模的变化不仅取决于名义动作空间大小，还受分配压力、稀疏决策机会及相互依赖智能体间冗余选择的影响。我们的研究结果表明，协调感知评估是合作MARL回报基准评估的必要补充。
