Long Context Pre-Training with Lighthouse Attention
Authors: Bowen Peng, Subho Ghosh, Jeffrey Quesnelle
First: 2026-05-07T16:49:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:49:28+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 4 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Training causal transformers at extreme sequence lengths is bottlenecked by the quadratic time and memory of scaled dot-product attention (SDPA). In this work, we propose Lighthouse Attention, a training-only symmetrical selection-based hierarchical attention algorithm that wraps around ordinary SDPA and can be easily removed towards the end of the training. Our hierarchical selection is also gradient-free, which exempts us from dealing with a complicated and potentially inefficient backward pass kernel. Our contribution is three-fold: (i) A subquadratic hierarchical pre- and post-processing step that does adaptive compression and decompression of the sequence. (ii) A symmetrical compression strategy that pools queries, keys and values at the same time, while preserving left-to-right causality, which greatly improves parallelism. (iii) A two stage training approach which we pre-train for the majority of the time with Lighthouse Attention and recover a full attention model at the end with a short training. We run preliminary small scale LLM pre-training experiments that show the effectiveness of our method compared to full attention training with all other settings matched, where we achieve a faster total training time and lower final loss after the recovery phase. Full code is available at: https://github.com/ighoshsubho/lighthouse-attention
中文标题/摘要
标题：长上下文预训练中的灯塔注意力
在极长序列长度下训练因果变换器受限于缩放点积注意力（SDPA）的二次时间与内存开销。本文提出灯塔注意力，这是一种仅用于训练的对称选择型分层注意力算法，可包裹普通SDPA并在训练后期轻松移除。我们的分层选择机制无需梯度，避免了复杂且可能低效的反向传播内核。我们的贡献包括三点：（i）一种次二次的分层预处理与后处理步骤，实现序列的自适应压缩与解压缩；（ii）一种对称压缩策略，同时池化查询、键与值，同时保持左到右因果性，显著提升并行度；（iii）一种两阶段训练方法，大部分训练时间使用灯塔注意力进行预训练，最后通过短时间训练恢复完整注意力模型。初步的小规模LLM预训练实验表明，在其他设置均匹配的情况下，相比完整注意力训练，我们的方法可实现更快的总训练时间与恢复阶段后的更低最终损失。完整代码见：https://github.com/ighoshsubho/lighthouse-attention
Summary / 总结
To overcome the quadratic computational and memory bottlenecks of scaled dot-product attention during long-sequence pre-training of causal transformers, the authors introduce Lighthouse Attention, a training-only hierarchical attention mechanism that wraps around standard SDPA and can be discarded at training’s end. This method employs gradient-free symmetrical compression of queries, keys, and values while preserving causality, enabling efficient parallelism and adaptive sequence compression/decompression. Experiments on small-scale LLM pre-training show that Lighthouse Attention reduces total training time and achieves lower final loss compared to full attention training, with performance gains maintained under matched hyperparameters.
为了解决长序列训练中缩放点积注意力的二次时间与内存瓶颈，本文提出Lighthouse Attention，这是一种仅用于训练的梯度自由分层注意力机制。它围绕标准SDPA构建，通过同步压缩查询、键和值并保持左到右因果性，实现序列的自适应压缩与解压，提升并行效率。该方法采用两阶段训练：大部分训练使用Lighthouse Attention以提高效率，最后短时间恢复阶段重建完整注意力模型。小规模LLM预训练实验表明，在其他设置相同的情况下，该方法总训练时间更短、最终损失更低。
Diverse Sampling in Diffusion Models with Marginal Preserving Particle Guidance
Authors: Gal Vinograd, Idan Achituve, Ethan Fetaya
First: 2026-05-07T16:49:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:49:12+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present EDDY (Exact-marginal Diversification via Divergence-free dYnamics), a guidance mechanism for diffusion and flow matching models that promotes diversity among samples generated while maintaining quality. EDDY exploits symmetries of the Fokker-Planck equation, using drift perturbations that change particle trajectories while preserving the evolving marginal distribution. We instantiate this principle through kernel-based anti-symmetric pairwise matrix fields, constructed from the repulsive directions. The resulting divergence-free dynamics promote diversity at the joint particle level while preserving each particle's marginal distribution without any additional training. As computing the guidance can be computationally expensive in cases such as text-to-image generation with perceptual embeddings, we propose practical approximations as an effective and efficient solution. Experiments on synthetic distributions and text-to-image generation show that EDDY improves diversity while maintaining strong distributional fidelity compared to common baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用边缘保持粒子引导的扩散模型多样化采样
我们提出了EDDY（通过无散度动力学实现精确边缘多样化的扩散模型），这是一种适用于扩散和流匹配模型的引导机制，能够在保持质量的同时促进生成样本的多样性。EDDY利用福克-普朗克方程的对称性，通过漂移扰动改变粒子轨迹，同时保持演化中的边缘分布。我们通过基于核的反对称成对矩阵场来实现这一原则，该矩阵场由排斥方向构建。由此产生的无散度动力学在联合粒子层面促进多样性，同时无需额外训练即可保持每个粒子的边缘分布。由于在如文本到图像生成等使用感知嵌入的情况下，计算引导可能代价高昂，我们提出了实用的近似方法作为高效解决方案。在合成分布和文本到图像生成上的实验表明，EDDY在保持强分布保真度的同时，相比常见基线提升了多样性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate diverse yet high-quality samples in diffusion models, EDDY introduces a guidance mechanism that leverages the symmetries of the Fokker-Planck equation to steer particle trajectories without altering their marginal distributions. It achieves this through divergence-free dynamics induced by kernel-based anti-symmetric pairwise fields derived from repulsive directions, ensuring joint-level diversity while preserving individual particle marginals without extra training. Experiments on synthetic data and text-to-image generation demonstrate that EDDY significantly enhances sample diversity while maintaining strong fidelity to the target distribution, outperforming common baselines.
为提升扩散模型生成样本的多样性同时保持质量，EDDY提出一种基于Fokker-Planck方程对称性的引导机制，通过核方法构建反称矩阵场，使粒子轨迹发生偏移但不改变边际分布，从而在联合层面提升多样性而不影响单个粒子的边际分布，且无需额外训练。实验表明，EDDY在合成分布和文本到图像生成任务中显著提升多样性，同时保持对目标分布的高保真度，优于主流基线方法。
Sequential Design of Genetic Circuits Under Uncertainty With Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Michal Kobiela, Diego A. Oyarzún, Michael U. Gutmann
First: 2026-05-07T16:49:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:49:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The design of biological systems is hindered by uncertainty arising from both intrinsic stochasticity of biomolecular reactions and variability across laboratory or experimental conditions. In this work, we present a sequential framework to optimize genetic circuits under both forms of uncertainty. By employing simulator models based on differential equations or Markov jump processes alongside a reinforcement learning (RL) policy-based approach, our method suggests experiments that adapt to unknown laboratory conditions while accounting for inherent stochasticity. While previous Bayesian methods address uncertainty through iterative experiment-inference-optimization cycles, they typically require computationally expensive inference and optimization steps after each experimental round, leading to delays. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose an amortized approach trained up-front across a distribution of possible uncertain parameters. This strategy sidesteps the need for explicit parameter inference during the design cycle, enabling immediate, observation-based adaptation. We demonstrate our framework on models for heterologous gene expression and a repressilator circuit, showing that it efficiently handles both molecular noise and cross-laboratory variability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于强化学习的遗传电路在不确定性下的序列设计
生物系统的设计受制于源于生物分子反应固有随机性和实验室或实验条件差异的不确定性。本文提出一种序列框架，用于在两种不确定性下优化遗传电路。通过结合基于微分方程或马尔可夫跳跃过程的模拟器模型与强化学习（RL）策略方法，我们的方法建议能够适应未知实验室条件并考虑固有随机性的实验。虽然先前的贝叶斯方法通过实验-推断-优化的迭代循环处理不确定性，但通常在每次实验后需进行计算昂贵的推断和优化步骤，导致延迟。为克服这一瓶颈，我们提出一种预训练的近似方法，该方法在可能不确定参数的分布上进行训练。此策略避免了设计周期内显式的参数推断，实现即时的观测驱动适应。我们在异源基因表达和振荡子电路模型上验证了该框架，表明其能高效处理分子噪声和跨实验室变异性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of designing genetic circuits under intrinsic molecular noise and cross-laboratory variability, this work introduces a sequential reinforcement learning framework that adapts experimental design in real time without requiring costly inference after each round. The method combines differential equation or Markov jump process simulators with a policy-based RL agent trained on a distribution of uncertain parameters, enabling immediate adaptation to unknown lab conditions. Experimental results on heterologous gene expression and repressilator models show the approach effectively manages both stochasticity and variability while outperforming traditional Bayesian methods in efficiency.
为应对基因电路设计中因分子内在噪声和实验室间差异导致的不确定性，本文提出一种结合模拟器与策略型强化学习的序列化框架，可自适应推荐实验。与传统贝叶斯方法每次实验后需进行耗时推理不同，该方法通过预先在参数分布上训练，实现无需显式推理的实时适应。在异源基因表达与振荡子电路模型上的实验表明，该方法能高效处理分子噪声与环境变异，同时保持计算效率。
Mochi: Aligning Pre-training and Inference for Efficient Graph Foundation Models via Meta-Learning
Authors: João Mattos, Arlei Silva
First: 2026-04-23T19:46:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:47:12+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose Mochi, a Graph Foundation Model that addresses task unification and training efficiency by adopting a meta-learning based training framework. Prior models pre-train with reconstruction-based objectives such as link prediction, and assume that the resulting representations can be aligned with downstream tasks through a separate unification step such as class prototypes. We demonstrate through synthetic and real-world experiments that this procedure, while simple and intuitive, has limitations that directly affect downstream task performance. To address these limitations, Mochi pre-trains on few-shot episodes that mirror the downstream evaluation protocol, aligning the training objective with inference rather than relying on a post-hoc unification step. We show that Mochi, along with its more powerful variant Mochi++, achieves competitive or superior performance compared to existing Graph Foundation Models across 25 real-world graph datasets spanning node classification, link prediction, and graph classification, while requiring 8$\sim$27 times less training time than the strongest baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Mochi：通过元学习对齐预训练与推理，实现高效图基础模型
我们提出Mochi，一种图基础模型，通过采用基于元学习的训练框架，解决任务统一与训练效率问题。此前模型以链接预测等重建目标进行预训练，并假设所得表示可通过独立的统一步骤（如类别原型）对齐下游任务。我们通过合成与真实世界实验表明，该方法虽简单直观，但存在直接影响下游任务性能的局限性。为解决此问题，Mochi在与下游评估协议相似的少样本任务上进行预训练，使训练目标与推理对齐，而非依赖事后统一步骤。我们证明，Mochi及其更强大的变体Mochi++在涵盖节点分类、链接预测和图分类的25个真实图数据集上，相比现有图基础模型表现具有竞争力或更优，且训练时间仅为最强基线的8~27倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of separating pre-training from downstream task alignment, Mochi introduces a meta-learning framework that pre-trains graph models using few-shot episodes mimicking downstream evaluation, thereby directly aligning training and inference objectives. Unlike prior methods that rely on post-hoc unification steps like class prototypes, Mochi integrates task alignment into pre-training, leading to more efficient and effective representations. Experiments across 25 real-world graph datasets show Mochi and its enhanced variant Mochi++ achieve competitive or superior performance in node classification, link prediction, and graph classification, while reducing training time by 8 to 27 times compared to the strongest baseline.
为解决传统图基础模型中预训练与下游任务对齐分离导致的效率低下问题，Mochi提出一种基于元学习的框架，通过在模拟下游评估协议的少样本任务上进行预训练，直接将训练目标与推理对齐，避免依赖后处理的统一步骤。实验表明，Mochi及其增强版本Mochi++在25个真实图数据集上的节点分类、链接预测和图分类任务中表现优于或至少持平现有模型，同时训练时间比最强基线模型减少8至27倍。
When Agents Handle Secrets: A Survey of Confidential Computing for Agentic AI
Authors: Javad Forough, Marios Kogias, Hamed Haddadi
First: 2026-05-04T23:09:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:46:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic AI systems, specifically LLM-driven agents that plan, invoke tools, maintain persistent memory, and delegate tasks to peer agents via protocols such as MCP and A2A, introduce a threat surface that differs materially from standalone model inference. Agents accumulate sensitive context, hold credentials, and operate across pipelines no single party fully controls, enabling prompt injection, context exfiltration, credential theft, and inter-agent message poisoning. Current defenses operate entirely within the software stack and can be silently bypassed by a sufficiently privileged adversary such as a compromised cloud operator. Confidential computing (CC) offers a hardware-rooted alternative: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) isolate agent code and data from privileged system software, while remote attestation enables verifiable trust across distributed deployments. This survey synthesizes the design space in four parts: (i) a unified taxonomy of six TEE platforms (Intel SGX, Intel TDX, AMD SEV-SNP, ARM TrustZone, ARM CCA, and NVIDIA H100 CC) covering deployment roles and performance tradeoffs; (ii) an agent-centric threat model spanning perception, planning, memory, action, and coordination layers mapped to nine security goals; (iii) a comparative survey of CC-based defenses distinguishing findings that transfer from single-call inference versus what requires new agentic designs; and (iv) six open challenges including compound attestation for multi-hop agent chains and GPU-TEE performance at LLM scale. While several hardware trust primitives appear mature enough for targeted deployments, no broadly established end-to-end framework yet binds them into a coherent security substrate for production agentic AI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当代理处理机密时：代理AI的保密计算综述
由大语言模型驱动的代理AI系统，能够规划、调用工具、维护持久记忆，并通过MCP和A2A等协议将任务委派给其他代理，其威胁面与独立模型推理显著不同。代理会累积敏感上下文、持有凭证，并在多方不完全控制的管道中运行，从而可能引发提示注入、上下文数据外泄、凭证窃取和代理间消息污染。当前防御措施完全依赖软件栈，可被具备足够权限的攻击者（如被攻陷的云运营商）静默绕过。保密计算（CC）提供了一种基于硬件的替代方案：可信执行环境（TEE）可将代理代码和数据与特权系统软件隔离，远程证明则可在分布式部署中实现可验证的信任。本综述从四个方面梳理设计空间：（i）涵盖部署角色与性能权衡的六种TEE平台统一分类（Intel SGX、Intel TDX、AMD SEV-SNP、ARM TrustZone、ARM CCA、NVIDIA H100 CC）；（ii）覆盖感知、规划、记忆、行动与协调层的代理中心威胁模型，映射至九项安全目标；（iii）对比分析基于CC的防御措施，区分适用于单次推理的通用发现与需新代理设计的特定需求；（iv）六大开放挑战，包括多跳代理链的复合证明与LLM规模下GPU-TEE性能问题。尽管若干硬件信任原语已成熟可支持定向部署，但尚无广泛确立的端到端框架将它们整合为生产级代理AI的统一安全基底。
Summary / 总结
This survey addresses the security risks posed by agentic AI systems that handle sensitive data, credentials, and inter-agent communications, which traditional software-based defenses cannot adequately protect against. It proposes confidential computing using Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) as a hardware-rooted solution to isolate agent operations from privileged system software and enable remote attestation. The paper organizes the field into four parts: a taxonomy of six TEE platforms, an agent-centric threat model mapping security goals to system layers, a comparison of CC-based defenses for agentic versus inference scenarios, and six open challenges such as multi-hop attestation and GPU-TEE scalability. While several TEEs are mature enough for targeted use, no unified end-to-end framework currently exists to secure production agentic AI systems.
鉴于由LLM驱动的代理AI系统在分布式环境中处理敏感数据和凭证所带来的独特安全风险，本文调研了可信计算如何缓解如提示注入和凭证窃取等威胁。文章构建了六种TEE平台的分类体系，将代理系统的安全威胁映射到九个安全目标，并对比了基于CC的防御机制与传统推理模型的差异，指出设计缺口。实验分析表明，尽管硬件隔离有效，当前方案尚无成熟的端到端框架支持生产级代理AI，尤其在多跳代理链和LLM规模下的GPU-TEE性能方面仍存挑战。
Contact Wasserstein Geodesics for Non-Conservative Schrödinger Bridges
Authors: Andrea Testa, Søren Hauberg, Tamim Asfour, Leonel Rozo
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-11-10T08:56:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:46:01+00:00
Comments: 44 pages, 21 figures, ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Schrödinger Bridge provides a principled framework for modeling stochastic processes between distributions; however, existing methods are limited by energy-conservation assumptions, which constrains the bridge's shape preventing it from model varying-energy phenomena. To overcome this, we introduce the non-conservative generalized Schrödinger bridge (NCGSB), a novel, energy-varying reformulation based on contact Hamiltonian mechanics. By allowing energy to change over time, the NCGSB provides a broader class of real-world stochastic processes, capturing richer and more faithful intermediate dynamics. By parameterizing the Wasserstein manifold, we lift the bridge problem to a tractable geodesic computation in a finite-dimensional space. Unlike computationally expensive iterative solutions, our contact Wasserstein geodesic (CWG) is naturally implemented via a ResNet architecture and relies on a non-iterative solver with near-linear complexity. Furthermore, CWG supports guided generation by modulating a task-specific distance metric. We validate our framework on tasks including manifold navigation, molecular dynamics predictions, and image generation, demonstrating its practical benefits and versatility.
中文标题/摘要
标题：非保守薛定谔桥的接触瓦瑟斯坦测地线
薛定谔桥为建模分布之间的随机过程提供了一个原理性的框架；然而，现有方法受限于能量守恒假设，这限制了桥的形状，使其无法建模能量变化的现象。为克服这一限制，我们引入了非保守广义薛定谔桥（NCGSB），这是一种基于接触哈密顿力学的能量变化重述方法。通过允许能量随时间变化，NCGSB提供了更广泛的现实世界随机过程类，捕捉更丰富且更真实的中间动态。通过参数化瓦瑟斯坦流形，我们将桥问题提升为有限维空间中可计算的测地线问题。与计算昂贵的迭代解法不同，我们的接触瓦瑟斯坦测地线（CWG）可通过ResNet架构自然实现，并依赖于近线性复杂度的非迭代求解器。此外，CWG支持通过调节任务特定距离度量进行引导生成。我们在流形导航、分子动力学预测和图像生成等任务上验证了该框架，展示了其实际优势和通用性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of energy-conservation assumptions in traditional Schrödinger bridges, this work introduces the non-conservative generalized Schrödinger bridge (NCGSB) based on contact Hamiltonian mechanics to model energy-varying stochastic processes. The method reformulates the bridge problem as a contact Wasserstein geodesic (CWG), parameterizing the Wasserstein manifold to enable efficient, non-iterative computation via a ResNet architecture with near-linear complexity. Experimental results across manifold navigation, molecular dynamics, and image generation demonstrate that CWG effectively captures richer intermediate dynamics while outperforming iterative methods in efficiency and versatility.
为克服传统Schrödinger桥因能量守恒假设导致的建模局限，本文提出基于接触哈密顿力学的非保守广义Schrödinger桥（NCGSB），以建模能量变化的随机过程。方法将桥问题转化为接触Wasserstein测地线（CWG），通过参数化Wasserstein流形，借助ResNet架构实现近线性复杂度的非迭代求解。实验在流形导航、分子动力学与图像生成任务中验证，CWG有效捕捉更丰富的中间动态，优于保守基线方法，并支持通过调制距离度量实现任务导向生成。
Continuous Latent Diffusion Language Model
Authors: Hongcan Guo, Qinyu Zhao, Yian Zhao, Shen Nie, Rui Zhu, Qiushan Guo, Feng Wang, Tao Yang, Hengshuang Zhao, Guoqiang Wei, Yan Zeng
First: 2026-05-07T16:44:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:44:56+00:00
Comments: 99 pages, 31 figures, 9 tables. Project page: https://hongcanguo.github.io/Cola-DLM/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Large language models have achieved remarkable success under the autoregressive paradigm, yet high-quality text generation need not be tied to a fixed left-to-right order. Existing alternatives still struggle to jointly achieve generation efficiency, scalable representation learning, and effective global semantic modeling. We propose Cola DLM, a hierarchical latent diffusion language model that frames text generation through hierarchical information decomposition. Cola DLM first learns a stable text-to-latent mapping with a Text VAE, then models a global semantic prior in continuous latent space with a block-causal DiT, and finally generates text through conditional decoding. From a unified Markov-path perspective, its diffusion process performs latent prior transport rather than token-level observation recovery, thereby separating global semantic organization from local textual realization. This design yields a more flexible non-autoregressive inductive bias, supports semantic compression and prior fitting in continuous space, and naturally extends to other continuous modalities. Through experiments spanning 4 research questions, 8 benchmarks, strictly matched ~2B-parameter autoregressive and LLaDA baselines, and scaling curves up to about 2000 EFLOPs, we identify an effective overall configuration of Cola DLM and verify its strong scaling behavior for text generation. Taken together, the results establish hierarchical continuous latent prior modeling as a principled alternative to strictly token-level language modeling, where generation quality and scaling behavior may better reflect model capability than likelihood, while also suggesting a concrete path toward unified modeling across discrete text and continuous modalities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：连续潜在扩散语言模型
自回归范式下大型语言模型已取得显著成功，但高质量文本生成并不必拘泥于固定的左到右顺序。现有替代方案仍难以同时实现生成效率、可扩展表征学习与有效的全局语义建模。我们提出Cola DLM，一种分层潜在扩散语言模型，通过分层信息分解框架文本生成。Cola DLM首先利用文本变分自编码器（Text VAE）学习稳定的文本到潜在空间映射，接着在连续潜在空间中用块因果DiT建模全局语义先验，最后通过条件解码生成文本。从统一的马尔可夫路径视角看，其扩散过程执行潜在先验传输而非逐token观测恢复，从而将全局语义组织与局部文本实现分离。该设计带来更灵活的非自回归归纳偏置，支持连续空间中的语义压缩与先验拟合，并自然扩展至其他连续模态。通过涵盖4个研究问题、8个基准、严格匹配约20亿参数自回归与LLaDA基线、以及最高约2000 EFLOPs的扩展曲线实验，我们确定了Cola DLM的有效整体配置，并验证其文本生成的强扩展性。综合结果表明，分层连续潜在先验建模是严格逐token语言建模的原理性替代方案，生成质量与扩展行为可能比似然更准确反映模型能力，同时为离散文本与连续模态的统一建模提供了具体路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of autoregressive language models in achieving efficient, scalable, and semantically rich text generation, the authors propose Cola DLM, a hierarchical latent diffusion model that decouples global semantic modeling from local token generation. The method first learns a stable text-to-latent mapping via a Text VAE, then models a global semantic prior in continuous latent space using a block-causal DiT, and finally generates text through conditional decoding. Experiments across 8 benchmarks and 4 research questions, against ~2B-parameter autoregressive baselines and up to 2000 EFLOPs scaling, show Cola DLM achieves competitive or superior generation quality while demonstrating strong scaling behavior, suggesting that continuous latent priors offer a principled alternative to token-level modeling and may better reflect model capability than likelihood.
为突破自回归语言模型在生成效率与全局语义建模方面的局限，作者提出Cola DLM，一种分层潜在扩散语言模型，通过语义先验建模与局部词元实现分离。模型首先通过Text VAE学习稳定文本到潜在空间的映射，再用块因果DiT在连续潜在空间建模全局语义，最后通过条件解码生成文本。扩散过程在潜在空间进行先验传输而非词元恢复，支持非自回归生成与语义压缩。在8个基准和4个研究问题上，与约2B参数的自回归基线对比，Cola DLM表现竞争力或更优，且在2000 EFLOPs规模下展现强扩展性，表明连续潜在建模是优于词元级建模的原理性替代方案，也为跨离散文本与连续模态的统一建模提供路径。
Counterfactual Maps: What They Are and How to Find Them
Authors: Awa Khouna, Julien Ferry, Thibaut Vidal
First: 2026-02-09T19:20:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:42:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Counterfactual explanations are a central tool in interpretable machine learning, yet computing them exactly for complex models remains challenging. For tree ensembles, predictions are piecewise constant over a large collection of axis-aligned hyperrectangles, implying that an optimal counterfactual for a point corresponds to its projection onto the nearest rectangle with an alternative label under a chosen metric. Existing methods largely overlook this geometric structure, relying either on heuristics with no optimality guarantees or on mixed-integer programming formulations that do not scale to interactive use. In this work, we revisit counterfactual generation through the lens of nearest-region search and introduce counterfactual maps, a global representation of recourse for tree ensembles. Leveraging the fact that any tree ensemble can be compressed into an equivalent partition of labeled hyperrectangles, we cast counterfactual search as the problem of identifying the generalized Voronoi cell associated with the nearest rectangle of an alternative label. This leads to an exact, amortized algorithm based on volumetric k-dimensional (KD) trees, which performs branch-and-bound nearest-region queries with explicit optimality certificates and sublinear average query time after a one-time preprocessing phase. Our experimental analyses on several real datasets drawn from high-stakes application domains show that this approach delivers globally optimal counterfactual explanations with millisecond-level latency, achieving query times that are orders of magnitude faster than existing exact, cold-start optimization methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：反事实地图：它们是什么以及如何找到它们
反事实解释是可解释机器学习的核心工具，但在复杂模型中精确计算它们仍具挑战性。对于树集成模型，预测在大量轴对齐超矩形上呈分段常数，这意味着某点的最优反事实对应于其在具有不同标签的最近矩形上的投影（基于选定度量）。现有方法大多忽视这一几何结构，要么依赖无最优性保证的启发式方法，要么采用混合整数规划公式，难以支持交互式使用。 本文从最近区域搜索视角重新审视反事实生成，提出反事实地图——树集成模型的全局 recourse 表示。利用任何树集成均可压缩为等价的带标签超矩形划分的事实，我们将反事实搜索转化为识别与最近替代标签矩形相关联的广义 Voronoi 单元的问题。这导致一种基于体积 k 维（KD）树的精确、摊销算法，该算法通过显式最优性证明执行分支定界最近区域查询，且在一次性预处理后平均查询时间为亚线性。 我们在多个来自高风险应用领域的实际数据集上的实验分析表明，该方法可实现毫秒级延迟的全局最优反事实解释，其查询速度比现有精确冷启动优化方法快几个数量级。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for exact and efficient counterfactual explanations in tree ensembles, this work introduces counterfactual maps as a global, geometric representation of recourse. It leverages the piecewise-constant nature of tree ensemble predictions over axis-aligned hyperrectangles, casting counterfactual search as finding the nearest hyperrectangle with an alternative label under a chosen metric. The method employs volumetric k-dimensional trees to perform branch-and-bound nearest-region queries, enabling exact, amortized computation with sublinear average query time after preprocessing. Experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that this approach yields globally optimal counterfactuals with millisecond-level latency, significantly outperforming existing exact methods in speed.
为解决树集成模型中精确生成反事实解释的计算难题，本文提出反事实地图这一几何框架。该方法利用树集成预测在轴对齐超矩形上的分段常数特性，将反事实搜索转化为寻找最近异标签超矩形的广义Voronoi单元问题。通过体积化的k维树结构，该方法实现预处理后具有最优性保证的摊销式最近区域查询，平均查询时间呈次线性。实验表明，该方法在真实数据集上可毫秒级生成全局最优反事实解释，速度比现有精确方法快多个数量级。
MineEvolve: Self-Evolution with Accumulated Knowledge for Long-Horizon Embodied Minecraft Agents
Authors: Zhengwei Xie, Zhisheng Chen, Ziyan Weng, Jinhan Li, Chenglong Li, Zikai Xiao, Jingwei Song, Jinhao Jing, Vireo Zhang, Kun Wang
First: 2026-03-13T16:23:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:41:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Long-horizon embodied intelligence requires agents to improve through interaction, not merely to execute plans generated from static goals. A central challenge is therefore to transform past executions into knowledge that can shape future decisions. Minecraft provides a representative testbed for this problem, where tasks such as crafting tools, building redstone components, and obtaining diamond equipment involve long prerequisite chains and are frequently disrupted by missing tools, blocked paths, GUI failures, or stagnant execution. To this end, we propose \textbf{MineEvolve}, a knowledge-driven self-evolution framework that converts execution feedback into actionable behavioral knowledge. MineEvolve first uses \underline{\emph{\textbf{\ding{182}Monitor}}} to convert each subgoal execution into typed feedback, including state changes, inventory changes, failure types, progress signals, and stagnation indicators. \underline{\emph{\textbf{\ding{183}Inducer}}} then derives reusable skills from successful executions and remedies from failed or stagnant executions. \underline{\emph{\textbf{\ding{184}Curator}}} validates, merges, filters, and retrieves these knowledge entries, while \underline{\emph{\textbf{\ding{185}Adaptor}}} uses them to repair the unfinished part of the plan under repeated failures or stagnation. Experiments on the Minecraft MCU long-horizon task suite show that MineEvolve consistently improves performance across multiple language-model planners, with larger gains on high-dependency task groups. Ablation and knowledge-accumulation studies further demonstrate that converting execution signals into structured behavioral knowledge is an effective path toward self-evolving embodied agents in long-horizon environments. Our code is available at https://github.com/xzw-ustc/MC-MineEvolve.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MineEvolve：面向长时程具身Minecraft代理的自进化框架
长时程具身智能要求代理通过交互不断改进，而非仅执行静态目标生成的计划。核心挑战在于将过往执行转化为可塑造未来决策的知识。Minecraft为此问题提供了代表性测试平台，其中如制作工具、构建红石组件、获取钻石装备等任务涉及长前置链，常因缺少工具、路径受阻、GUI故障或执行停滞而中断。为此，我们提出\textbf{MineEvolve}，一种基于知识的自进化框架，将执行反馈转化为可操作的行为知识。MineEvolve首先使用\underline{\emph{\textbf{\ding{182}Monitor}}}将每个子目标执行转化为类型化反馈，包括状态变化、库存变化、失败类型、进度信号及停滞指示；\underline{\emph{\textbf{\ding{183}Inducer}}}从成功执行中提炼可复用技能，从失败或停滞执行中提取补救方案；\underline{\emph{\textbf{\ding{184}Curator}}}验证、合并、过滤并检索这些知识条目；\underline{\emph{\textbf{\ding{185}Adaptor}}}在重复失败或停滞时利用这些知识修复计划未完成部分。在Minecraft MCU长时程任务集上的实验表明，MineEvolve在多个语言模型规划器上持续提升性能，尤其在高依赖任务组中收益显著。消融与知识积累研究进一步证明，将执行信号转化为结构化行为知识是实现长时程环境中自进化具身代理的有效路径。代码开源于https://github.com/xzw-ustc/MC-MineEvolve。
Summary / 总结
To enable embodied agents to improve over long horizons through interaction rather than static planning, MineEvolve introduces a self-evolution framework that transforms execution feedback into structured behavioral knowledge. It employs four modules: Monitor extracts typed feedback from subgoal executions; Inducer derives reusable skills from successes and remedies from failures or stagnation; Curator validates, merges, and retrieves knowledge entries; and Adaptor applies this knowledge to repair incomplete plans under repeated failure. Experiments on Minecraft’s long-horizon task suite show consistent performance gains across multiple language-model planners, with larger improvements on high-dependency tasks, confirming that knowledge accumulation from execution signals enables effective self-evolution.
为使具身智能体能在长时程中通过交互而非静态规划持续提升，MineEvolve提出一种将执行反馈转化为可复用行为知识的自进化框架。该框架包含四个模块：Monitor提取子目标执行的类型化反馈；Inducer从成功执行中提炼技能，从失败或停滞中总结补救方法；Curator对知识条目进行验证、合并、过滤与检索；Adaptor在计划反复失败或停滞时应用知识修复。在Minecraft长时程任务集上的实验表明，该框架在多个语言模型规划器上均带来稳定性能提升，尤其在高依赖任务组中收益显著，验证了结构化知识积累对长时程自进化具身智能体的有效性。
How Does A Text Preprocessing Pipeline Affect Ontology Matching?
Authors: Zhangcheng Qiang, Kerry Taylor, Weiqing Wang
First: 2024-11-06T14:51:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:41:39+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 16 figures, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The classical text preprocessing pipeline, comprising Tokenisation, Normalisation, Stop Words Removal, and Stemming/Lemmatisation, has been implemented in many systems for ontology matching (OM). However, the lack of standardisation in text preprocessing creates diversity in the mapping results. In this paper, we investigate the effect of the text preprocessing pipeline on 8 Ontology Alignment Evaluation Initiative (OAEI) tracks with 49 distinct alignments. We find that Tokenisation and Normalisation (categorised as Phase 1 text preprocessing) are more effective than Stop Words Removal and Stemming/Lemmatisation (categorised as Phase 2 text preprocessing). We propose two novel approaches to repair unwanted false mappings that occur in Phase 2 text preprocessing. One is a pre hoc logic-based repair approach used before text preprocessing, employing an ontology-specific check to find common words that cause false mappings. The other repair approach is the post hoc large language model (LLM)-based approach, used after text preprocessing, which utilises the strong background knowledge provided by LLMs to repair non-existent and counter-intuitive false mappings. The experimental results indicate that these two approaches can significantly improve the matching correctness and the overall matching performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：文本预处理管道如何影响本体匹配？
经典的文本预处理管道（包括分词、规范化、停用词移除和词干提取/词形还原）已在许多系统中用于本体匹配（OM）。然而，文本预处理缺乏标准化导致映射结果多样化。本文研究了文本预处理管道对8个本体对齐评估倡议（OAEI）赛道、49个不同对齐结果的影响。我们发现，分词和规范化（归类为第一阶段文本预处理）比停用词移除和词干提取/词形还原（归类为第二阶段文本预处理）更有效。我们提出两种新颖方法修复第二阶段文本预处理中产生的非预期错误映射：一种是预处理前的逻辑驱动修复方法，通过本体特定检查识别导致错误映射的常见词；另一种是预处理后的基于大语言模型（LLM）的修复方法，利用LLM提供的强大背景知识修复不存在或反直觉的错误映射。实验结果表明，这两种方法可显著提升匹配正确率和整体匹配性能。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how variations in the classical text preprocessing pipeline—comprising tokenisation, normalisation, stop word removal, and stemming/lemmatisation—affect ontology matching performance across eight OAEI tracks and 49 alignments. The study finds that Phase 1 steps (tokenisation and normalisation) have a more substantial impact on matching accuracy than Phase 2 steps (stop word removal and stemming/lemmatisation), which often introduce false mappings. To address this, the authors propose two repair methods: a pre-hoc logic-based approach that identifies ontology-specific false mapping triggers before preprocessing, and a post-hoc LLM-based approach that leverages large language models to correct erroneous mappings after preprocessing. Experimental results show both methods significantly improve matching correctness and overall performance.
该研究探讨了经典文本预处理流程（包括分词、归一化、停用词移除和词干/词形还原）对本体匹配性能的影响，覆盖八个OAEI赛道和49个对齐任务。研究发现，第一阶段步骤（分词与归一化）对匹配准确性影响更大，而第二阶段步骤（停用词移除和词干/词形还原）常引入错误映射。为此，作者提出两种修复方法：一种是预处理前的逻辑规则方法，通过本体特定检查识别并过滤导致错误映射的常见词；另一种是后处理阶段的基于大语言模型的方法，利用LLM的背景知识修正非存在或反直觉的错误映射。实验结果表明，两种方法均显著提升了匹配正确率和整体性能。
Efficient Pre-Training with Token Superposition
Authors: Bowen Peng, Théo Gigant, Jeffrey Quesnelle
First: 2026-05-07T16:41:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:41:37+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 11 figures, 28 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Pre-training of Large Language Models is often prohibitively expensive and inefficient at scale, requiring complex and invasive modifications in order to achieve high data throughput. In this work, we present Token-Superposition Training (TST), a simple drop-in method that significantly improves the data throughput per FLOPs during pre-training without modifying the parallelism, optimizer, tokenizer, data, or model architecture. TST is done in two phases: (i) A highly efficient superposition phase where we combine many contiguous tokens into one bag and train using a multi-hot cross-entropy (MCE) objective, and (ii) a recovery phase where we revert back to standard training. We extensively evaluate TST on the scale of 270M and 600M parameters and validate on 3B and a 10B A1B mixture of experts model, demonstrating that it is highly robust in different settings. Ultimately, TST consistently outperforms baseline loss and downstream evaluations, and under equal-loss settings, TST yields up to a 2.5x reduction in total pre-training time at the 10B A1B scale.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过令牌叠加实现高效的预训练
大语言模型的预训练通常在大规模下成本高昂且效率低下，需要复杂的侵入性修改才能实现高数据吞吐量。本文提出了令牌叠加训练（TST），这是一种无需修改并行性、优化器、分词器、数据或模型架构的简单即插即用方法，显著提升了预训练阶段每FLOPs的数据吞吐量。TST包含两个阶段：(i) 高效的叠加阶段，将多个连续令牌合并为一个集合，使用多热交叉熵（MCE）目标进行训练；(ii) 恢复阶段，恢复到标准训练。我们在270M和600M参数规模上广泛评估TST，并在3B和10B A1B混合专家模型上进行验证，证明其在不同设置下高度稳健。最终，TST在各项基线损失和下游评估中持续表现更优；在等损失条件下，TST在10B A1B规模下可将总预训练时间减少高达2.5倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost and inefficiency of large language model pre-training, this work introduces Token-Superposition Training (TST), a simple drop-in method that boosts data throughput per FLOPs without altering model architecture, optimizer, tokenizer, or data pipeline. TST operates in two phases: first, it superimposes contiguous tokens into multi-hot bags and trains using a multi-hot cross-entropy objective for efficiency; second, it recovers to standard training. Experiments across models ranging from 270M to 10B parameters show TST is robust and consistently outperforms baseline methods, achieving up to a 2.5x reduction in pre-training time at the 10B scale under equal-loss conditions.
为解决大语言模型预训练成本高、效率低的问题，本文提出Token-Superposition Training（TST），一种无需修改模型架构、优化器、分词器或数据流程的简单插件式方法。TST分两阶段：第一阶段将连续token合并为多热向量并用多热交叉熵训练；第二阶段恢复为标准训练。在270M至10B参数规模的模型上实验表明，TST具有强鲁棒性，性能持续优于基线损失，在10B模型下同等损失条件下可将预训练时间缩短2.5倍。
DC-DiT: Adaptive Compute and Elastic Inference for Visual Generation via Dynamic Chunking
Authors: Akash Haridas, Utkarsh Saxena, Parsa Ashrafi Fashi, Mehdi Rezagholizadeh, Vikram Appia, Emad Barsoum
First: 2026-03-06T14:59:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:40:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion Transformers rely on static patchify tokenization, assigning the same token budget to smooth backgrounds, detailed object regions, noisy early timesteps, and late-stage refinements. We introduce the Dynamic Chunking Diffusion Transformer (DC-DiT), which replaces fixed patchification with a learned encoder-router-decoder scaffold that adaptively compresses the 2D input into a shorter token sequence through a chunking mechanism learned end-to-end with diffusion training. DC-DiT allocates fewer tokens to predictable regions and noisy timesteps, and more tokens to detailed regions and later refinement stages, yielding meaningful spatial segmentations and timestep-adaptive compression schedules without supervision. Furthermore, the router provides an importance ordering over retained tokens, enabling elastic inference: a single checkpoint can be evaluated at flexible compute budgets with a smooth quality-compute tradeoff. Additionally, DC-DiT can be upcycled from pretrained DiT checkpoints and is also compatible with orthogonal dynamic computation approaches. On class-conditional ImageNet generation, DC-DiT reduces inference FLOPs by up to 36.8% and improves FID by up to 37.8% over DiT baselines, yielding a stronger quality--compute Pareto frontier across model scales, resolutions, and guidance settings. More broadly, these results suggest that adaptive tokenization is a general mechanism for making visual generation both more efficient and more flexible at inference time.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DC-DiT：通过动态分块实现视觉生成的自适应计算与弹性推理
扩散Transformer依赖静态分块标记化，为平滑背景、细节物体区域、噪声早期时间步和后期精修阶段分配相同标记预算。我们提出动态分块扩散Transformer（DC-DiT），用一个可学习的编码器-路由器-解码器框架替代固定分块，通过与扩散训练联合端到端学习的分块机制，自适应地将2D输入压缩为更短的标记序列。DC-DiT对可预测区域和噪声时间步分配较少标记，对细节区域和后期精修阶段分配更多标记，无需监督即可实现有意义的空间分割和时间步自适应压缩调度。此外，路由器为保留标记提供重要性排序，支持弹性推理：单个检查点可在灵活计算预算下评估，实现平滑的质量-计算权衡。DC-DiT还可从预训练DiT检查点升级，并兼容正交动态计算方法。在类别条件ImageNet生成任务中，DC-DiT相比DiT基线最多减少36.8%推理FLOPs，提升37.8% FID，跨模型规模、分辨率和引导设置实现更强的质量-计算帕累托前沿。更广泛地说，这些结果表明自适应标记化是使视觉生成在推理时更高效、更灵活的通用机制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of static tokenization in diffusion transformers, which uniformly allocates computational resources regardless of content complexity or timestep needs, the authors propose DC-DiT: a dynamic chunking mechanism that adaptively compresses 2D inputs into variable-length token sequences via an end-to-end learned encoder-router-decoder architecture. This approach allocates fewer tokens to predictable or noisy regions and more to detailed or refinement stages, enabling spatially and temporally adaptive compression without supervision. Experiments on class-conditional ImageNet generation show DC-DiT reduces inference FLOPs by up to 36.8% while improving FID by up to 37.8% over DiT baselines, achieving a superior quality-compute tradeoff across model scales and resolutions, and supporting elastic inference through flexible token budgets.
为了解决扩散Transformer中静态分块导致的计算资源分配不均问题，作者提出DC-DiT，通过学习型编码器-路由器-解码器架构替代固定分块，动态压缩2D输入为可变长度的令牌序列。该架构在扩散训练中端到端学习，根据区域细节和时间步噪声动态调整令牌分配，减少冗余区域计算，增加精细区域与后期优化阶段的资源。实验表明，DC-DiT在ImageNet生成任务中推理FLOPs降低最高36.8%，FID提升最高37.8%，在不同模型规模、分辨率和引导设置下实现更优的质量-计算帕累托前沿，并支持弹性推理。
Hedging Memory Horizons for Non-Stationary Prediction via Online Aggregation
Authors: Yutong Wang, Yannig Goude, Qiwei Yao
First: 2026-05-07T16:38:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-07T16:38:21+00:00
Comments: Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study online prediction under distribution shift, where inputs arrive chronologically and outcomes are revealed only after prediction. In this setting, predictors must remain stable in quiet regimes yet adapt when regimes shift, and the right adaptation memory is unknown in advance. We propose MELO (Memory-hedged Exponentially Weighted Least-Squares Online aggregation), a model-agnostic method that hedges across adaptation scales: it wraps any non-anticipating base-predictor pool with exponentially weighted least-squares (EWLS) adaptation experts at multiple forgetting factors, and aggregates raw and EWLS-adapted forecasts with MLpol, a parameter-free online aggregation rule. Under boundedness conditions, we establish deterministic oracle inequalities showing that it competes with both the best raw predictor and the best bounded, time-varying affine combinations of the base predictions, up to a path-length-dependent tracking cost and a sublinear aggregation overhead. We evaluate MELO on French national electricity-load forecasting through the COVID-19 lockdown using no regime indicators, lockdown dates, or policy covariates. MELO reduces overall RMSE by 34.7\% relative to base-only MLpol and achieves lower overall RMSE than a TabICL reference supplied with an external COVID policy-response covariate. Moreover, MELO requires only lightweight per-step recursive updates without model retraining.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过在线聚合在非平稳预测中对冲记忆地平线
我们研究在分布偏移下的在线预测问题，其中输入按时间顺序到达，预测结果仅在预测后才公布。在此设定下，预测器必须在平稳期保持稳定，而在环境变化时能够适应，且合适的适应记忆长度事先未知。我们提出MELO（记忆对冲指数加权最小二乘在线聚合），这是一种模型无关的方法，可在不同适应尺度上进行对冲：它将任意非前瞻性的基础预测器集合包裹在多个遗忘因子的指数加权最小二乘（EWLS）适应专家中，并使用MLpol（一种无参数的在线聚合规则）聚合原始预测与EWLS适应预测。在有界性条件下，我们建立了确定性Oracle不等式，表明其在路径长度依赖的跟踪成本和次线性聚合开销下，能与最佳原始预测器和最佳有界、时变仿射组合的基础预测器竞争。我们在法国全国电力负荷预测中评估MELO，使用COVID-19封锁期间的数据，未使用任何环境指示器、封锁日期或政策协变量。MELO相比仅使用基础预测器的MLpol整体RMSE降低34.7%，且在提供外部COVID政策响应协变量的TabICL参考模型下仍实现更低的整体RMSE。此外，MELO仅需轻量级每步递归更新，无需模型重训练。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of adapting online predictors to non-stationary environments without prior knowledge of regime shifts, the paper introduces MELO, a model-agnostic method that combines exponentially weighted least-squares adaptation experts with multiple forgetting factors and aggregates their outputs using MLpol. MELO dynamically balances stability and adaptation by hedging across memory horizons, and theoretically guarantees performance close to the best raw predictor or time-varying affine combinations, up to a sublinear overhead. Empirical evaluation on French electricity load forecasting during the COVID-19 lockdown—without using regime indicators or external covariates—shows MELO reduces RMSE by 34.7% compared to base-only MLpol and outperforms a TabICL baseline with external policy data, while requiring only lightweight recursive updates without model retraining.
为应对非平稳环境下在线预测器缺乏先验知识的制度切换问题，本文提出MELO方法，该方法通过多遗忘因子的指数加权最小二乘适应专家与MLpol聚合规则相结合，动态平衡稳定性与适应性。在有界性假设下，该方法可保证与原始预测器及时间变化的仿射组合预测器竞争的确定性Oracle不等式。在法国疫情封锁期间的电力负荷预测实验中，MELO在无制度指标或政策协变量的情况下，相较仅用MLpol的基线方法整体RMSE降低34.7%，且优于使用外部政策协变量的TabICL基线，同时仅需轻量级递归更新无需模型重训练。
